Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets, including Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, and QVHighlights, demonstrate consistent gains at stringent IoU thresholds (e.g., IoU=0.7), alongside enhanced ranking performance. The framework's computational efficiency and compatibility with existing models make it a versatile and impactful solution for real-world TSG applications.

</details>


### [34] [Uni-Loc: Weakly Supervised 3D CT Pathology Localization via Spatially Grounded Vision-Language Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13330)
*Hao-Chih Lee,Zahi A. Fayad,Xueyan Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Uni-Loc：一种视觉-语言框架，用扫描级标签统一病理分类与3D CT定位，通过病理条件查询与图像特征的空间相似度同时产出预测与定位图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D CT 中异常的精确定位是把自动预测与临床病理发现对应的关键，但体素级标注昂贵且稀缺，希望仅用扫描级弱监督同时完成分类与3D定位。

Method: 构造病理条件查询（pathology-conditioned queries），用其与图像特征的空间相似度生成分类得分与定位图；引入 token-to-voxel 上采样模块实现高分辨率定位；用梯度引导阈值（无额外可学习参数）从相似度图中提取病理区域。

Result: 在 5 个定位任务、355 例带标注 CT 上取得宏平均体素级 AUROC/AUPR 96.1%/34.5%，优于 relevance propagation（80.7%/13.9%）与 gradient saliency（88.7%/8.1%）；分类 AUROC 在 CT-RATE 为 88.39%、RAD-ChestCT 为 78.03%，均优于匹配基线。

Conclusion: 扫描级弱监督即可实现有效的 3D 病理定位并保持有竞争力的分类性能，无需病理特异的体素级监督；但 AUPR 绝对值偏低，说明体素级精度与判别性仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Accurate localization of abnormalities in three-dimensional (3D) CT scans is essential for linking automated predictions with clinically relevant pathological findings. We propose Uni-Loc, a vision-language framework that unifies pathology classification and 3D localization using scan-level supervision. Uni-Loc derives both predictions and localization maps from spatial similarities between pathology-conditioned queries and image features. A token-to-voxel upsampling module enables high-resolution localization, while gradient-guided thresholding identifies pathology regions without additional learned parameters. Evaluated on five localization tasks comprising 355 annotated CT scans, Uni-Loc achieves macro-averaged voxel-level AUROC/AUPR of 96.1\%/34.5\%, outperforming relevance propagation (80.7%/13.9%) and gradient saliency (88.7%/8.1%). Uni-Loc also achieves classification AUROC of 88.39% on CT-RATE and 78.03% on RAD-ChestCT, improving over a matched baseline. These results demonstrate effective 3D pathology localization without requiring pathology-specific voxel-level supervision.

</details>


### [35] [How Hyperbolic Are Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13384)
*Freek Byrman,Partha Das,Stijn Verdenius,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文实证检验双曲视觉-语言模型（MERU、HyCoCLIP、ARGENT、PHyCLIP）中指数映射的实际作用，发现其近似为恒等映射，且用欧氏VLM配合相同层级损失即可复现相同评估分数与径向分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲VLM被宣称具有更强零样本性能与可解释的半径-泛化性结构，但曲率究竟在何处、多大程度上真正起作用缺乏检验；作者希望从指数映射这一唯一曲率入口入手，判断双曲几何是否是层级视觉-语言学习的必要基底。

Method: 1) 对四个代表性双曲VLM的指数映射进行数值分析：考察映射前后成对距离与角度的变化，并做"推理时移除指数映射"的消融；2) 论证指数映射被恒等函数良好逼近；3) 对照实验：在CC3M上从头训练欧氏VLM，使用完全相同的层级损失，比较训练统计量、评估分数与径向分布。

Result: 指数映射前后成对距离和角度几乎不变，推理阶段去掉该映射评估分数不变；欧氏模型在约最后90%训练步中训练统计量与双曲版本几乎无法区分，评估分数与径向分布一致。

Conclusion: 在当前实践方式下，欧氏空间已是层级视觉-语言学习的充分基底；双曲VLM报告的收益主要来自层级损失而非曲率，这要求未来研究重新设计才能真正发挥双曲几何的理论优势。

Abstract: Hyperbolic vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as a rapidly growing area of research, reporting strong zero-shot performance alongside an interpretable embedding space in which radius orders representations by generality. But how hyperbolic are hyperbolic VLMs in practice? We investigate this at the exponential map, the single operation through which curvature enters an otherwise Euclidean network. We show that across MERU, HyCoCLIP, ARGENT, and PHyCLIP, the exponential map is well approximated by the identity function, i.e., pairwise distances and angles in the pre-map Euclidean space barely change under projection, and removing the map at inference leaves evaluation scores unchanged. Motivated by this observation, we train Euclidean VLMs on CC3M from scratch under the same hierarchy losses. Over roughly the final 90% of the training steps, the training statistics are nearly indistinguishable from those of their hyperbolic counterparts, and the resulting models match the evaluation scores and recover the same radial distributions. Our findings establish Euclidean space as a sufficient substrate for hierarchical vision-language learning as it is currently practiced, and motivate new paradigms in which hyperbolic geometry can deliver on its theoretical promise. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [36] [Prompt2Echo (P2Echo): Text-Prompted Multi-View Echocardiography Segmentation with One Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13425)
*Moein Heidari,Alvin Kimbowa,Mahdi Tabatabaei,Afshin Bozorgpour,Sina Ghorbani Kolahi,River Jiang,Wenjin Chen,David J. Foran,Jasmine Grewal,Dorit Merhof,Ilker Hacihaliloglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2Echo：以结构化视图感知文本提示驱动单一模型完成多标准超声心动图切面分割，核心是DyITA（可微路由+token差分算子）动态调制解码器特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图超声心动图自动分割对完整心功能评估至关重要，但不同声窗间解剖结构与外观差异巨大，导致单一模型难以通用；现有基础模型适配依赖隐式潜在先验，缺乏显式、可控的视图/结构条件。

Method: 统一框架P2Echo：用结构化视图感知文本提示显式条件化分割。DyITA模块将可微路由机制与token差分算子结合，依据所请求视图与目标结构动态调制解码器特征，实现无需改动结构即可切换处理通路；另构建内部大规模数据集与三个外部数据集（含留出评估）。

Result: 在内部大规模数据集上与三个外部数据集留出评估中，P2Echo达到或超过视图专用专家模型与SOTA通用模型，且计算开销显著更低。

Conclusion: 显式视图-结构文本条件化优于隐式先验适配，为多切面心脏分析提供高效、可控、可扩展的统一方案；代码已开源。属医学影像领域工作，方法思路（可微路由式条件调制）可迁移但非本关注核心方向。

Abstract: Automated segmentation in multi-view echocardiography is essential for comprehensive cardiac assessment, yet remains challenging due to substantial anatomical and appearance variability across standard acoustic windows. In this paper, we present P2Echo, a unified framework capable of segmenting diverse cardiac structures across multiple standard echocardiographic views using a single model controlled by structured view-aware text prompts. Unlike recent foundation model adaptations that rely on implicit latent priors, P2Echo explicitly conditions the segmentation process via a novel Dynamic Image-Text Alignment (DyITA) module. DyITA integrates a differentiable routing mechanism and token differential operators to dynamically modulate decoder features according to the requested view and target structure. This allows the network to adaptively switch its processing pathways for different views without architectural changes. Extensive experiments on a large-scale internal dataset (seen during training) and held-out evaluation on three external datasets demonstrate that P2Echo matches or exceeds both view-specific specialists and state-of-the-art universal models at a fraction of their computational cost, offering an efficient, controllable solution for multi-plane cardiac analysis. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [37] [Camera3R: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Heterogeneous Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13453)
*Ruxiao Duan,Yunwen Zhou,Erin Hong,Dongxu Zhao,Eric Turner,Federico Tombari,Alex Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Camera3R：一种低秩适配（LoRA 类）框架，用极少参数与训练时间把透视相机预训练的前馈 3D 重建网络扩展到任意非透视相机模型，同时保持原有透视输入性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3R 基座模型虽在大规模透视数据集上训练，但难以泛化到非透视（鱼眼、全景等）及未知异构相机模型，限制了在真实任意相机配置场景中的部署；从头重训代价过高。

Method: 低秩适配：在冻结的透视预训练 3R 网络权重上插入低秩增量分支，仅训练少量新增参数即可使网络适配任意相机模型；训练成本低，且不破坏原有透视分支能力。

Result: 实验表明仅需约 0.5% 额外参数、不到一天训练，即可将基座 3R 模型适配到非透视相机，并在透视输入上完整保留原始性能；代码开源。

Conclusion: 为前馈 3R 模型提供了低成本、可保留原能力的相机模型泛化路径，利于在异构、未知相机条件下的实际部署。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) from unconstrained multi-view images has recently emerged as a prominent research direction in 3D computer vision. Various foundational reconstruction models have been proposed with different architectures and reconstruction paradigms. Despite being trained on large-scale perspective datasets with substantial computational resources, these models generally struggle to generalize to non-perspective images. This limitation hinders their deployment in applications involving arbitrary, unknown, and potentially heterogeneous camera models. To build on the capabilities of existing reconstruction models with minimal retraining, we propose Camera3R, a low-rank adaptation framework that efficiently extends perspective-pretrained 3R networks to any other camera models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with only 0.5% additional parameters and less than a day of training, Camera3R adapts a foundational 3D reconstruction model to non-perspective cameras while fully preserving its original performance on perspective inputs. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [38] [Hand-Object Contact Transition Detection in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13486)
*Huy Anh Nguyen,Chang Dong,Feras Dayoub,Minh Hoai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架做自我中心视频中的手-物接触转换检测，同时定位"接触(touch)"与"释放(release)"两个时间边界；联合建模手部时空表征与物体感知特征，无需推理时外部检测器；构建ContactTransition数据集(4,021视频,8,498 touch/8,077 release标注)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦于"hand-object interaction"整体检测或手部状态识别，缺少对接触发生/结束这一瞬态转换的显式定位；两阶段方案依赖外部物体检测与HOI检测器，误差级联且推理成本高。

Method: 以手部为中心的时空特征为基础，融合直接从视频学习的物体感知(object-aware)特征实现手-物联合建模；引入面向接触转换的transition-aware event spotting公式，将两个边界建模为事件起止点；端到端训练，推理阶段不依赖外部物体或HOI检测器；数据集附带object-of-interest监督。

Result: 在两个转换类型上一致优于强两阶段基线与端到端event spotting基线；检测到的接触转换可作为下游自我中心手-物推理的有效时间锚点。

Conclusion: 接触转换检测可作为独立且有用的时序任务，统一手-物表征加事件spotting公式是有效方案，并为下游具身/自我中心推理提供时序定位基础。

Abstract: This paper tackles the task of hand-object contact transition detection in egocentric video, which aims to localize the two temporal boundaries of a contact episode: the moment a hand first establishes contact with an object (touch) and the moment contact is released (release). We propose a unified framework that jointly models the interacting hand and object to accurately localize both transition types. The proposed framework augments hand-centric spatiotemporal representations with object-aware features learned directly from the video and introduces a transition-aware event spotting formulation tailored to contact transitions, without relying on external object or hand-object interaction detectors during inference. To support this task, we present ContactTransition, a dataset comprising 4,021 egocentric videos with 8,498 touch and 8,077 release annotations, together with object-of-interest supervision. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong two-stage and end-to-end event-spotting baselines on both transition types. Finally, we show that detected contact transitions provide effective temporal anchors for downstream egocentric hand-object reasoning.

</details>


### [39] [When Reconstruction Helps Event-Camera Person Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13488)
*Yifan Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较事件相机数据到通用视觉模型输入的五种表示方式（灰度累积、两种时间编码、E2VID 与 FireNet+ 强度重建），在 DSEC 真实数据 192 个窗口上评估行人检测触发性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用视觉模型用作事件相机的录制触发器，需要把异步亮度变化转换为模型可解释的表示；本文考察不同表示带来的检测收益是否值得连续触发的代价与误启动。

Method: 在配对的真实事件数据上对比两种强度重建器（E2VID、FireNet+）、两种时间编码与直接灰度累积；用仅基于开发集校准的阈值，在四个留出 DSEC 记录的 192 个窗口上统计检测数与背景误报数，并做统一 10% 背景误报上限的回顾性对比；另用 Qwen3-VL-4B 作为下游检测器验证，并做 96 秒探索性回放考察阈值选择对片段捕获与录制启动的影响。

Result: E2VID 在开发集标定阈值下取得 40/52 行人检测、12/140 背景误报；最强直接时间编码在可行固定阈值下仅 14 检测、7 误报；统一 10% 误报上限下 E2VID 为 40 检测，直接编码最多 19。FireNet+ 准备开销更低，冻结阈值下 21 检测、0 误报。Qwen3-VL-4B 上 E2VID 为 33/52、2/140，最佳直接渲染（三 bin 图像）为 9/52、9/140。

Conclusion: 在受控输入历史下，强度重建相较直接编码带来明确的检测收益，但需与连续触发的计算开销和误启动相权衡；阈值选择对片段捕获与录制启动高度敏感。

Abstract: Event cameras record asynchronous changes in brightness. To use a general visual model as a recording trigger, a system must represent these measurements in a form the model can interpret. We compare direct grayscale accumulation, two temporal encodings, and two intensity reconstructors on paired real event data. On 192 windows from four held-out DSEC recordings, thresholds calibrated only on development data yielded 40 of 52 person detections and 12 of 140 background positives with E2VID. The strongest direct temporal encoding at an admissible fixed cutoff yielded 14 detections and seven background positives. A retrospective comparison at a common 10% background-positive cap yielded 40 detections for E2VID and at most 19 for any direct encoding. FireNet+ had lower preparation cost and produced 21 detections with zero observed background positives at its frozen cutoff. On the same 192 windows, Qwen3-VL-4B yielded 33/52, 2/140 with E2VID and 9/52, 9/140 with the highest-recall direct rendering, the three-bin image (person detections, background positives). A 96-second exploratory replay illustrates the sensitivity of episode capture and recording starts to cutoff selection. The experiments identify a detection benefit from reconstruction under controlled input history and distinguish it from the cost and false starts of continuous triggering.

</details>


### [40] [Reasoning-Aware Relational Representation Learning for Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13496)
*Jiawei Xiao,Boya Wang,Longtian Qiu,Shan Ning,Xuming He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RaRe将MLLM生成的思维链(CoT)理由转化为结构化关系嵌入，通过自监督对比对齐与面向特异性的强化学习优化理由生成，实现开放词表场景图生成(OV-SGG)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OV-SGG方法依赖数据集特定的物体-谓词共现模式，在长尾分布与定位噪声下导致关系表征偏置、纠缠，削弱稀有关系识别与未见谓词泛化。

Method: 构建推理感知的关系表征学习框架：1) 将MLLM生成的CoT rationale转成关系嵌入；2) 用自监督对比学习把rationale嵌入与文本化关系描述对齐；3) 用特异性导向的强化学习目标精炼rationale生成，促使产生针对语义物体对的关系证据；直接优化rationale衍生的表征空间而非仅作为预测中间迹。

Result: 在VG150与PSG上相较已有基线提升类别均衡识别效果；但学习带来的额外增益随数据集与proposal设置而变化，泛化收益并不稳定。

Conclusion: 利用MLLM推理理由直接优化关系表征空间是缓解长尾偏差与提升开放词表关系的可行途径，但增益依赖数据与候选框条件，稳健性有待加强。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Scene Graph Generation (OV-SGG) requires models to recognize visual relationships beyond the training vocabulary, yet existing methods often rely on dataset-specific object--predicate co-occurrence patterns. Under long-tailed distributions and noisy object localization, such reliance leads to biased, entangled relation representations, limiting both rare-relation recognition and generalization to unseen predicates. To address these challenges, we propose RaRe, a reasoning-aware relational representation learning framework that transforms MLLM-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales into structured relation embeddings. Rather than using CoT only as an intermediate reasoning trace for final prediction, RaRe directly optimizes the rationale-derived representation space to improve inter-relation discriminability. Specifically, RaRe first aligns rationale embeddings with textualized relation descriptions through self-supervised contrastive learning, and then refines rationale generation with a specificity-oriented reinforcement learning objective that encourages semantically object-pair-specific relational evidence. Experiments on VG150 and PSG demonstrate improved category-balanced recognition over existing baselines, while the additional gains from learning vary across datasets and proposal settings. Code available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [Dark Matters: VAE-Aware HDR Encoding for SDR-to-HDR Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13504)
*Donghoon Cha,Bomseok Park,Hansl von Kwon,Ku DoHyung,Hanwool Sul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EquivFlow：一种紧凑、可逆的曝光等变编码，将曝光变化映射为加性偏移并保留尺度无关的色度分量，用于解决 SDR-to-HDR 重建中冻结 VAE 潜空间的暗部坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDR-to-HDR 属病态逆问题，需同时恢复高光截断、暗部压死与量化伪影。潜在扩散模型可提供生成先验，但 HDR 信号经过预训练 VAE 的方式受限：常见 log 类编码会在生成前抑制低亮度色度差，导致冻结 VAE 潜空间出现"暗侧坍塌"。

Method: 不改动 VAE，而是设计新的输入编码：EquivFlow 为紧凑可逆的曝光等变编码，把曝光变化转化为可加性平移，同时保留尺度无关的色度分量。在多种预训练图像/视频 VAE 上做编码级测试，并集成到 SDR-to-HDR 视频扩散模型中。

Result: 编码级测试显示曝光结构特性与深暗区色度传输在不同宿主 VAE 上均保持；集成到 SDR-to-HDR 视频扩散后，在测试设置下取得一致的色度保真增益，同时保持有竞争力的重建保真度。

Conclusion: 问题根源在于编码而非生成模型：通过曝光等变、可逆的潜空间编码可直接修复暗部色度坍塌，且具备跨 VAE 的可迁移性，为 HDR 生成提供了轻量、即插即用的方案。

Abstract: SDR-to-HDR reconstruction is an ill-posed problem in which clipped highlights, crushed shadows, and quantization artifacts must be recovered while preserving the original content. Latent diffusion models provide powerful generative priors for restoring missing detail, but their effectiveness is constrained by how HDR signals pass through the pretrained VAE. We find that common log-like encodings suppress low-luminance chromatic differences before generation, producing a dark-side collapse in the frozen-VAE latent. We introduce EquivFlow, a compact, invertible exposure-equivariant encoding that maps exposure changes to additive shifts while retaining a scale-independent chromatic component, without modifying the VAE. Encoding-level tests across pretrained image and video VAEs show that its exposure structure and deep-dark chroma transport persist across the tested hosts. Integrated into SDR-to-HDR video diffusion, EquivFlow delivers consistent chromatic-fidelity gains across the tested settings while retaining competitive reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [42] [Distribution Shift Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery using Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13510)
*Abdullah Almansour,Ozan K. Tonguz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对 Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD 三个开源 OOD/漂移检测工具在遥感跨数据集分布偏移场景下做实证对比，评估检测效果与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV 遥感平台算力受限，且部署数据常因地理、传感器、季节等因素偏离训练分布，需在保证预测精度之外具备分布偏移监测能力；现有工具缺乏在资源受限场景下的效果-时延权衡评估。

Method: 实证案例研究：选取三个开源检测工具（Alibi-Detect、NannyML、PyTorch-OOD），构建跨数据集遥感偏移设置，统一评测检测有效性（检测能力）与计算延迟（处理开销）。

Result: 不同方法与实现之间的检测行为和处理成本存在差异，揭示检测能力与响应时间之间的权衡；未提出新算法。

Conclusion: 结果动机指向轻量化、延迟感知（latency-aware）的监控机制，以适配算力受限的机载系统。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery acquired by Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) supports wide range of applications; however, deploying machine learning (ML) models on resource-constrained aerial platforms introduces challenges beyond predictive accuracy, particularly when deployment data differ from the distributions observed during training. Such shifts are common in remote sensing due to geographic, sensor, seasonal, and other visual variations. A practical solution is to equip deployed models with mechanisms that detect emerging distribution shifts and support subsequent responsive actions. This paper presents an empirical case study of three open-source detection tools: (i) Alibi-Detect; (ii) NannyML; and (iii) PyTorch-OOD. Using cross-dataset remote-sensing shifts, we evaluate both detection effectiveness and computational latency of these tools. Our results reveal differences in detection behavior and processing cost across methods and implementations, highlighting trade-offs between detection capability and response-time. These findings motivate further work toward lightweight, and latency-aware monitoring mechanisms for resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [43] [TMAL: Topology-preserving Multi-offset Affinity Learning for Curvilinear Structure Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13511)
*Suho Han,Soungsill Park,Kanghyun Ryu,Yoseob Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出拓扑保持多偏移亲和力学习(TMAL)，用软亲和力+多偏移方向亲和力+解码器特征加权融合,并加辅助分割分支,提升视网膜血管与裂缝的连通性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级监督不建模像素间连接关系；拓扑损失仍输出普通掩膜；连通性方法使用固定局部范围的二值连接标签，无法区分细段与粗区，也无法描述更大偏移处的连接。

Method: TMAL：(1) 软亲和力标签，强调边界附近细区域；(2) 在多个偏移处预测方向性亲和力以覆盖更远距离；(3) 用解码器特征生成权重融合各偏移亲和力，结合局部连续性与更广上下文；(4) 辅助分割分支提供区域级监督以保持重叠精度。

Result: 在DRIVE视网膜血管数据集与DeepCrack裂缝数据集上取得最高Dice、IoU、clDice，并显著降低连通分量误差Δβ0。

Conclusion: 多偏移软亲和力学习可有效替代固定范围二值连通性标签，兼顾像素精度与结构连续性，适用于细长曲线结构分割。

Abstract: Curvilinear structures such as retinal vessels and surface cracks need both pixel-level accuracy and structural continuity. Pixel-wise supervision does not model the connections between pixels, and topology-based losses still produce ordinary masks. Connectivity-based methods predict these connections directly, but they use binary connectivity targets over a fixed local range. Such labels give the same value to thin segments and to thick regions, and they cannot describe connections at larger offsets. To address these limitations, we propose Topology-preserving Multi-offset Affinity Learning (TMAL). TMAL uses soft affinities that emphasize thin regions near the boundary, and it predicts directional affinities at several offsets to reach further. It then combines these affinities with weights that come from the decoder features, so local continuity and wider context are used together. An auxiliary segmentation branch adds region-level supervision and keeps the overlap accuracy high. On the retinal vessel dataset DRIVE and the crack inspection dataset DeepCrack, TMAL reaches the highest Dice, IoU, and clDice scores, and it greatly reduces the connected-component error $\Delta\beta_0$. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [Evidential Deep Learning with conditional entropy for glaucoma detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13513)
*Hai Siong Tan,Kuancheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新的条件熵正则项用于面向眼底图像青光眼检测的 Evidential Deep Learning（EDL），使不确定度校准误差显著低于 Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准与传统 Dempster-EDL，并结合 Grad-CAM 聚焦指数分析可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像诊断模型需要可靠的不确定度量化；传统 EDL 以 Dempster 不确定性（vacuity/AU）作为正则，存在校准不佳问题，缺乏物理/解剖一致的可解释性验证。

Method: 将条件熵（conditional entropy）作为 EDL 训练的正则化函数以约束证据分布，在 EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2 与 RIM-ONE-DL 数据集上训练青光眼分类模型；与 Deep Ensembles、MC Dropout、事后校准及常规 Dempster-EDL 对比校准误差；引入基于 Grad-CAM 热图空间集中度的 focus index，比较正确/错误分类样本的分布分离度并分析假阴性激活与视盘位置的偏移关系。

Result: 所提条件熵正则 EDL 的校准误差明显低于所有对比方法；EDL 模型在正确与错误分类样本间的 focus index 分布分离度更大；假阴性样本出现偏离视盘的局部 Grad-CAM 激活，与青光眼判读中视盘的关键临床意义相吻合。

Conclusion: 条件熵正则为 EDL 提供了更优的不确定度校准，同时带来更符合生理解剖的可解释性，说明不确定度与解释一致性可协同提升。

Abstract: We propose a novel conditional entropy function as a basis for quantifying predictive uncertainty in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) for glaucoma detection from retinal fundus images. Using the EyePACS-AIROGS-light-V2 and RIM-ONE-DL datasets for validation, we found that our proposed EDL model regularized by conditional entropy function produced uncertainty estimates with substantially lower calibration errors than Deep Ensembles, MC Dropout, post-hoc calibration and the conventional EDL framework based on Dempster uncertainty. To further probe our models' interpretability, we analyzed Grad-CAM heatmaps using a focus index representing spatial concentration. Compared with the other uncertainty quantification methods, EDL models exhibited greater separation between the index distributions of correctly and incorrectly classified fundus images. In particular, false negatives were associated with localized Grad-CAM activations that were displaced from the optic disc, consistent with its established clinical importance in glaucoma assessment. Overall, our findings suggest that our proposed EDL framework for glaucoma detection provides not only enhanced uncertainty calibration but also admits a more physiologically consistent interpretability.

</details>


### [45] [Seeing the Small: Deformable Duality Mamba for Retinal Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13514)
*Chang Yu,Siqing Li,Yuanchen Liu,Zhenyu Bu,Luming Yang,Alper Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D²Mamba，以非因果状态空间对偶（SSD）结合可变形Mamba扫描，建模病灶区域的空间组织与形状自适应，用于糖尿病视网膜病变分割，尤其改善微动脉瘤等极小、不规则病灶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR病灶形状差异大、微动脉瘤仅占数像素，现有分割方法难以捕捉病灶区域的空间组织关系，作者假设引入全局上下文与形状自适应扫描可提升此类小而不规则病灶的分割效果。

Method: D²Mamba：将非因果状态空间对偶（SSD，提供类双向全局上下文）与可变形Mamba扫描（扫描路径随病灶形状自适应形变）耦合；在DDR数据集上五折交叉验证，与代表性分割基线在统一训练协议下对比，并对各组件做消融。

Result: 综合表现最优，mIoU 33.87%，比最强基线高2.99个百分点；在最小、最易漏检的微动脉瘤上提升最大，Dice提高10.06个百分点；消融验证各组件有效。代码与预训练权重已开源。

Conclusion: 结果支持"空间自适应状态空间建模有利于微小病灶分割"的假设，可变形扫描+非因果SSD是处理多尺度、不规则小目标分割的有效组合思路。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) lesions vary widely in shape, and microaneurysms (MAs) span only a few pixels. We hypothesize that modeling the spatial organization of lesion regions, with global context and shape-adaptive scanning, improves the segmentation of such small and irregular lesions. To test this, we propose D$^2$Mamba, which couples non-causal state space duality with deformable Mamba scanning. We evaluate it with five-fold cross-validation on the DDR dataset, comparing against representative segmentation baselines under an identical training protocol, and conduct ablations on each component. D$^2$Mamba achieved the best overall performance, with 33.87% mIoU, 2.99 percentage points above the strongest baseline. The largest gain appeared on MAs, the smallest and most easily missed lesions, where Dice improved by 10.06 percentage points. These results confirm our hypothesis that spatially adaptive state space modeling benefits tiny-lesion segmentation. Code and pretrained weights are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [46] [UniWeaver: Harnessing Unified Multimodal Models for Interleaved Text-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13530)
*Hongxu Chen,Zixuan Zhong,Donghao Zhou,Haodong Li,Xiaomin Yu,Tianhao Li,Shiyao Yu,Zhongwei Wan,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWeaver：模型无关的结构化框架，整合多模态规划、生成与视觉反馈，生成连贯的交错图文内容；并构建UniWeaver-10K训练集与UniWeaver-Bench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型(UMMs)缺乏对文本与图像两模态的原生协调能力，且将粗粒度用户请求转化为连贯的多模态组合需要显式规划。

Method: 提出UniWeaver结构化、模型无关的harness框架，串联多模态规划→生成→视觉反馈闭环；构建覆盖20类任务族、1万条任务的UniWeaver-10K数据集及执行轨迹用于训练UMMs；提出面向任务的UniWeaver-Bench，从文本覆盖度、写作质量、视觉覆盖度、图文对齐四维评测。

Result: 实验显示UniWeaver达到SOTA，并在多种UMM骨干上一致提升交错图文生成质量。

Conclusion: 将规划-生成-反馈的agent式流程外置于模型，可作为通用harness增强现有UMM的交错多模态生成能力，具备跨骨干可迁移性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) have opened up new possibilities for generating rich interleaved text-image content. However, they generally lack native support for coordinating the two modalities, and translating coarse user requests into coherent multimodal compositions also requires explicit planning. We propose UniWeaver, a structured, model-agnostic harness framework that integrates multimodal planning, generation, and visual feedback to produce coherent interleaved outputs. To train UMMs for this task, we construct UniWeaver-10K, a dataset of 10,000 tasks across 20 task families, paired with execution trajectories guided by UniWeaver. We also introduce UniWeaver-Bench, a task-oriented benchmark systematically assessing text coverage, writing quality, visual coverage, and image-text alignment. Experiments show that UniWeaver achieves state-of-the-art performance and improves interleaved text-image generation across multiple UMM backbones.

</details>


### [47] [AgenticConflictLens: From Long Traffic Videos to Verifiable Conflict Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13540)
*Boshra Khalili,Sonia Moshfeghi,Jinwoo Jang,Andrew W Smyth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgenticConflictLens：面向长时固定摄像头交通视频的智能体式、查询条件化冲突抽取框架，粗到细检索+选择性验证，仅检视约9%-12%视频即在Bellevue/NYC DOT上取得0.84/0.80 F1与0.77/0.73 tIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通冲突、近失、违章转弯等安全关键事件在长时监控中稀疏且短暂，现有流程对全量视频均匀处理、不随分析者查询自适应；视觉语言方法缺乏参与者级别的交通冲突核验。

Method: 自然语言安全查询驱动的Traffic-Aware Controller，结合结构化交通记忆与专用冲突分析工具，决定搜索位置、关注对象与证据核实/否决；粗到细搜索先召回候选区间再选择性精细验证；输出含细化时间区间、参与者对、语义区域、轨迹、运动证据、不确定性、拒因与支撑片段的冲突记录。

Result: 在Bellevue与NYC DOT共96小时标注数据上，冲突存在检测F1（0.84/0.80）与时间IoU（0.77/0.73）最优，同时仅检视8.7%与11.9%的视频。

Conclusion: 查询条件化的智能体式、可审计抽取可实现可扩展且证据可溯的城市交通安全分析，显著降低人工审阅成本。

Abstract: Traffic cameras continuously record urban road-user behavior, providing proactive safety evidence beyond crash reports. Yet safety-critical events - conflicts, near misses, unsafe turns, sudden braking, and crosswalk interactions - are rare and brief, buried in hours of routine footage. Existing traffic-video pipelines extract detections, trajectories, and risk scores but process footage uniformly, without adapting to an analyst's query. Vision-language methods improve scene understanding but often lack traffic-specific verification of participant-level conflicts. We introduce AgenticConflictLens, an agentic, query-conditioned framework for efficient, auditable conflict extraction from long fixed-camera traffic videos. Given a natural-language safety query, a Traffic-Aware Controller combines structured traffic memory with specialized conflict-analysis tools to choose where to search, which road users to examine, and which evidence to verify or reject. A coarse-to-fine search retrieves candidate intervals and applies detailed verification selectively. The framework produces conflict records with refined time intervals, participant pairs, semantic regions, trajectories, motion evidence, uncertainty, rejection reasons, and supporting clips. On 96 annotated hours from Bellevue and NYC DOT, AgenticConflictLens achieves the highest conflict-existence F1 and temporal IoU on both datasets: 0.84 F1/0.77 tIoU on Bellevue and 0.80 F1/0.73 tIoU on NYC DOT, while inspecting only 8.7% and 11.9% of video, respectively. These results support scalable, evidence-grounded urban traffic-safety analysis.

</details>


### [48] [PhyRubric: Rubric-Grounded Diagnostic Evaluation of Physical Commonsense in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13576)
*Huiyi Chen,Weitai Kang,Shicheng Fan,Xinshuo Wang,Zhixiang Shen,Yan Yan,Philip S. Yu,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyRubric：基于评分细则（rubric）的T2V物理常识诊断评测框架，先按视频字幕生成专属细则（语义项+物理项），再依据带时间戳的证据给出逐项判定与分数；构建PhyRubric-Bench（5大物理域、34类）与PhyRubric-Train（3,788视频），微调开源评审模型PhyRubric-Judge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V物理常识评测依赖标注者凭直觉给单一总分，主观性强，且无法指出具体违反哪条物理原理；而T2V被定位为"世界模拟器"时，物理一致性评测尤为关键。

Method: 提出PhyRubric：在观看视频前先由字幕拆解出字幕专属细则（语义项与物理项），再基于时间戳证据做逐项verdict并聚合为分数；实例化为PhyRubric-Bench（5个物理域、34个类别），扩展到3,788条视频的PhyRubric-Train，并微调开源judge（PhyRubric-Judge）。

Result: 在10个T2V模型上评测显示物理常识仍远未解决；PhyRubric-Judge与人类判断的相关性优于现有开源judge，与Gemini 4 Argon相当；进一步分析给出对T2V模型的可操作改进启示。

Conclusion: 细粒度、证据驱动的细则化评测可提供比单一总分更可解释、更可靠的物理常识诊断信号，且可通过微调开源模型实现接近前沿闭源模型的评审能力。

Abstract: As text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly positioned as world simulators, it is essential to evaluate whether their videos conform to physical commonsense. However, existing benchmarks rely on annotators who rate each video with a single score based on intuition, yielding ratings that are subjective and do not tell which physical principle is violated. We introduce PhyRubric, a rubric-grounded diagnostic framework for evaluating physical commonsense in T2V generation. For each caption, PhyRubric constructs a caption-specific rubric before any video is seen, decomposing the caption into semantic and physical items, and derives scores from item-level verdicts grounded in timestamped evidence. We instantiate this framework as PhyRubric-Bench, which spans 34 categories across five physical domains, and scale it up to PhyRubric-Train with 3,788 videos, on which we fine-tune the open-source PhyRubric-Judge. Empirical results across ten T2V models show that physical commonsense remains far from solved, and that PhyRubric-Judge correlates more closely with human judgments than existing open-source judges while matching the frontier proprietary Gemini 4 Argon. Further analysis provides actionable insights for T2V models.

</details>


### [49] [Lightweight Deep Learning Framework for Astrocyte-Centric 3D Reconstruction across Normal and Stress-associated Ultrastructural States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13581)
*Bowen Deng,Gwang-Jin Jeong,Seulgi Noh,Ji Young Mun,Na Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AstroSeg-vEM：轻量级、以星形胶质细胞为中心的框架，结合 Transformer 2D 分割与专门后处理，直接在原始高分辨率 EM 切片上完成 vEM 中星形胶质细胞的形态保持分割、3D 重建与定量形态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星形胶质细胞形态与其支持、调控神经环路功能的能力密切相关，但从体电子显微镜（vEM）数据重建其形态高度依赖人工、计算资源消耗大，成为规模化应用的瓶颈。

Method: Transformer 基 2D 分割 + 专用后处理流程，保留原始面内分辨率，适配各向异性 vEM 数据与人工校对流程；在四个小鼠海马数据集（含正常与应激相关星形胶质细胞超微结构状态）上评估。

Result: 留出测试集平均 Dice 0.92、加权 Dice 0.95；轻量架构可在消费级硬件上高效完成高保真重建，跨形态状态表现出良好鲁棒性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 为大规模星形胶质细胞超微结构分析及未来星形胶质细胞—神经元相互作用研究提供了可扩展、易获取的平台；后续可探索各向异性数据中的拓扑一致性（如过度分割/欠分割的合并与分裂校正）及与功能数据的关联。

Abstract: The morphology of astrocytes is closely associated with their ability to support and modulate neural circuit functions; however, its reconstruction from volume electron microscopy (vEM) data remains labor-intensive and computationally resource-intensive, placing substantial barriers on its broader adoption and scalability despite the high spatial resolution of vEM. We present AstroSeg-vEM, a lightweight, astrocyte-centric framework for morphology-preserving astrocyte segmentation, 3D reconstruction, and quantitative morphological analysis in vEM images. AstroSeg-vEM combines transformer-based 2D segmentation with dedicated post-processing methods and operates directly on high-resolution EM sections, making it well suited to anisotropic vEM data and downstream proofreading workflows while preserving the full in-plane resolution of the original EM data. Across four mouse hippocampal datasets representing normal and morphologically defined stress-associated astrocyte ultrastructural states, AstroSeg-vEM demonstrates robust performance and generalization, achieving average Dice and weighted Dice values of 0.92 and 0.95, respectively, on a held-out test dataset. Its lightweight architecture enables efficient, high-fidelity reconstruction on consumer-grade hardware, providing a scalable and accessible platform for large-scale astrocyte ultrastructure analysis and future astrocyte-neuron interaction studies.

</details>


### [50] [When to Start Denoising? Adaptive Denoising Entry for Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13620)
*Zifu Zhang,Yuqiao Li,Ding Ding,Shengxi Li,Mai Xu,Daowen Li,Kai Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ADE-GVC：首个时间步感知的生成式视频压缩框架，用轻量预测器从压缩潜变量自适应选择扩散去噪起始时间步，配合时序适配器注入压缩感知条件，在极低码率下同时提升保真度与真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式生成视频压缩性能最优，但其效果严重依赖压缩潜变量在扩散过程中的注入时机（去噪起始时间步），该因素直接决定重建保真度与感知真实感的权衡，此前缺乏显式建模与自适应选择机制。

Method: 将去噪起始时间步选择建模为几何优化问题；设计轻量预测器从压缩潜变量估计自适应起始时间步；用时序适配器提取压缩感知条件并注入预训练视频扩散模型，实现条件化重建。

Result: 在感知指标上达 SOTA，重建真实感显著优于现有生成式视频压缩方法；相比最新开源基线 GLC-Video 平均节省 40.99%（DISTS）和 95.24%（PSNR）码率；并观察到预测时间步与原始/压缩潜变量失真之间存在强定量关系，为优化建模提供实证支持。

Conclusion: 去噪起始时间步是保真度—真实感权衡的关键控制量，可通过预测器自适应学习并在几何优化框架下得到实证验证；该时间步感知范式可作为生成式视频压缩的通用设计原则。

Abstract: Generative video compression offers a promising solution for compressing and reconstructing videos under extremely low bitrate constraints, among which the diffusion-based solutions still achieve the best performance by providing the powerful generative latent prior. However, their effectiveness critically depends on when the compressed latent is introduced into the diffusion process, which directly governs the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism. To address this limitation, we identify denoising start-timestep selection as a key factor governing this trade-off and formulate it as a geometric optimization problem. Based on this formulation, we propose a novel adaptive diffusion entry method for generative video compression (ADE-GVC), the first timestep-aware generative video compression framework, featuring a lightweight predictor that estimates an adaptive denoising start point from the compressed latent. A temporal adapter further extracts compression-aware conditions and injects them into a pretrained video diffusion model, enabling the reconstruction of videos with both high fidelity and realism. More importantly, we observe a strong quantitative relationship between the predicted timesteps and the distortion between the original and compressed latents, providing empirical support for our optimization formulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADE-GVC achieves the state-of-the-art performance on perceptual metrics and substantially improves reconstruction realism over existing generative video compression methods. Notably, our ADE-GVC achieves an average bitrate savings of 40.99\% (on DISTS) and 95.24\% (on PSNR) compared to the latest open-source baseline GLC-Video.

</details>


### [51] [PUMA: Probability-Constrained Sequential Interaction for Forensic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13621)
*Zhouli Yu,Mengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PUMA，用空间约束的非平衡最优传输实现语义-取证特征匹配，并在共享位置接口上做位置级凸选择以自适应决定跨分支交互顺序，用于图像篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义-取证融合方法虽提供互补表征，但在空间对应过程中未显式保留质量失配信息，也无法自适应选择跨分支信息传播顺序，导致空间对应、匹配质量与交互次序的效应相互纠缠、难以区分。

Method: 构造行随机的跨分支与噪声分支内算子，采用空间约束的非平衡最优传输在归一化前保留有符号传输质量残差；通过共享位置接口在两个顺序算子间做逐位置凸选择以决定交互顺序；噪声特征经门控残差路径更新。

Result: 在四个测试集上取得较强的定位性能，在COVERAGE与NIST16上的分数超过所对比的代表性方法的已发表结果；实验验证了局部匹配状态与自适应顺序交互对跨源定位及多种后处理条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 保留传输质量失配的局部匹配状态并结合自适应顺序交互，可有效改善语义-取证跨源融合的篡改定位效果，但缺少效率与跨数据集泛化的定量分析。

Abstract: Image manipulation localization must jointly exploit semantic structure and noise-sensitive forensic evidence. However, the effects of spatial correspondence, matching quality, and interaction order are often conflated. Existing semantic-forensic fusion methods provide complementary representations, but do not explicitly preserve quality-mismatch information during spatial correspondence or adaptively select the order in which information is propagated across branches. We propose PUMA (Probability-constrained Unbalanced Matching and Aggregation), which combines spatially constrained unbalanced optimal transport with position-wise convex selection between two sequential operators through a shared positional interface. Before normalization, PUMA retains signed transport-mass residuals, constructs row-stochastic cross-branch and within-noise-branch operators, and updates noise features through a gated residual path. PUMA achieves strong localization results on four test sets, with scores on COVERAGE and NIST16 exceeding the published results of the representative methods listed in our comparison. The experiments support the utility of local matching states and adaptive sequential interaction for cross-source localization and several post-processing conditions.

</details>


### [52] [FlashMotion: Ultra-Fast Long-Term 3D Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13626)
*Kaixing Yang,Xulong Tang,Ronghui Li,Xiangyue Zhang,Ziqiao Peng,Mingshuang Luo,Chenxi Zhao,Haoyu Wu,Ju Fan,Hongyan Liu,Puwei Wang,Zijiao Zeng,Jun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashMotion：面向严格推理预算的高质量长时程3D人体动作生成框架，联合设计MeanFlow少步生成与轻量BiMamba架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时交互应用（游戏、XR、数字人、具身仿真）需要超高速且高质量的长时程3D动作生成，但现有多步采样的生成策略与长序列下高算力/高显存的模型架构构成两个耦合瓶颈。

Method: 轨迹层面：提出3D动作空间MeanFlow公式化建模，结合运动学与动力学一致性约束实现少步生成，并引入Adaptive Soft masking实现时序连贯的动作编辑；单步层面：设计轻量非自回归BiMamba架构，配合条件引导的状态选择与AdaIN条件融合，提升跨模态对齐与长序列泛化。

Result: 在文本到动作、语音到手势、音乐到舞蹈三个任务上，质量与效率均达到整体SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹级少步生成与单步级轻量架构的协同设计，可在严格推理预算下实现高质量长时程3D人体动作生成，为实时交互式管线提供可行方案。

Abstract: 3D human motion generation plays a crucial role in game development, extended reality (XR), digital humans, and embodied simulation. Beyond motion generation, practical pipelines include rigging, retargeting, and rendering, making ultra-fast long-term 3D motion generation without compromising quality valuable for real-time interactive applications. Despite promising progress, high-quality long-horizon motion generation under tight inference budgets remains challenging, owing to 2 coupled bottlenecks: (1) generative strategies that require many sampling steps for high-quality 3D motion generation and (2) model architectures with substantial per-step computational and memory costs for long sequences. We present FlashMotion, a co-designed framework for high-quality long-horizon 3D motion generation under a strict inference budget, covering text-to-motion, music-to-dance, and speech-to-gesture tasks. To enhance trajectory-level efficiency, we introduce a 3D motion-space MeanFlow formulation with kinematic and dynamic consistency constraints for few-step generation, together with an Adaptive Soft masking mechanism for temporally coherent motion editing. To improve per-step efficiency, we design a lightweight non-autoregressive BiMamba architecture with condition-guided state selection and AdaIN-based condition fusion mechanism, enhancing cross-modal alignment and generalization to longer motion sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlashMotion achieves overall state-of-the-art performance in both quality and efficiency across text-to-motion, speech-to-gesture, and music-to-dance generation. Code is available at FlashMotion ( this https URL ).

</details>


### [53] [DepthGS: Depth-Guided Gaussian Splatting with Adaptive Regularization for High-Quality Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13628)
*Xiao Ren,Yu Liu,Ning An,Jian Cheng,Xin Qiao,You-Fu Li,He Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthGS 从理论上推导了单视图与多视图设定下深度偏差与重建误差的关系并给出误差上界，据此设计了两项自适应正则策略：单视图的深度差引导法向正则（用局部深度差计算几何可靠性权重调制法向约束强度）与多视图的跨视图深度一致性正则，在 DTU、Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples 上取得有竞争力的表面重建与视角合成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 已扩展到表面重建，但深度信息与重建误差之间的关系缺乏系统分析，导致几何优化策略多凭经验设计、缺少理论依据；论文希望填补这一空白，用理论指导正则化设计。

Method: 先推导单视图与多视图条件下深度差异与重建误差的理论关系并建立重建误差上界；再据此设计两类自适应正则：①单视图优化中以局部深度差计算几何可靠性权重，自适应调制法向约束强度（深度差引导自适法向正则）；②多视图优化中约束相邻视图深度图与"当前视图深度预测经变换投影到相邻视图"所得深度预测之间的一致性（跨视图深度一致性正则）。

Result: 在 DTU、Mip-NeRF360、Tanks and Temples 三个数据集上的实验表明，DepthGS 在表面重建与新颖视角合成两项任务上均达到有竞争力的性能，验证了理论分析所导出的正则策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文将深度-重建误差关系的理论刻画转化为可落地的自适应正则项，为前馈式/逐场景 3DGS 表面重建提供了原则化的几何优化思路；属增量式 splatting 变体，创新集中在正则化设计与理论分析层面。

Abstract: Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient representation for novel view synthesis, offering fast differentiable rendering and high visual fidelity, and has increasingly been extended to surface reconstruction. However, the relationship between depth information and reconstruction error remains insufficiently explored, limiting the principled design of geometric optimization strategies. To fill the above gap, we propose DepthGS, a depth-guided Gaussian splatting framework. We derive theoretical relations between depth discrepancies and reconstruction error under single-view and multi-view settings, and rigorously establish corresponding upper bounds on reconstruction error. The above analysis informs the design of adaptive regularization strategies in both single-view and multi-view optimization. More specifically, for single-view optimization, we propose a depth-difference-guided adaptive normal regularization strategy that modulates the normal constraint strength using a geometric reliability weight computed from local depth differences. For multi-view optimization, we introduce a cross-view depth consistency regularization strategy that promotes consistency between neighboring-view depth maps and the corresponding depth predictions obtained by transforming the current view's depth predictions into those neighboring views. Extensive experiments on DTU, Mip-NeRF360, and Tanks and Temples demonstrate that DepthGS achieves competitive performance in both surface reconstruction and novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [54] [picoEmergencyNet : Attention-Enhanced Ultra-Small Deep Learning Model for Aerial Image Classification in Emergency Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13638)
*Christos Kyrkou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: picoEmergencyNet：面向航空影像灾害分类的超轻量 CNN，仅 23k 参数、7M FLOPs、0.1 MB 存储，在 AIDER/AIDERv2 上达 96.1% 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机（UAV）端实时灾害识别受限于内存、算力与延迟预算，现有高精度模型计算代价过大难以部署于资源受限平台。

Method: 架构上结合深度可分离空洞卷积（depthwise dilated convolution）实现多尺度特征提取，配合高效通道注意力（ECA 类）与通道混洗（channel shuffling），在极低算力下保持表达力；无硬件专用优化。

Result: AIDER/AIDERv2 上最高 96.1% 准确率，可匹配或超越参数量高数个量级的轻量 SOTA；Jetson AGX Orin 上 GPU 258 FPS、CPU 46 FPS，激活内存比对比模型低约 7 倍。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的架构特化而非模型缩放，即可实现高精度、TinyML 就绪的实时 UAV 灾害监测；对资源受限边缘部署具有工程参考价值。

Abstract: Real-time disaster recognition from aerial imagery requires deep learning models that operate under strict memory, compute, and latency constraints. While recent approaches achieve high accuracy, their computational cost limits deployment on resource-constrained UAV platforms. This work introduces picoEmergencyNet, an ultra-lightweight convolutional neural network designed for aerial image disaster classification. The proposed architecture combines depthwise dilated convolutions with efficient channel attention and channel shuffling to enable multi-scale feature extraction at minimal computational cost. picoEmergencyNet has 23k parameters and 7M FLOPs, requiring only 0.1 MB of model storage. Evaluated on the AIDER and AIDERv2 disaster datasets, it achieves up to 96.1% accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art lightweight models that are orders of magnitude larger. Deployment experiments on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin show that, without hardware-specific optimizations, picoEmergencyNet achieves 258 FPS on GPU and 46 FPS on CPU with a 7x lower activation memory footprint compared to competing models. These results demonstrate that careful architectural specialization, rather than model scaling, enables highly efficient and accurate TinyML-ready disaster recognition for real-time UAV-based emergency monitoring.

</details>


### [55] [Predicting Single-Cell Spatial Gene Expression from H&E using Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13649)
*Ashley P. Tsang,Evan Liu,Conrad Foo,Bob Chen,Hassaan Maan,Donny Chan,Elisa Penna,Veronica Ibarra Lopez,Hector Corrada Bravo,Aicha BenTaieb,Lisa M. McGinnis,Runmin Wei,Kenneth T. Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作系统评估并改进了病理学基础模型在单细胞空间基因表达预测任务上的适配策略，较零样本基线提升平均基因级Pearson相关2.67倍，全基因面板AUROC达0.800。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从常规H&E染色图像直接预测基因表达，可获得低成本、可扩展的空间分子信息；但病理基础模型多采用图块级预训练目标，难以捕捉单细胞级分子预测所需的细粒度细胞特征，因此需要领域知识与任务专属的适配方法。

Method: 使用配对的H&E与Xenium 5K数据，提出并系统评估领域知识引导（domain-informed）、任务特定的微调/适配策略，将病理基础模型改造用于单细胞空间基因表达预测。

Result: 平均基因级Pearson相关较标准零样本基线提升2.67倍，达到该任务的最优性能；全基因面板基因检测AUROC = 0.800；预测性能随生物学背景显著变化，空间可变基因上Pearson r = 0.643。

Conclusion: 尽管单细胞测量稀疏，H&E衍生的表示仍能恢复具有生物学意义的空间表达模式；同时揭示了基于形态学在细胞级进行分子推断的潜力与局限。

Abstract: Predicting gene expression directly from hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) images offers a scalable and cost-effective approach to deriving spatial molecular insights from routine histology. Pathology foundation models, typically pretrained using tile-level objectives, may not adequately capture the fine-grained cellular features required for single-cell molecular prediction. In this work, we systematically evaluate and establish domain-informed and task-specific strategies for adapting pathology foundation models to single-cell spatial gene expression prediction. Using paired H&E and Xenium 5K data, our approach improves mean gene-wise Pearson correlation by 2.67x over the standard zero-shot baseline and achieves state-of-the-art performance on this task. We demonstrate strong gene-detection performance across the full gene panel (AUROC = 0.800) and importantly identify that predictive performance varies substantially across biological contexts, with our model achieving its highest Pearson correlation for spatially variable genes (r = 0.643). Our findings demonstrate that, despite the challenges of sparse single-cell measurements, H&E-derived representations can recover biologically meaningful spatial expression patterns, highlighting both the opportunities and limitations of morphology-based molecular inference at cellular resolution.

</details>


### [56] [EgoHMP: Achieving Precise Human Motion Prediction in 3D Scenes via Egocentric Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13654)
*Xin Zhao,Pengzhan Zhou,Ziyi Li,Riheng Jia,Zhida Qin,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoHMP uses egocentric views and head motion as intent carriers for 3D human motion prediction, with vision-motion alignment, anomaly-suppressing intent prediction, and a contact-aware diffusion decoder.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Human gaze is used to infer intent for HMP but suffers from ambiguity, drift, and eye-tracking hardware dependence; cognitive science suggests egocentric views encode interaction affordances and head motion precedes motion planning.

Method: EgoHMP: (1) vision-motion alignment encoder aligning visual and motion feature spaces; (2) egocentric intent predictor with modality-aware modulation to suppress invalid cues and output interaction probability map; (3) contact-aware motion decoder that predicts a global trajectory as self-prompt (mitigating diffusion optimization conflict), plus multi-stage denoiser incorporating human contact modeling, and a GCN-based motion decoder.

Result: Extensive experiments report state-of-the-art performance on HMP benchmarks (no concrete numbers or real-robot evidence given in the abstract).

Conclusion: Egocentric vision plus head motion is a robust, hardware-light substitute for gaze-based intent signals, and trajectory self-prompting plus contact-aware denoising improves stability and physical plausibility of predicted motion.

Abstract: The complex interactive dependencies between human behavior and 3D scenes make accurately predicting human intent a major challenge for human motion prediction (HMP). While recent studies use human gaze to infer intent, gaze signals suffer from ambiguity, drift, and a reliance on eye-tracking hardware. Research in cognitive science indicates that egocentric views inherently encode potential interactions, while head motion serves as a crucial precursor in motion planning. Motivated by this, we propose EgoHMP, a novel framework that uses egocentric views and head motions as robust carriers of interaction intent. By translating this intent into an envisioning of future interactions, EgoHMP achieves precise HMP in 3D scenes. EgoHMP first employs a vision-motion alignment encoder to align the visual and motion feature spaces. Based on these aligned features, an egocentric intent predictor with a modality-aware modulation mechanism adaptively suppresses invalid interaction cues to predict an accurate interaction probability map. Furthermore, to enhance the stability and realism of the generated motions, we propose a contact-aware motion decoder. Specifically, inspired by the human cognitive process of planning trajectories prior to action execution, we first predict a global trajectory to serve as a self-prompt, effectively mitigating the optimization conflict in diffusion models. Subsequently, we introduce a multi-stage denoiser, which relieves motion artifacts by incorporating human contact modeling through a multi-stage optimization strategy. Finally, a GCN-based motion decoder is employed to synthesize physically plausible and semantically consistent motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EgoHMP achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [57] [CJRR-Net: A Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking for Future-View Selection in Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13700)
*Huiting Xi,Marsha Mariya Kappan,Eduardo Benitez Sandoval,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CJRR-Net，在MPI-INF-3DHP的8相机标定系统中，基于当前视图姿态观测、相机配置以及将估计3D姿态及其不确定性重投影到各候选视角所得的候选专属几何特征，对未来最优视角进行排序选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机人体姿态采集与运动分析中，选一个有信息量的视角对可靠的人体姿态估计至关重要；已有视角选择方法（如共享输入MLP、PoseErrNet）未充分利用候选视角专属的几何信息。

Method: 构建Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking Network：融合当前视角的2D/3D姿态观测、相机标定配置，以及每个候选相机专属的重投影几何特征（将估计3D姿态与不确定性投影到候选视图中），以排序方式挑选下一时刻的观察视角；在8台已标定相机的固定设置下评测，并与共享输入MLP、改造版PoseErrNet对比，另用Blender rollout演示连续选择过程。

Result: 所选视角的2D MPJPE为105.18像素，优于共享输入MLP的114.64像素和改造PoseErrNet的117.56像素；在各遮挡分组中均取得所列策略中的最低平均误差。

Conclusion: 候选视角专属几何特征对单视角选择的收益在固定8相机场景中得到验证；但结论限于该受控设置，泛化性（不同相机数量/布局、真实部署）尚未验证。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems support performance recording, sports and human-movement analysis, where selecting an informative viewpoint is important for reliable human pose estimation. We propose the Candidate-Joint Reprojection Ranking Network (CJRR-Net) for future-view selection among eight calibrated cameras in MPI-INF-3DHP. The model combines current-view pose observations, camera configuration, and candidate-specific geometric features obtained by reprojecting the estimated 3D pose and its uncertainty into each candidate view. CJRR-Net achieves a selected-view 2D mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) of 105.18 pixels, compared with 114.64 pixels for the shared-input MLP and 117.56 pixels for adapted PoseErrNet. It also achieves the lowest mean error among the listed policies in each evaluated occlusion group. A blender rollout illustrates repeated camera selection. These findings support candidate-specific geometry for single-view selection in the evaluated fixed eight-camera setting.

</details>


### [58] [Hint, Not Hole: Localization-Robust Video Overlay Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13711)
*Dongyang Zhang,Wei Zhang,Renjie Zou,Zhiwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HintErase 将视频叠加物（字幕/水印/logo）定位当作"提示"而非"掩码空洞"，用受损提示训练、真值监督，实现漏检可补、误检可保的单模型擦除，并配流式长视频管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频修复在给定区域内效果好，但实际掩码来自检测器/分割器，存在漏检（笔画、光晕、整帧、整物体）与误检（字符间背景、框周、真实场景文字）双向错误；把掩码当洞的擦除器会原样继承这些错误。

Method: 输入未掩码的完整视频加一路提示通道；训练时按真实定位器的失效方式损坏提示，监督始终跟随真实叠加物标注，迫使模型学习"超越提示"的擦除与保留；全片交付用流式管线处理隔行源、镜头切换、漏检/抖动提示、回贴与音频，显存与视频时长无关。

Result: 单一模型可在提示漏检处完成擦除、在提示误覆盖处保留背景、并在提示落在真实场景文字上时保留该文字；可产出整片交付，项目页提供输入/提示/输出并排的定性对比。

Conclusion: 把定位退化为提示、用鲁棒训练吸收定位误差，是比"掩码即空洞"更实用的范式；工程侧以流式管线解决长视频与源素材问题。摘要仅给出定性结果，缺少定量指标与基线对比。

Abstract: Video overlay removal recovers the scene beneath burned-in subtitles, karaoke lyrics, titles, credits, logos and watermarks. Generative video models now fill a known region convincingly, but in practice the region comes from a detector or a segmenter, and it is wrong in both directions: it misses strokes, halos, whole frames and whole objects, and it covers background between characters, around boxes and on genuine scene text. Erasers that treat the mask as a hole, regenerating inside and copying outside, inherit every one of these errors. We present HintErase, which treats localization as a hint, not a hole. The model sees the full, unmasked video together with a hint channel; during training the hint is corrupted the way real localizers fail, while supervision follows the true overlay. The result is a single model that removes overlay the hint missed, keeps background the hint wrongly covered, and is trained to keep genuine scene text even when the hint lands on it. For full-length releases, a streaming pipeline handles interlaced sources, shot cuts, missed and jittering hints, paste-back and audio, with memory independent of the length of the video. This report describes the approach, the pipeline that turns it into full-length deliveries, and qualitative results; video results, with input, hint and output side by side, are on the project page.

</details>


### [59] [Effects of Laser Beam Width and Measurement Noise on Tree Diameter Estimation in Laser Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13739)
*Jesse Muhojoki,Nuutti Ervasti,Eric Hyyppä,Aapo Erkkilä,Eppu Honkanen,Tuomas Yrttimaa,Mikko Vastaranta,Juha Hyyppä*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析移动激光扫描（MLS）中激光束宽与测距/扫描角噪声异方差性对树干曲线（直径）估计的系统性偏差影响，提出无需标定的偏差解析预测与补偿方法，在北方森林试验中将直径估计RMSE降至亚厘米级，但胸径小于10 cm的树木受树皮、非圆度等因素限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光扫描虽已长期用于树木结构与几何测量，但传感器特性（尤其是激光束直径）及测量条件带来的误差仍是主要误差源，会产生系统性与随机误差，而现有方法缺乏对这类误差来源的量化分析与可迁移的校正手段。

Method: 通过仿真与实测实验相结合，识别测量噪声中的异方差性（测距与扫描角方差差异）与lidar束宽为主要误差源；推导直径偏差随距离和树径变化的模型，并仅利用传感器技术规格进行偏差解析预测与补偿，无需额外标定。

Result: 偏差可被可靠预测并抵消（对DBH>10 cm树木）；实验中补偿方法使北方森林测试场地的树干直径估计RMSE降至亚厘米级；DBH<10 cm的小树因直径本身未知、树皮差异与非圆度导致偏差敏感，方法适用性受限。

Conclusion: 束宽与噪声异方差性是MLS树木几何估计的关键系统偏差来源，可通过基于传感器规格的解析建模实现免标定补偿，为森林资源调查中的高精度树干测量提供实用方案，但小径木测量精度仍受几何与表面特性制约。

Abstract: Laser scanning has been utilized for decades to measure tree structure and geometry. Despite continuous advances in sensor technology, uncertainties arising from scanner characteristics and measurement conditions has remained relevant sources of error. In particular, factors such as laser beam diameter can introduce both systematic and random errors in the reconstructed tree geometry. This study investigates the main sources of these errors in mobile laser scanning (MLS) and quantifies their effects on stem curve (stem diameter) estimation. We discovered through simulations and experiments that the heteroscedasticity (difference in variance in ranging and scan angle) in the measurement noise is a key factor along with the beam width of the lidar, which cause the bias of the diameter estimates to depend not only on distance, but also on the tree diameter. We demonstrated that this bias can be reliably predicted and negated for trees with a diameter at the breast height greater than 10 cm using technical specifications of the sensor without any additional calibration. In experiments, the proposed bias compensation method reduced the root-mean-square error of stem diameter estimates to sub-centimeter level on boreal forest test sites. However, for small trees with diameter below 10 cm, the diameter bias becomes sensitive to the diameter of the tree$\unicode{x2013}$an unknown factor$\unicode{x2013}$and other factors, such as different bark and out-of-roundness of the stem, limiting the precision of lidar for measuring geometry of trees and applicability of the presented method.

</details>


### [60] [Exploring Synchrony for Communication in Modular Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13759)
*Nikolas Willems,Qiyang Sun,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SyncNet模块化网络，将模块内容与相位状态分离，用学习到的Kuramoto动力学演化相位并以相位对齐决定共享通道通信，类比知觉绑定问题；在SQOOP系统泛化任务上取得最佳held-out准确率，但消融显示增益主要来自模块化分解而非同步机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化网络希望各模块学习可复用、可重组的子功能以支持组合泛化，但模块结构本身不能保证角色分化，且无约束的模块间通信会破坏已产生的专门化，因此需要研究如何组织模块间通信。作者将其类比为知觉绑定问题，借鉴同步（synchrony）作为协调分布式表征的机制。

Method: 提出SyncNet：模块内容与动态相位状态解耦，由可学习的Kuramoto动力学演化相位，相位对齐程度决定通过共享信道的通信（相位对齐的模块间才交互，形成内容可寻址的"广播"）。在Sort-of-CLEVR（关系推理）与SQOOP（系统泛化，低pair variety需更多测试时重组）上评测；并做测试时相位覆盖与模块-区域置换消融，另训练一个从零开始的内容寻址变体作对照。

Result: 在SQOOP三种最低pair variety设定下held-out准确率最优，最难设定上领先5.1个百分点；在Sort-of-CLEVR上有竞争力但低于最强注意力基线。消融表明性能依赖动态演化的相位：使用固定相位等同于无寻址，置换模块-区域分配几乎同样损伤性能；然而内容寻址变体从零训练可与SyncNet持平。

Conclusion: 同步式通信能带来收益，但内容寻址变体等同表现说明增益主要源于模块化分解而非同步机制本身；Kuramoto相位动态并非必要，其对通信组织的具体贡献仍有待进一步分离验证。

Abstract: One motivation for modular neural networks is to divide computation across components that learn distinct, reusable functions, which could then be recombined to support compositional generalisation. In practice, modular structure alone does not guarantee that such roles develop, and unrestricted communication between modules can undermine any specialisation that does emerge. This raises the question of how modular communication should be organised. The problem can be viewed as an analogue of the perceptual binding problem, where synchrony has been proposed as a mechanism for coordinating distributed representations. We introduce SyncNet, a modular architecture that separates module content from a dynamical phase state: learnt Kuramoto dynamics evolve the phases, while phase alignment determines communication through a shared channel. We evaluate SyncNet on Sort-of-CLEVR for relational reasoning and on SQOOP for systematic generalisation, where lower pair variety demands more recombination at test time. SyncNet gives the best held-out accuracy of the models compared at the three lowest pair varieties, by 5.1 percentage points at the hardest, and is competitive on Sort-of-CLEVR though below the strongest attention baselines. Test-time overrides show that performance depends on phases that evolve: fixed phases cost as much as no addressing at all, and permuting which module holds which region is nearly as damaging. A content-addressed variant trained from scratch nonetheless matches it, so the benefit appears to come from the modular decomposition rather than synchrony itself.

</details>


### [61] [RF3DGS: Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting with Self-Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13772)
*Qiang Wen,Guotao Meng,Junhong Ruan,Hongyu Liu,Chenyang Lei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RF3DGS 利用多视角光度先验与3D warping 构造无反射透射伪标签，自适应孪生渲染损失在保留高频细节的同时自监督优化3DGS，实现无需反射GT的3D反射去除，并发布首个基于Unity的3D反射去除基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆展示等高保真可视化场景需要去除玻璃/镜面反射，而现有2D反射去除方法难以在3D表示中一致地消除反射；本文希望将"同一3D点跨视角最小像素强度近似无反射信息"这一广泛采用的光度先验推广到3D高斯泼溅框架中。

Method: 先用几何先验优化原始3DGS得到粗粒度无反射深度图；结合深度图与光度先验，通过3D warping从辅助视角聚合无反射信息，生成参考视角的干净透射伪标签；再提出自适应孪生渲染（ATR）损失，在利用伪标签监督的同时保留原始退化图像的高频细节，形成自监督优化流程；并基于Unity渲染引擎构建首个3D反射去除定量评测基准。

Result: 在合成与真实场景上均取得state-of-the-art的反射去除效果；新基准支持严格的定量评估（摘要未给出具体数值指标）。

Conclusion: 将光度先验、几何深度与3D warping 融合进3DGS自监督训练，可在无反射真值条件下有效去除3D反射并保留细节，为该任务提供了方法框架与首个评测基准。

Abstract: 3D reflection removal is crucial in applications demanding high-fidelity visualization, such as museum exhibitions. In this work, we propose Reflection-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting (RF3DGS), a novel method for superior reflection removal. RF3DGS exclusively leverages a widely-adopted photometric prior for reflection removal: that the minimum pixel intensity across multi-view images corresponding to the same 3D point strongly correlates with reflection-free information. However, generalizing this principle to a 3D Gaussian Splatting within a multi-view context is non-trivial. To address this, RF3DGS first optimizes a vanilla 3DGS model using a geometric prior to estimate a coarse, reflection-free depth map for each view. Leveraging these depth maps and photometric prior, a 3D warping process aggregates reflection-free information from auxiliary views to construct a clean transmission pseudo-label for a given reference view. This pseudo-label provides a robust self-supervised signal for optimization. We further introduce an Adaptive Twin Rendering (ATR) loss to synergistically utilize this pseudo-label while preserving high-frequency details from the original, reflection-degraded images. By adeptly fusing these internal 3D self-priors with the ATR loss, RF3DGS demonstrates state-of-the-art reflection removal performance across both synthetic and real-world scenarios. Furthermore, we establish the first 3D reflection removal benchmark, based on the Unity rendering engine, for rigorous quantitative evaluation.

</details>


### [62] [DisasterAgent: An Evidence-Driven Agent for Verifiable Disaster Report Generation From Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13776)
*Zhijian Li,Jiawei Song,Chao Ren,Yidan Zhang,Peijin Wang,Xian Sun,Kun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisasterAgent：证据驱动的遥感灾害报告生成智能体，通过“感知-增强-生成”流程从双时相光学影像构建时空感知图（STPG）作为结构化记忆，并在报告中附加行内引用来形成闭环证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感灾害评估正从简单损伤分类转向报告级综合分析，救援方不仅需要损伤描述，还需要可核验的证据。现有 VLM 与工具增强智能体虽具备多模态推理与感知能力，但难以提供高风险场景验证所需的证据支撑。

Method: 三阶段工作流：(1) 感知阶段调用专用视觉专家工具箱构建 Spatiotemporal Perception Graph（STPG），以结构化记忆组织灾害引起的时空变化；(2) 证据增强（EA）从 STPG 抽取证据，为每条陈述附加行内引用，形成从语句到视觉证据的闭环；(3) 生成可核验的灾害报告。

Result: 在 DisasterM3 与 RSCC 基准上一致优于通用 VLM、遥感专用模型及现有遥感智能体；DisasterM3 灾害报告生成平均分提升 13.5%，RSCC 上 ST5-SCS 提升 15.34%。

Conclusion: 以结构化感知图作为记忆、以引用闭环保证可核验性，是提升高风险场景下智能体报告可信度的有效范式；但验证仅限遥感基准，未涉及机器人或具身场景，代码尚未发布。

Abstract: Remote sensing disaster assessment is moving from simple damage classification toward comprehensive report-level analysis. In this setting, responders need not only damage descriptions but also verifiable evidence to guide response and recovery. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) and tool-augmented agents offer strong multimodal reasoning and perception, but they struggle to provide the evidence required for high-stakes verification. To address these limitations, we propose DisasterAgent, an evidence-driven agent that uses a Perception-Augmentation-Generation workflow to generate verifiable disaster reports from bitemporal optical imagery. DisasterAgent first invokes a specialized vision expert toolkit in the perception phase to build a Spatiotemporal Perception Graph (STPG), serving as a structured memory to store and organize disaster-induced changes across space and time. Evidence-based Augmentation (EA) then extracts evidence from the STPG, attaching inline citations that form a closed evidence loop from each statement to the supporting visual evidence. Extensive experiments on the DisasterM3 and RSCC benchmarks demonstrate that DisasterAgent consistently outperforms general-domain VLMs, remote sensing-specialized models, and existing remote sensing agents. Notably, compared with the original model, it improves the average score for disaster report generation on DisasterM3 by 13.5\% and ST5-SCS on RSCC by 15.34\%, providing a more accurate and verifiable solution for disaster emergency response. The code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Marrying Capacity and Precision for Efficient 2D Gaussian Image Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13784)
*Lin Dong,Qi Ming,Liuqian Wang,Wentao Chao,Yufei Gao,Chengming Liu,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CP-GS 在统一码率约束下联合优化2D高斯数量分配与属性量化精度，提升紧凑图像表示的率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D高斯图像表示将"高斯容量分配"与"参数量化精度"分开优化，忽略了内容相关的容量需求与属性间异质的量化敏感性，导致有限预算下率失真性能次优。

Method: 1) 内容自适应高斯表示：将固定高斯预算从冗余区域动态重分配到高表示需求区域；2) 分层失真感知量化：基于高效渲染标定，在属性间分配精度并在异质属性组内进一步细化；3) 联合优化：构建不同高斯数量与精度配置的候选表示，依据解码失真与实际序列化大小选择工作点。

Result: 在 DIV2K 与 Kodak 上，于多种预算条件下优于代表性2D高斯与隐式神经表示方法，同时保持固定总表示预算与较低优化开销。

Conclusion: 空间容量与参数精度的联合优化对紧凑高斯图像表示有实质收益，二者耦合优于分离优化范式。

Abstract: 2D Gaussian representations provide an explicit and efficient way to represent images, but their rate-distortion performance under limited budgets depends critically on how Gaussian capacity and parameter precision are allocated. Existing methods typically optimize these two resources separately, overlooking content-dependent capacity demands and heterogeneous quantization sensitivity. We present CP-GS, a capacity-precision coupled Gaussian splatting framework for budget-aware 2D Gaussian image representation that jointly optimizes capacity allocation and attribute precision under a shared rate constraint. CP-GS first constructs a content-adaptive Gaussian representation by dynamically reallocating a fixed Gaussian budget from redundant regions to areas with higher representation demand. It then introduces hierarchical distortion-aware representation quantization, using efficient rendering-based calibration to allocate precision across Gaussian attributes and further refine it within heterogeneous attribute groups. Finally, it jointly optimizes Gaussian capacity and attribute precision by constructing candidate representations with different Gaussian counts and precision configurations and selecting operating points according to decoded distortion and actual serialized size. Experiments on DIV2K and Kodak show that CP-GS achieves improved rate-distortion performance over representative 2D Gaussian and implicit neural representations across a range of budgets, while maintaining a fixed overall representation budget and low optimization cost. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly optimizing spatial capacity and parameter precision for compact Gaussian image representation.

</details>


### [64] [The Target Is a Predicate: Predicate-State Grounding for Semantic-Level Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13785)
*Yuhuan Hong,Yiming Ren,Zhiqiang Lin,Feize Wu,Xingyun Liu,Yitong Xie,Lei Zhou,Lisheng Wu,Junjie Wang,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Predicate-State Video Reasoning Segmentation (PS-VRS)：查询定义的是随对象与时间变化的动态谓词 P(o,t)，需显式建模目标"激活/缺席"状态；构建 PS-Bench（2,077 视频、16,250 查询，含状态与区间标注），并提出 PSG 的 State-Bind-Emit 流水线，仅对激活态用 SAM2 出掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理分割把目标视为"一旦识别即永久激活"，导致目标缺席区间过度输出掩码（false positive），且无法处理目标切换。需要显式的谓词状态建模来区分"目标存在但谓词不成立"与"目标出现"。

Method: PSG 三阶段：State——预测 [ACTIVE]/[VACANT] 状态 token；Bind——用稠密跨模态相似度将状态对齐到视频帧；Emit——仅在 ACTIVE 帧调用 SAM2 生成像素掩码。配套构建带状态标签、时间区间与像素掩码的 PS-Bench。

Result: 在 PS-Bench 上较现有基线提升 +9.32 J&F，并显著降低误报掩码发射；实验验证显式谓词状态建模对具有动态视觉见证的视频推理分割有效。

Conclusion: 将"谓词是否成立"作为可预测的一等状态量并与分割解耦，是提升视频推理分割时序精度的有效范式；主要贡献偏视觉理解侧，未涉及机器人闭环验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Predicate-State Video Reasoning Segmentation (PS-VRS), where a query defines a dynamic predicate $P(o,t)$ over objects and time rather than a fixed object identity. Existing methods typically treat the target as permanently active once identified, leading to over-emission of masks during vacant intervals and failure to handle target switching. To address this, we construct PS-Bench, a large-scale benchmark with 2,077 videos and 16,250 queries annotated with explicit active/vacant predicate states, temporal intervals, and pixel masks. We further propose Predicate-State Grounding (PSG), a State-Bind-Emit pipeline that: (1) predicts [ACTIVE]/[VACANT] state tokens; (2) aligns them to video frames via dense cross-modal similarity; and (3) emits masks via SAM2 only for active states. Experiments show that PSG outperforms existing baselines by +9.32 J&F on PS-Bench and significantly reduces false-positive mask emissions. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of explicit predicate-state modeling for video reasoning segmentation with dynamic visual witnesses.

</details>


### [65] [Relaxing Cross-Modal Correspondence for Multimodal Remote Sensing Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13807)
*Xianping Ma,Yu Zhang,Mingzhe Jiang,Wanxin Xie,Qing Zhu,Nan Xu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemPair配对松弛预训练策略，将跨模态对应从实例级放松到语义级（类一致跨实例配对），并用潜因子模型与MixPair混合配对实验证明精确实例对应并非多模态遥感预训练的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态遥感预训练默认依赖严格的实例级跨模态对应，将其视为获取可迁移表征的前提，导致对昂贵实例对齐数据的强依赖、难以规模化。

Method: 1) SemPair：用类一致的跨实例配对替代精确实例配对，实现配对松弛的预训练；2) 潜因子模型：将跨模态依赖分解为共享语义结构与实例特有关联，从理论上说明SemPair在期望意义下保留共享语义、边缘化实例特有关联；3) MixPair：在语义配对与精确配对之间做连续插值，沿连续谱研究对应粒度的影响。

Result: 大量实验表明SemPair取得与精确配对(ExaPair)相当的迁移性能；MixPair揭示下游迁移性能与实例配对比例之间呈非单调关系。

Conclusion: 精确实例对应不是有效多模态遥感预训练的必要前提；跨模态对应粒度应作为可迁移多模态表征学习的重要设计维度；该结论为降低对昂贵实例对齐数据的依赖、扩展多模态预训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal remote sensing pretraining commonly relies on exact instance-level correspondence across modalities, implicitly treating strictly paired observations as a prerequisite for learning transferable representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption by relaxing cross-modal correspondence from the instance level to the semantic level. We introduce Semantic Pairing (SemPair), a pairing-relaxed pretraining strategy that replaces exact instance pairs with class-consistent cross-instance pairs. To characterize the effect of correspondence relaxation, we formulate a latent-factor model that separates cross-modal dependence into shared semantic structure and instance-specific correlation, showing that SemPair preserves the former while marginalizing instance-specific cross-modal correlations in expectation. We further introduce Mixed Pairing (MixPair) as a controlled interpolation between semantic and exact pairing to study correspondence granularity along a continuous spectrum. Extensive experiments consistently show that SemPair achieves transfer performance comparable to exact pairing (ExaPair), while MixPair reveals a non-monotonic relationship between downstream transfer and the instance-pairing ratio. These results demonstrate that exact instance correspondence is not a prerequisite for effective multimodal remote sensing pretraining and establish cross-modal correspondence granularity as an important design dimension for transferable multimodal representation learning. More broadly, this finding suggests a practical route toward scaling multimodal pretraining with reduced dependence on costly instance-aligned data.

</details>


### [66] [FastWild-GS: Minutes-Scale In-the-Wild Gaussian Splatting via Compact Per-Image Appearance Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13808)
*Donghun Ryu,Seokhyun Kim,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastWild-GS 用128维 Photo Appearance Token 加逐通道仿射变换（6参数）建模每张野外照片的外观，小MLP从token与逐高斯特征预测颜色替代球谐，并在致密化/剪枝的误差评估中同样应用该变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外互联网照片在曝光、光照、瞬态物体上差异大，单一高斯颜色集无法解释全部照片；现有 in-the-wild 高斯泼溅方法每场景优化需数小时。

Method: 每张照片用128维 Photo Appearance Token 与逐通道 affine 变换（scale、offset，共6参数）表示外观；小型MLP结合该 token 与逐高斯特征回归高斯颜色，取代球谐系数；渲染后再施加同一变换，且用同一变换后的渲染计算致密化与剪枝所依据的误差，避免把外观差异误判为重建误差。

Result: PhotoTourism 上每场景训练 8.8 分钟，LPIPS 为对比方法中最低，比 LPIPS 次优的 AsymGS 快 23 倍；NeRF-OSR 上每场景 9.7 分钟取得对比方法中最佳平均 PSNR、SSIM 与 LPIPS。

Conclusion: 将外观建模解耦为低维 token 与逐通道仿射后处理，可显著加速野外高斯泼溅优化并提升新视角合成质量；但工作属逐场景优化，未涉及泛化或前馈推理。

Abstract: Internet photos of a scene differ in exposure, illumination and transient objects such as passers-by, so one set of Gaussian colours cannot explain all photos (the in-the-wild setting). Recent in-the-wild Gaussian Splatting methods mostly report hours of per-scene optimization. We present FastWild-GS, which describes each photo by a 128-dimensional Photo Appearance Token and a per-channel transform, a scale and an offset applied to the rendered image (six parameters); a small MLP predicts the colour of each Gaussian from this token and a per-Gaussian feature, replacing the spherical-harmonics colour. The same transform is applied to the renders that score Gaussians for densification and pruning, so appearance differences between photos are not counted as reconstruction error. On PhotoTourism, FastWild-GS trains in 8.8 minutes per scene with the lowest LPIPS among the compared methods, 23x faster than AsymGS, the next best in LPIPS. On NeRF-OSR, it has the best average PSNR, SSIM and LPIPS among the compared methods in 9.7 minutes per scene.

</details>


### [67] [CASCADE: Constraint-Aware SCAffolded DEcomposition for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13820)
*Jinchan Kim,Jinhee Im,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出基于LVLM的逻辑异常检测（LAD）采用"清单式"文本分解时存在文本-图像错配，导致实例混淆与尺度漂移两类失败，提出免训练的CASCADE/CASCADE++框架，为每条约束生成约束特定的视觉支架（scaffold），显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LAD方法用LVLM做checklist式独立文本校验，但所有检查项共享同一张未修改的原始图像，要求模型从单一视图中抽取多种形式的视觉证据，形成"约束特定感知鸿沟"。作者希望判定这些失败究竟源于推理不足还是感知不足。

Method: 在免训练设置下对比"文本推理引导"与"视觉感知引导"进行诊断实验；据此提出CASCADE：在清单分解框架上为每条约束构造约束特定的视觉支架（裁剪、缩放等视觉变换），再送入LVLM校验；CASCADE++进一步从约束结构与参考样本分布推导配置，实现对象级自适应支架。

Result: 诊断实验表明对同一图像做额外文本推理几乎无收益，而按约束调整视觉输入显著提升校验准确率。在MVTec-LOCO与VID-AD上，仅用3个参考样本的CASCADE++比全样本SOTA高3.9 AUROC，在VID-AD上较最强基线提升11.4，并可跨多种LVLM骨干零训练迁移。

Conclusion: 逻辑异常检测的瓶颈在于感知而非推理，约束特定的视觉证据构造（视觉支架）是关键；免训练、可跨骨干迁移的方案在少样本设定下即超越全样本SOTA。

Abstract: Logical Anomaly Detection (LAD) requires verifying whether all category-specific constraints, such as object counts, pairing relations, and measurement ranges, are satisfied by a query image given only a few normal references. Recent LAD methods based on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) formulate this as checklist verification, decomposing the decision into independent textual checks. This decomposition, however, is text-only. Every item still reads the same unmodified query image, so the LVLM must extract different forms of visual evidence from one raw image. We identify this text-image mismatch as a constraint-specific perception gap, the source of two failure modes: instance conflation for binding constraints and scale drift for calibration constraints. To diagnose whether these failures stem from insufficient reasoning or insufficient perception, we compare textual reasoning guides and visual perception guides in a training-free setting. Our analysis shows that reasoning over the same image provides little benefit, whereas adapting the visual input to each constraint substantially improves verification accuracy. Based on this finding, we propose CASCADE, a training-free framework that extends checklist decomposition using constraint-specific visual scaffolds. We further introduce CASCADE++, which adapts these scaffolds at the object level by deriving their configurations from the constraint structure and reference distribution. Across MVTec-LOCO and VID-AD, CASCADE++ surpasses the full-shot state of the art by 3.9 AUROC points with only three references, and improves over the strongest VID-AD baseline by 11.4, transferring across multiple LVLM backbones without any training.

</details>


### [68] [Satellite Pretraining Is Not One Thing: A Matched-Pair Study of Generic and Earth-Observation Foundation Models for Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13849)
*Sandarbh Agrawal,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在14个冻结编码器、5个RGB场景分类数据集上，用配对显著性检验比较5组"目标函数与架构相同、仅预训练数据不同"的EO/通用编码器，发现EO预训练并非普遍有益，且嵌入的silhouette分数几乎完美预测k-NN与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型常默认"卫星影像预训练"优于通用编码器，但以往对比未隔离预训练数据这一变量，作者希望严谨回答：在少标签土地利用/土地覆盖制图中，EO编码器是否真的优于强通用编码器。

Method: 构建5组匹配对（DINO/SatDINO、DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT、MAE/SatMAE、MAE/Scale-MAE、CLIP/RemoteCLIP），共14个冻结编码器、5个RGB场景分类数据集（0.06–30 m GSD），使用原生预处理，评估k-NN、线性与attentive probe及图像检索，覆盖6种低标签划分，并做配对逐图像显著性检验；另分析patch-token池化、attentive probing、silhouette分数与性能的关系。

Result: EO预训练效果不一致：三组显著提升（SatDINO、Scale-MAE、RemoteCLIP，后者仅在未见数据集上评估），两组显著下降（线性探针下DINOv3-SAT最多降22.5点、SatMAE最多降8.8点）；即便两个MAE均在fMoW-RGB上预训练，结果也相反。效应在patch-token池化与attentive probe下稳健，集中在小尺寸人造目标，且在1–5%标签时最大。冻结嵌入的silhouette分数对k-NN准确率和检索mAP预测极准（5个数据集中4个Spearman ρ≥0.96）。

Conclusion: "用卫星影像预训练"不能作为可靠的选型标准，候选checkpoint必须在目标数据上验证；冻结表征的可分性（silhouette）可作为低成本代理指标。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are pretrained on satellite and aerial imagery, but whether such an encoder beats a strong generic one for few-label land-use/land-cover mapping remains unclear, as prior comparisons rarely isolate the pretraining data. We study five matched pairs of Earth-observation (EO) and generic encoders sharing objective and architecture (DINO/SatDINO, DINOv3/DINOv3-SAT, MAE/SatMAE, MAE/Scale-MAE, CLIP/RemoteCLIP) within a benchmark of 14 frozen encoders on five RGB scene-classification datasets (0.06-30 m GSD). With native preprocessing, we evaluate k-NN, linear and attentive probes and image retrieval over six low-label splits, using paired per-image significance tests. EO pretraining is not uniformly helpful: on most datasets and metrics, it significantly improves three pairs (SatDINO, Scale-MAE, RemoteCLIP, the latter evaluated only on unseen datasets) and significantly hurts two (linear probe: DINOv3-SAT by up to 22.5 points, SatMAE by up to 8.8), with opposite outcomes even for two MAEs pretrained on the same fMoW-RGB imagery. These effects are robust to patch-token pooling and attentive probing, concentrate on small man-made objects, and are largest with 1-5% labels. Across encoders, the silhouette score of frozen embeddings predicts k-NN accuracy and retrieval mAP almost perfectly (Spearman $\rho \geq 0.96$ on four of five datasets). Pretraining on satellite imagery is thus not a reliable selection criterion; candidate checkpoints must be validated on target data.

</details>


### [69] [Towards Text-Only Remote Sensing Scene Classification via Semantic Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13851)
*Xianping Ma,Kunlin Wu,Xiaofeng Liu,Xiaokang Zhang,Yuanxin Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“纯文本遥感场景分类”（text-only RSSC）新设定与 SeProRS 基准：先用 VLM 把遥感图像转成文本“语义代理”，再用文本嵌入模型分类，并系统分析提示策略、代理生成器、跨生成器迁移与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“RSSC 必须是视觉中心任务”的默认假设，探索仅依赖文本语义表示时遥感场景能被识别到何种程度，为模态缩减、高效遥感理解建立可复现的研究方向与基准。

Method: 解耦式流水线：①VLM 图像→文本描述（semantic proxy）；②文本嵌入模型编码代理文本；③分类。对比多种提示策略与代理生成器，分析嵌入行为、跨生成器可迁移性及计算效率，在三个公开 RSSC 数据集上评测。

Result: 实验表明语义代理可保留相当程度的判别信息，但纯文本场景理解存在固有局限；同时给出提示策略/生成器选择对性能与效率的具体影响规律。

Conclusion: 不以替代图像 RSSC 为目标，而是定量界定语义代理式场景识别的可行性与边界，提供基准与分析框架，为未来高效、低模态遥感理解研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene classification (RSSC) is traditionally formulated as a vision-centric problem, where inference relies on rich visual representations extracted from images. In this work, we revisit this assumption by introducing text-only RSSC, a new problem setting that investigates whether remote sensing scenes can be recognized using only textual semantic representations. To enable this study, we propose SeProRS, which reformulates RSSC as a text-only classification task through semantic proxy generation. Specifically, a vision-language model (VLM) is first employed to convert remote sensing images into textual descriptions, termed semantic proxies, which are then encoded by a text embedding model for scene classification.
