Softmax normalization and cumulative decoding yield coherent, monotone confusion profiles; a plug-in decoder then selects a threshold for any $F_\beta$ objective without retraining the head. Across three independently trained Kvasir pipelines transferred to four polyp datasets, Joint exceeds continuous Otsu by $0.0080 \pm 0.0046$ external macro Dice, with positive gains in 3/3 runs. On the reference pipeline, three head seeds achieve $0.6324 \pm 0.0027$, 0.0085 above Otsu; direct-utility Ridge achieves 0.6342, with the paired difference interval spanning zero. The same Joint checkpoints outperform Otsu for $F_{0.5}$, Dice, and $F_2$. On developmental KITTI Road leave-one-corruption-family-out evaluation, Joint improves over scalar regression by 0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101], while direct utility is 0.0022 higher. These results establish joint confusion prediction as a useful, metric-retargetable inductive bias, without claiming Bayes-optimal $F_\beta$ decisions.

</details>


### [104] [UniFlow-RS: Unified Generative Prediction for Heterogeneous Remote-Sensing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14281)
*Jianlong He,Liang Liao,Jing Xiao,Chaofeng Chen,Gui-Song Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniFlow-RS：用单一潜在空间 Flow Matching 预测器统一多模态遥感异构任务，并以任务-模态自适应时间步采样（TMAS）协调联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感基础模型虽已在表征层统一多模态与多任务，但预测机制仍按任务分割（独立解码器、预测头、损失），导致预测过程碎片化，无法共享预测过程。

Method: 两大设计：1) 将异构任务输出统一映射为公共潜空间中的结构化视觉目标，复用同一预训练 flow predictor 建模，并用任务相关条件注入处理输入配置差异；2) 所有任务共用一套 Flow Matching 目标，去除任务专属预测损失；引入 TMAS，根据各任务-模态组的在线学习状态动态分配训练时间步，缓解优化不匹配。

Result: 单共享模型覆盖 6 个任务、13 个数据集，在若干基准上超过近期任务专用 SOTA，其余保持竞争力；同一权重在未见数据集、未见任务设置、遮挡条件与近红外假彩色影像上表现出强零样本迁移，无需目标域适配。

Conclusion: 预测过程的跨任务统一是可行且有效的，潜空间 flow matching + 自适应时间步采样可作为遥感通用预测范式。属遥感领域通用方法创新，与具身智能/3D重建核心方向关联较弱。

Abstract: Remote-sensing foundation models increasingly unify representations across sensing modalities and downstream tasks, yet their prediction mechanisms remain largely task-specific. Separate decoders, prediction heads, and objectives leave prediction fragmented across tasks. This naturally raises a question: can heterogeneous remote-sensing tasks share the prediction process? UniFlow-RS answers this question with a single latent flow-matching predictor shared across tasks. This requires addressing two key challenges: unifying heterogeneous prediction spaces and harmonizing joint optimization across tasks. First, we cast heterogeneous task outputs into structured visual targets in a common latent space, allowing them to be modeled by the same pretrained flow predictor. Task-dependent conditioning further accommodates differences in input configurations. Second, we optimize all tasks with a common Flow Matching objective, eliminating task-specific prediction losses. To mitigate optimization mismatch across tasks and modalities, we introduce Task-Modality Adaptive Timestep Sampling (TMAS), which dynamically allocates training timesteps according to the online learning state of each task-modality group. Using a single shared model, UniFlow-RS handles six tasks and thirteen datasets, surpassing recent task-specific state-of-the-art methods on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on the others. Beyond these benchmarks, the same checkpoint demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer across unseen datasets, task settings, occlusion conditions, and near-infrared false-color imagery, without target-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [105] [TaoMate-R1: Temporal Role Specialization for Streaming Multi-Reference Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14307)
*Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Xinyang Li,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaoMate-R1：按"场景建立 vs. 续写"时间角色划分LoRA的流式多参考视频生成器，配合Self Gradient Forcing联合训练，显著降低指令切换处的边界跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多参考视频生成需先用指定人物/物体建立场景，再在多变的后续指令下延续，但单一适配参数难以兼顾"建立"与"续写"两类异质任务；现有按操作（去噪 vs. 上下文写入）划分参数的方案未必是合理共享粒度。

Method: 冻结骨干网络，为场景建立与续写各分配一个独立低秩适配器，每个适配器同时承担本角色的去噪与上下文写入；提出Self Gradient Forcing，借助各角色自身构造的历史联合训练两个角色；并用固定回放(fixed-replay)梯度分析刻画两种参数共享方案所允许的更新方向，在同等双适配器预算下对照实验。

Result: 32个多参考案例对比下，10段约两分钟、含指令切换且共享教师音频条件的流中，平均边界亮度跳变由1.96降至0.613，边界特征相似度更高；在短交互、长流与公开参考组合上平均CLIP与DINO时序相似度均更优。

Conclusion: 将适配参数按"场景建立/续写"的时间角色分组，优于按操作分组，适合流式多参考视频生成；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Streaming multi-reference video generation establishes a scene from specified people and objects, then continues it under changing instructions. We introduce TaoMate-R1, a streaming multi-reference generator that organizes adaptation parameters through temporal role specialization. It assigns separate low-rank adapters to scene establishment and continuation on a frozen backbone. Each adapter performs both denoising and context writing for its role; Self Gradient Forcing jointly trains the roles through the history they construct. This allocation contrasts with operation-based specialization, which separates denoising from context writing across all chunks. A fixed-replay gradient analysis characterizes the update directions admitted by the two sharing schemes. On 32 multi-reference cases, we compare the schemes at the same two-adapter budget. Across ten approximately two-minute streams with changing instructions and shared teacher-audio conditioning, TaoMate-R1 reduces the mean boundary luminance jump from 1.96 to 0.613, with higher boundary feature similarity. It also achieves higher mean CLIP and DINO temporal similarity across short interactions, long streams, and public reference compositions. These results support grouping adaptation parameters by scene establishment and continuation for streaming multi-reference generation. Our code and model are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [From Video Frames to Robot Meta-Frames: A Unified Latent Interface for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14365)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetaWAM将机器人各类低维信号(本体感知、动作、价值)通过频域分带+DCT逆变换折叠进单一"meta-frame"潜帧,使注入表示与预训练视频扩散模型先验对齐,无需额外编解码器即可统一支持策略/世界模型/价值预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM把机器人信号注入视为格式细节:每个信号占一帧或用专用编解码器,导致潜表示偏离预训练VDM的先验分布,重构误差大。作者主张信号注入本质是分布对齐问题。

Method: 以冻结VDM去噪器的相对重构误差作为诊断探针,发现空间编码远离先验、频域打包更贴合。据此提出MetaWAM:每条信号分配互不重叠的频域信道带,经逆DCT提升至空间潜空间,形成可解码且与先验对齐的接口;固定频带布局+slot-aware路由使同一meta-frame支持策略、世界模型与价值预测。

Result: RoboCasa上成功率68.9%,将逐信号帧压缩为单帧,推理延迟降低39%;三个真实Franka任务上达85.0%成功率。

Conclusion: 将机器人信号注入重构为分布对齐问题并以频域打包实现简洁统一的meta-frame接口,在仿真与真机上均取得强表现且降低延迟,为WAM的可扩展多任务融合提供了新设计范式。

Abstract: Pretrained video diffusion models (VDMs) offer a powerful foundation for World Action Models (WAMs), which jointly predict robot actions and future observations. Building such models requires injecting low-dimensional robot signals (e.g. proprioception, actions, and values) into a latent space shaped by natural videos, and a key design choice is the interface through which these signals enter: existing schemes treat it as a formatting detail, spending a separate latent frame on each signal or bolting on dedicated encoders and decoders. We argue that signal injection is instead a distribution-alignment problem: how well a representation matches the prior of the pretrained VDMs during denoising. Using the relative reconstruction error of a frozen VDM denoiser as a probe, we find that conventional spatial encodings sit far off this prior, whereas frequency-domain packing yields latents that the denoiser corrects far less. Motivated by this diagnostic result, we propose MetaWAM, which folds all robot signals into a single unified meta-frame: each signal is assigned to a disjoint frequency-domain channel band and lifted to the spatial latent by an inverse DCT, yielding a decodable interface aligned with the pretrained VDM prior without any added module or architectural change. A fixed band layout together with slot-aware routing lets the same meta-frame support policy, world-model, and value prediction over a shared set of robot variables. On RoboCasa, MetaWAM reaches 68.9% success while collapsing the per-signal frames into one and cutting inference latency by 39%, and attains 85.0% success on three real-world Franka tasks. Code and models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [107] [What Factors Matter for Collection-Level Counting with Human-AI Collaboration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14380)
*Seoyun Jeong,Logan Lawrence,Owen L. Martin,Benjamin Koger,Daniel Sheldon,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 跨12个任务研究人在回路计数框架下模型、估计器与适配策略的选择，发现多模态模型为强零样本起点、微调检测器在大标注预算/重复部署时更优，主动优先标注可提升估计效率，小规模试点可预测排序质量；航拍叉角羚部署中仅审查3.2%图像即达±5%容差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和政策决策需估计大规模图像集合中的目标总量，穷举人工标注不可行，而原始模型预测在域偏移下存在偏差；已有的人机协同估计方法缺乏对新部署下模型/估计器/适配策略选择的实践指导。

Method: 在统一的人在回路计数框架下，跨12个任务（生物医学显微、地面与航空野生动物调查、城市结构航拍影像）系统比较模型选择、估计器与适配策略；采用模型预测优先化人工标注单元（非均匀采样），并用小规模试点集估计各模型的排序质量。

Result: 现代多模态模型在多数任务上提供强免训练起点；微调检测器在大标注预算与重复部署时更具优势；模型引导的主动标注提升估计效率；试点集可有效预测排序质量，为新任务策略选择提供指导；航拍叉角羚实地部署中中位仅审查3.2%图像即达±5%精度容差。

Conclusion: 提供面向新计数任务的模型、估计器与适配策略选择实证指南，并在真实野外调查中验证了人机协同估计的高样本效率与可用性。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model's ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey's $\pm 5\%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2\%$ of collected images.

</details>


### [108] [OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14420)
*Runjia Li,Minghao Chen,Junyu Xie,Philip Torr,Andrea Vedaldi,Tomas Jakab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从预训练基座模型 V-JEPA 2 出发，用免标注对比式适配（同运动不同场景拉近、同场景不同运动推远）在不加重建瓶颈的情况下提取“动作表征”，并在动作表达力、场景不变性、动作条件视频生成三维度跨域评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型训练受限于动作监督：动作标签稀缺、规格不统一、无法随无标注视频语料扩展。现有“从零联合训练动作编码器+下一帧重建解码器”的潜动作方法有两个缺陷：从零学动作空间泛化到未见域差；重建瓶颈在“表达力 vs. 场景解耦”之间难以权衡（紧瓶颈丢细节，松瓶颈泄漏场景）。

Method: 以 V-JEPA 2 帧对嵌入作为动作信息先验，提出免标签对比式适配框架：正样本为不同场景下的相同运动帧对，负样本为同场景下不同运动的帧对，从而重塑预训练表征空间几何；不引入帧重建瓶颈。评测上提出统一协议，含动作表达力、场景不变性、动作条件视频生成质量三轴，覆盖机器人操作、相机导航、游戏、驾驶四类基准（多数在适配阶段未见）。

Result: 相比免标注潜动作方法，所学表征表达力显著更强，同时场景不变性与之相当；由此构建的世界模型能更忠实地把演示过的动作迁移到新场景，且在新域上仍有优势。

Conclusion: 表明“从预训练视频表征出发做几何重塑”比“从零学潜动作+重建瓶颈”是更可扩展的动作监督替代路径，并提出可复用的多轴评测协议；局限在于适配仍依赖运动/场景划分式对比构造，动作语义的物理可解释性未直接给出。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

</details>


### [109] [Metric-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14436)
*Luka Dragar,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Borut Batagelj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先预测后解释"的解耦式可解释深度伪造检测流程：视觉基础模型集成分类，Qwen3-VL-8B生成决策条件解释，再由第二个Qwen3-VL-8B将解释压缩为非专家摘要，并用指标对齐的GRPO优化两个解释器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测仅在真/假分类上评估，而实际应用需要说明预测依据；端到端联合视觉语言模型难以准确判断真实性，会对被篡改图像生成"真实"叙述。

Method: 解耦流水线：(1) 视觉基础模型集成做真假分类；(2) 决策条件化（conditioned on 预测结果）由Qwen3-VL-8B生成视觉证据解释；(3) 对假图由第二个Qwen3-VL-8B在不看图像的情况下将解释压缩为简短非专家摘要；(4) 对两个解释器施加与评测指标对齐的GRPO，直接优化挑战赛评价标准。基于XPlainVerse基准训练与评估。

Result: 同条件下GRPO将复杂BERTScore-F1从0.686提升至0.712，并改善grounding（实体F1与证据F1）；提交在复杂BERTScore-F1上排名第一，总排名第七（ACM MM 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛）。

Conclusion: 解耦的"预测-解释"架构可规避联合VLM的真实性判断偏差，且用指标对齐的强化学习直接优化解释质量是有效增益手段；但属于竞赛型工作，样本限定于单一基准，缺乏跨域泛化与真实部署验证。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Joint vision-language models struggle with authenticity assessment, producing "real" narratives for manipulated images. To address this, we propose a decoupled predict-then-explain pipeline as our submission to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge, with methods trained and evaluated on the XPlainVerse benchmark. A vision-foundation-model ensemble first classifies the image, then a Qwen3-VL-8B model generates a decision-conditioned explanation of the visual evidence. For fake images, a second Qwen3-VL-8B compresses that text into a short non-expert summary without using the image. We further apply metric-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize both explainers against the challenge criteria. Under matched conditions, GRPO raises complex BERTScore-F1 from 0.686 to 0.712 and also improves grounding (entity and evidence F1); our submission ranked first on complex BERTScore-F1 and seventh overall.

</details>


### [110] [UGG: Unified Generative Geometry Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14440)
*Amani Kiruga,Zhiyi Li,Ruojin Cai,Hansen Lillemark,Xiaoshan Wu,Ayush Tewari,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UGG：统一4D场景理解框架，用跨模态、跨时间的不同噪声水平把RGB视频生成与几何（深度、相机位姿）预测统一为单一生成模型中的条件补全任务，支持异构数据训练与推理时基于几何证据的多假设搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D场景理解研究分裂为两类：前馈模型直接预测几何、生成模型做视觉合成；两者割裂，且难以利用几何/视觉标注子集不完整的异构数据。作者希望用生成式建模统一视觉合成与几何预测。

Method: 将RGB视频、深度、相机位姿视为同一生成过程中的不同模态，通过对各模态与时间步分配不同噪声水平，把生成与几何预测统一表述为条件补全；这一形式支持在几何标注不完整的数据集上联合训练；推理时用观测RGB帧提取的几何证据进行多假设搜索与重排序。

Result: 在相机可控视频生成、深度估计与相机位姿预测三类任务上均优于非统一基线，并在稀疏观测等困难设置下表现有效；摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 生成式统一建模可作为4D场景理解的有效范式，兼具异构数据利用与推理时搜索能力；但摘要缺少与SOTA的定量对比、实时性及真实场景泛化证据。

Abstract: Understanding 4D scenes is a core challenge in visual intelligence, requiring models to jointly capture scene geometry, appearance, and dynamics from partial observations. However, existing approaches are subdivided into feedforward models for direct geometric prediction, and generative models for visual synthesis. We introduce UGG, a unified framework for 4D scene understanding that jointly models RGB video and geometric representations such as depth and camera pose. By assigning different noise levels across modalities and time, UGG formulates visual generation and geometric prediction as conditional completion within a single generative model. This flexible formulation enables the model to learn from diverse, heterogeneous datasets with different subsets of geometric and visual annotations. Furthermore, our generative formulation enables inference-time search over multiple plausible scene hypotheses, using geometric evidence extracted from observed RGB frames to improve geometric prediction without ground truth geometry. We demonstrate UGG's effectiveness over non-unified baselines across camera-controlled video generation, depth estimation, and camera pose prediction, including in challenging sparse observation settings. Code and visualizations are available at this https URL

</details>


### [111] [A Lightweight Differentiable Method for Real-Time Skin Rendering and Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14451)
*Mario Marquez-Balduque,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Jorge Lopez-Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微分的屏幕空间次表面散射（SSSS）框架，从单光单视角轻量捕获中联合优化空间变化的基础贴图与散射距离，按面部区域估计散射参数并生成可直接用于实时渲染引擎的混合剖面图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤逆渲染通常依赖离线路径追踪估计物理属性，其结果无法直接映射到游戏引擎等交互应用所用近似着色器；需要直接估计面向实时渲染管线的次表面散射参数以弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建可微Screen-Space Subsurface Scattering模块，使基础反照率贴图与散射距离可联合优化；按不同面部区域分别估计参数以刻画局部皮肤厚度差异，并融合为剖面图（profile map）；捕获采用低成本单光源、单视角配置；优化使用掩码损失，仅在次表面散射效应主导区域施加监督；实验含45人Monk肤色量表的真实数据、合成数据、多光源验证单光假设，以及倾斜视角验证角度一致性。

Result: 方法可从轻量输入中有效恢复散射参数，渲染结果具备真实感皮肤外观，并与标准实时着色器直接兼容；真实数据集跨肤色分布验证了人群泛化性，多光源与倾斜视角实验分别支持单光假设与角度一致性。

Conclusion: 将逆渲染目标对齐到实时近似着色器空间是可行的，可微分屏幕空间散射模型为交互式皮肤外观的实用化采集与生产管线提供了端到端方案。

Abstract: Inverse rendering for skin typically relies on offline path tracing to estimate physical properties, which fail to map directly to the approximate shaders utilized in interactive applications, such as game engines. We bridge this gap by directly estimating subsurface scattering parameters tailored for real-time rendering pipelines. We introduce a differentiable Screen-Space Subsurface Scattering (SSSS) framework that enables the joint optimization of spatially varying base textures and scattering distances. To account for local variations in skin thickness, we estimate these parameters across distinct facial regions, generating a blended profile map ready for production runtime engines. Our pipeline utilizes a low-cost, single-light, single-view capture setup designed for accessible and practical appearance acquisition. Optimization is driven by a masked loss function targeting regions where subsurface scattering effects are dominant. We validate our approach on a real-world dataset of $45$ individuals across the Monk Skin Tone Scale, ensuring demographic generalization. Furthermore, we complement this evaluation with synthetic data, a multi-light setup validating our single-light assumption, and a tilted-view configuration confirming angular consistency. Results demonstrate that our method effectively recovers scattering parameters from lightweight inputs, yielding realistic skin appearance directly compatible with standard real-time shaders.

</details>


### [112] [C$^2$-Routing: Complementary Cropping and Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14469)
*Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出C²-Routing：面向VQA的模型无关视觉token削减框架，用裁剪/压缩双策略自适应路由，并以“安全门”按原始与缩减输入的联合正确性分布决定回退，在预算已限制为1024 token的前提下进一步降低36.53% FLOPs、42.36%视觉token，平均相对分数仅降0.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA中视觉语言模型计算开销大，模型无关的输入缩减方法可在黑盒场景通用；但硬性token上限会丢信息，单一缩减策略（裁剪或整图压缩）难以适配不同场景，需自适应选择并防止精度崩塌。

Method: 观察裁剪（突出小目标与局部细节）与整图压缩（保留大目标/分布式证据的上下文）互补，据此设计路由模块依图像与问题自适应选择策略；再引入安全门，利用原始输入与缩减输入下的联合正确性预测分布指导回退，避免关键信息丢失；所有训练与评测在RTX 3090上完成，基线统一以1024视觉token为上限。

Result: 在3个VLM、18个数据集（局部细节、全局场景、场景文本、文档）上评测：在token已封顶条件下，端到端FLOPs再降36.53%、视觉token再降42.36%，平均相对任务分数仅下降0.77%。

Conclusion: 裁剪与压缩路由加正确性门控是有效的模型无关视觉输入缩减方案，可在黑盒VLM上以极小精度代价换取显著算力节省，适用于消费级GPU部署。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) imposes substantial computational overhead on vision-language models (VLMs), motivating model-agnostic input reduction methods that can be readily applied across diverse VLMs, including in black-box settings. Simply enforcing a visual-token cap reduces computation, but it can discard information needed to answer the question. A single reduction strategy also struggles to accommodate different scenarios. Our key observation is that cropping and compression are complementary: cropping highlights small targets and local details, whereas whole-image compression better preserves context for large targets or distributed evidence. Based on this observation, we propose C$^2$-Routing, which adaptively selects cropping or compression according to the input image and question. Since both strategies may still discard key information, we further design a safety gate that uses the predicted joint correctness distribution under original and reduced inputs to guide fallback and protect accuracy. To accommodate consumer GPU resource constraints, all training and evaluation experiments run on RTX 3090 GPUs. To measure gains beyond token capping, we use full-image baselines with the same cap of 1,024 visual tokens. We conduct extensive evaluation on three VLMs and 18 datasets spanning local details, global scenes, scene text, and documents. With the visual budget already capped, C$^2$-Routing further reduces end-to-end FLOPs by 36.53% and visual tokens by 42.36% on average, with a mean relative task-score decrease of only 0.77%.

</details>


### [113] [BoviShift: A Six-Year Multi-Attribute Audit of Visual Cattle Phenotyping Across Animals and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14476)
*Mobasshir Bhuiya Shagor,Nilambar Halder Tonmoy,Md Saef Ullah Miah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoviShift: 6 consecutive annual cohorts (2021–2026) of an operational cattle market, 2,657 cattle / 10,610 images / 2,566 walk-around videos, used to compare image-random vs. cattle-disjoint splitting and future-cohort transfer for weight/age/height/breed/color prediction with a ResNet-50 multi-task baseline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Livestock vision datasets are prone to correlated-image leakage (same animal in train and test) and unrealistic image-random splits, and real deployment requires transfer to animals captured in future years; existing datasets lack long-spanning, temporally consecutive cohorts to quantify these effects.

Method: Rigorous cohort-aware evaluation protocol: four configurations (image-random split, pooled cattle-disjoint split on development cohorts, future-2025 evaluation, locked future-2026 evaluation) on a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline across five traits (weight, age, height, breed, coat color); ablations on multi-task training, still-view aggregation, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation.

Result: Image-random vs. cattle-disjoint gap is task-dependent, not a uniform leakage penalty; future-cohort degradation is attribute- and cohort-specific (breed and 2025 weight/age fall at or below constant baselines, height stays above baseline, color transfers to 2025 but degrades in 2026); multi-task training improves all five metrics in single-seed ablation; still-view aggregation helps selectively; zero-shot video-frame aggregation reveals a still-to-video domain gap.

Conclusion: The main contribution is a benchmark + evaluation-protocol lesson: identity-aware and temporal splits must be treated as first-class design choices in animal/livestock vision, and per-attribute conclusions are needed instead of aggregate leakage claims. Limited by a single backbone, single seed, and prediction-only scope.

Abstract: Computer vision offers a scalable, non-contact means of estimating livestock traits, but correlated images and video frames make reliable evaluation difficult. Image-random splitting can place views of the same animal in both training and test sets, while deployment further requires transfer to animals captured in future years. We introduce BoviShift, a corpus reconstructed from six consecutive annual cohorts of an operational cattle marketplace collected from 2021 to 2026. It contains 2{,}657 cattle, 10{,}610 still images, and 2{,}566 walk-around videos across nine recorded breed categories. We evaluate three splitting strategies through four configurations: image-random and pooled cattle-disjoint splits over the same development cohorts, followed by separate future-2025 and locked future-2026 evaluations. Using a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline, we assess weight, age, height, breed, and coat color. Differences between image-random and cattle-disjoint splitting are task-dependent rather than a universal leakage penalty. Future-cohort behavior is also attribute- and cohort-specific: breed declines relative to pooled evaluation; mean weight and age performance fails to beat constant baselines in 2025; height remains above baseline; and color transfers well to 2025 but degrades in 2026. Multi-task training improves all five point estimates in a single-seed ablation, still-view aggregation helps selectively, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation exposes a still-to-video domain gap. To our knowledge, BoviShift is the longest-spanning cattle-vision resource combining consecutive annual cohorts with physical and visual attributes.

</details>


### [114] [RS-JEPA: Remote Sensing Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with Scan-Path Views for Cross-Sensor Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14496)
*Tanishq Rachamalla,Srishti Kaushik,Muhammad Attique Khan,Gustau Camps-Valls,Swalpa Kumar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RS-JEPA：基于Vision Mamba共享编码器的紧凑联合嵌入预测框架，用于跨传感器（SAR-光学、PAN-MS）遥感影像检索；提出"扫描路径视图"（同一图像的两种读取顺序作为两视图）、对抗视图耦合(AVC)与邻域一致性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨传感器遥感检索自监督方法存在缺陷：要么重建传感器特有的像素细节，要么仅与共注册的双胞胎图像对齐，或依赖118–173M（最高398M）参数的大Transformer，模型臃肿且视图来源匮乏。

Method: 1) 双传感器共享的Vision Mamba（状态空间）编码器，将图像视为有序patch序列；2) 扫描路径视图：把同一图像的两种读取顺序视作像素完全相同的两个视图，用一阶可见patch预测另一阶的隐藏patch；3) Adversarial View Coupling，学习最难的可见patch划分以保持预测难度；4) 邻域一致性损失，对齐两传感器的场景相似度结构。检索网络仅3.88M参数（训练时14.57M）。

Result: 在SAR-光学（BEN-14K、CBRSIR-VS）与PAN-MS（DSRSID）基准上，十二种复现方法中F1@5最高：72.92、80.35、94.43；参数量比先前JEPA检索器少8–16倍。

Conclusion: 读取顺序可作为一种廉价有效的自监督视图来源；轻量状态空间JEPA在多传感器遥感检索中兼顾效率与精度，但仍依赖共注册图像对训练。

Abstract: Cross-sensor remote sensing image retrieval aims to find scenes with the same content across heterogeneous sensors, such as SAR and optical or panchromatic (PAN) and multispectral (MS) imagery, without manual labels. Existing self-supervised methods either reconstruct sensor-specific pixel detail, align each image only with its co-registered twin, or rely on large transformers with 118-173M parameters (and up to 398M). We introduce RS-JEPA, a compact joint-embedding predictive framework built on a Vision Mamba encoder shared by both sensors. Since a state-space encoder reads an image as an ordered sequence of patches, we propose scan-path views, which treat two reading orders of the same image as two views with identical pixels and predict the hidden patches in one order from the visible patches in the this http URL keep this prediction challenging, we propose Adversarial View Coupling (AVC), which learns the hardest split of the visible patches, and a neighbourhood consensus loss that aligns the scene similarities of both sensors. Extensive experiments on SAR-optical (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) and PAN-MS (DSRSID) benchmarks demonstrate that RS-JEPA achieves the highest F1@5 among twelve re-implemented methods (72.92, 80.35 and 94.43) with a retrieval network of only 3.88M parameters (14.57M in training, 8-16times fewer than prior JEPA retrievers). Like prior methods, RS-JEPA trains on co-registered pairs. Our results highlight reading order as a source of self-supervised views. code: this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [Measuring Robustness and Efficiency in a Connectome-Constrained Fly Visual System Model: Linking an Adversarial and a Perceptual Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14498)
*Alexandre Garcia-Duran,Alejandro Rodriguez-Garcia,Manuel Molano-Mazón,Alexandre Hyafil,Srikanth Ramaswamy,Janne K. Lappalainen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过受果蝇突触连接组约束的连续时间RNN（FlyNet）与CNN/RAFT/无连接组约束CTRNN在可微3D渲染碰撞检测任务上对比，发现连接组约束主要带来计算效率（FLOPs少达5个数量级）而非鲁棒性；并揭示了对抗纹理与反向phi错觉共享同一低层运动统计量的电路级脆弱机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物视觉系统在代谢与布线约束下仍可靠工作，而人工视觉系统能耗高且易受对抗扰动。作者想检验：具脑式布线的网络是否在更低算力下更鲁棒。这一问题关系到连接组约束究竟是带来效率优势还是鲁棒性优势。

Method: 在可微3D渲染管线中构建碰撞检测任务，将受果蝇视叶突触连接组约束的、任务优化的连续时间递归网络（FlyNet）与CNN、RAFT及无连接组约束的sCTRNN/CTRNN进行系统基准测试。评估覆盖对抗攻击（白盒）、常见视频损坏、对抗纹理与反向phi错觉，并做反事实纹理控制实验与输入运动统计量定向优化。

Result: FlyNet对对抗攻击脆弱，但在同等算力下对常见视频损坏比RAFT更鲁棒，且FLOPs少达5个数量级；然而CTRNN在几乎所有扰动下都比FlyNet更鲁棒。递归结构提升RAFT对白盒攻击的鲁棒性，时序上下文提升对常见损坏的鲁棒性。FlyNet的位置/速度表征在对抗纹理下退化，但在等距离的反事实纹理下可恢复并正确分类，排除"陌生纹理"解释；反向phi错觉同样破坏该表征并反转同一低层运动统计量，单独优化该统计量即可诱发分类失败。

Conclusion: 连接组约束带来的主要是效率（低边数）而非鲁棒性收益；连接组约束模型可作为生成关于生物知觉脆弱性的可检验假设的工具，把对抗失效机制归因于具体电路级缺陷。

Abstract: Biological visual systems compute reliably under tight metabolic and wiring constraints, whereas artificial vision systems consume far more energy and can be sensitive to adversarial perturbations. Whether networks with brain-like wiring are more robust at lower computational cost is unclear. We address this by benchmarking a task-optimized, continuous-time recurrent neural network of the fly visual system constrained by its synaptic connectome (FlyNet) against artificial neural networks. We compare it against CNNs, RAFT, and continuous-time recurrent neural networks without detailed connectome constraints (sCTRNN, CTRNN) on a collision-detection task within a differentiable 3D rendering pipeline. FlyNet is adversarially vulnerable but, relative to its compute, more robust to common video corruptions than RAFT, while using up to five orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs. The CTRNNs remain more robust than FlyNet across almost all tested perturbations, suggesting that connectome constraints benefit efficiency, through their low edge count, over robustness. Recurrence improves robustness to white-box attacks in RAFT and temporal context improves robustness to common corruptions. FlyNet's position and velocity representations degrade under adversarial textures but recover under counterfactual textures that restore correct classification while staying equally far from training, ruling out unfamiliar textures as the cause of failure. The reverse-phi illusion similarly disrupts these representations and inverts the same low-level motion statistic as the adversarial textures. Optimizing this input statistic alone, without access to the network, suffices to induce classification failure, attributing both perturbations to the same circuit vulnerability. Connectome-constrained models can thus surface adversarial failure mechanisms as testable hypotheses about biological perceptual vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [116] [Toward Foundation Detection Models via Example-Based Grounding in EM-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14518)
*Sheethal Bhat,Bogdan Georgescu,Awais Mansoor,Florin C. Ghesu,Sasa Grbic,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨异质标注空间的医学病灶检测多域基础模型，采用学生-教师训练策略融合多数据集，并在EM-DETR示例驱动框架上联合多域学习，在CXR与乳腺摄影上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学病灶检测缺乏大规模、标准化标注数据集，现有数据集异质性强，难以构建稳健的基础模型。

Method: 1) 学生-教师训练策略融合标签空间异质的多源数据，缓解灾难性遗忘并改善特征学习；2) 将示例驱动的EM-DETR扩展为多域联合学习，实现跨模态细粒度检测。

Result: CXR四个关键征象的图像级AUC较此前SOTA提升约1个百分点；CXR与乳腺摄影mAP50领先，较单数据集训练的EM-DETR提升2–3个百分点。

Conclusion: 异质数据融合与示例驱动检测相结合可支撑可扩展的医学影像基础模型，兼顾鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: DeepLearning (DL)-based Computer-Aided Diagnostic (CAD) systems have shown great promise in supporting clinicians, but their performance heavily depends on the availability of large, well-annotated datasets. In medical lesion detection, the scarcity of comprehensive, stan dardized annotations and the heterogeneity of existing datasets limit the development of robust foundation models. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to building a multi-domain foundation model for le sion detection by integrating diverse medical image datasets annotated for both anatomical structures and abnormalities. We first propose a student-teacher training strategy to effectively combine datasets with heterogeneous label spaces, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and im proving feature learning. In addition, building on our previously pro posed example-based EM-DETR framework, we adapt it to jointly learn from multiple domain-specific datasets, enabling fine-grained, example driven detection across modalities. Our model achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results, with image-level AUC scores exceeding previous SOTA by 1% point on four key findings in CXRs. We achieve a leading mAP50 in CXRs and mammography, surpassing EM-DETR trained on a single dataset by 2-3% points. This approach paves the way for scalable foundation models in medical imaging that leverage heterogeneous data while maintaining robust and generalizable performance.

</details>


### [117] [Medical Image Segmentation: Foundation Model Adaptation versus Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14519)
*Emre Kutlusan,Yongmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医学图像分割中，比较 DINOv2 基础模型（从仅调偏置到全量微调）与从头训练的 U-Net/ResU-Net。外部机构数据集上基础模型泛化更好；训练分布内数据充足时专用模型仍有竞争力且边界更精确；仅需微调极小容量，所需容量由成像域而非微调策略决定；并发现常用息肉测试集与另一基准存在图像重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型与任务专用模型的对比多局限于训练分布内数据，且基线配置单一、缺乏依据，无法证明在新临床站点上的性能；同时"需要适配多少参数、由什么决定"尚不清楚。

Method: 以 DINOv2 为基础模型，采用从仅调偏置项到全量微调的多种适配策略；与从头训练的 U-Net、ResU-Net 对比；覆盖结肠镜、乳腺超声、皮肤镜三个域；在训练来源的留出数据和来自其他机构的独立数据集上测试。

Result: 适配后的基础模型在所有外部数据集上泛化更好；训练分布内数据充足时专用模型具竞争力且边界分割更精确，基础模型更易漏检小病灶；仅需微调极小比例参数，所需容量由成像域决定而非适配方法；发现广泛使用的息肉测试集与另一基准存在重复图像。

Conclusion: 跨站点部署时应采用基础模型适配，并按目标域选择适配容量；与具身智能/3D 核心方向无关，属医学影像领域 CV 的实证对比研究，价值在结论与基准数据泄漏提示，而非可迁移的方法创新。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large natural-image collections are increasingly adapted for medical image segmentation, in the expectation that they will replace models trained for each task. Most comparisons, however, use data from the training distribution and a single task-specific baseline whose configuration is rarely justified, and therefore provide limited evidence of performance at new clinical sites. It also remains unclear how much of a foundation model must be adapted, and what determines this. We adapted a foundation model, DINOv2, with strategies ranging from tuning only its bias terms to full fine-tuning, and compared it with U-Nets and ResU-Nets trained from scratch. Experiments covered colonoscopy, breast ultrasound and dermoscopy, with testing on held-out data from the training source and on independent datasets from other institutions. The adapted foundation model generalised better on every external dataset. Within the training distribution, the task-specific models remained competitive when training data were abundant, and delineated boundaries more precisely, while the foundation model more often missed small lesions. Only a small fraction of the foundation model required adaptation, and the capacity needed was determined by the imaging domain rather than by the adaptation method. A widely used polyp test set was also found to duplicate images from another benchmark. Foundation-model adaptation is therefore warranted where models must work across sites, with capacity selected on the target domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Post-OCR Correction of Mixed-Script Uzbek Text: Byte-Level ByT5 with an Empirical Homoglyph Noise Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13609)
*Mannon Ochilov,Oybek Narzullayev,Bahodir Zaripov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 乌兹别克语OCR中，西里尔/拉丁混排页面的同形字错误密度约为单文种文本的5倍；作者用字节级ByT5+基于359个手工标注错误事件估计权重的噪声生成器+Levenshtein约束的保守解码，在本地硬件上完成训练，Gold集上CER从0.0578降至0.0369，恢复91.3%同形字且仅引入84个新错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌兹别克语正处在文字转换期，官方与技术文档中同页甚至同句内混用西里尔与拉丁字母，跨字母表视觉相同的字符导致大量OCR错误，且这类错误并非源于图像质量。同时工程地质与ICT基础设施报告不允许离开机构，因此全流程必须本地部署，无法依赖云服务。

Method: 先统计并量化混排页面的同形字错误密度（约为单文种文本的5倍）；构建字节级ByT5纠错模型；设计噪声生成器，其采样权重由359个手工分类的错误事件估计，用于模拟真实同形字/字符级噪声进行训练；推理端采用Levenshtein距离有界的保守解码规则以抑制过度修改；全流程本地硬件执行。

Result: 在5,204对行的Gold集上，字符错误率从0.0578降至0.0369（约36%相对下降），同形字恢复率达91.3%，同时仅引入84个新错误，表明保守解码有效控制了误纠。

Conclusion: 针对多字母表混排造成的同形字混淆，结合真实错误分布驱动的合成噪声与受限解码的字节级Seq2Seq纠错，可在完全本地化、隐私受限的条件下取得显著CER改善且副作用极小。

Abstract: In Uzbek official and technical documents, a large share of OCR errors is caused not by poor image quality but by characters that are visually identical across the two alphabets in use. Because the script transition is still under way, Cyrillic and Latin appear on the same page and often within a single sentence, and in our measurements the density of homoglyph errors on such pages is close to five times higher than in single-script text. We study this problem in a setting where the corpus of engineering-geological and ICT infrastructure reports may not leave the organisation, so every stage was executed on local hardware. The proposed system combines a byte-level ByT5 model with a noise generator whose sampling weights are estimated from 359 manually classified error events, together with a Levenshtein-bounded conservative decoding rule. On a Gold set of 5,204 line pairs it lowers the character error rate from 0.0578 to 0.0369, restores 91.3% of the homoglyphs and introduces only 84 new errors.

</details>


### [119] [Word-Level Alignment of OCR and Ground Truth under Zoning and Reading-Order Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13752)
*Fonseca Cacho,Jorge Ramón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视2019年提出的OCR后处理“Zoning Problem”：当分栏/分区错误打乱阅读顺序时，单调对齐器会“失位”。作者提出一套在阅读顺序置换条件下测试正确性的协议，并以构建期词级gold评估正确匹配F1、级联长度与恢复能力，比较了2019位置感知对齐器、其re-anchoring扩展、RETAS-lite基线、多区域对齐器（唯一词+唯一n-gram锚点配合chunk-to-window分配）、genalog版RETAS与原始Java RETAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR后处理需要词级对齐的训练/评估数据，但数据集很少提供词与ground truth的映射；而单调对齐器在分区错误导致阅读顺序被置换时会失去位置跟踪。作者希望用一个可复现的协议，系统地量化各类对齐方法在阅读顺序置换下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在OCR侧施加阅读顺序置换，并利用构建时已知的词级gold，评测正确匹配F1、级联长度与恢复率。对比六种方法：2019位置感知对齐器、其re-anchoring扩展、RETAS-lite（RETAS风格基线）、多区域对齐器（unique-word与unique n-gram锚点+chunk-to-window分配）、genalog的RETAS、原始Java RETAS。实验数据：带块移动/段落交换/列交错的人工字符噪声、历史报纸阅读顺序置换、Tesseract OCR的几何推导gold、小规模word-box集合。

Result: 合成实验显示：局部跟踪在大位移后会失效；依赖唯一锚点的方法在唯一锚点稀缺时崩溃。在合成的boilerplate页面上，2019方法在所测方法中F1最高。历史报纸与几何gold评测受近似gold和文章顺序约定的限制。首个多区域版本存在二次方扩展成本。

Conclusion: 局部跟踪在大幅阅读顺序变化下仍很脆弱；锚点类方法能处理多数此类情形，但唯一锚点稀缺时失效。近似gold、文章顺序约定、缺少残差标注以及缺乏现代多栏词级ground truth仍是主要局限。对实际OCR后处理流程而言，这是一个诊断性、对齐鲁棒性导向的工作，而非新的OCR识别模型。

Abstract: OCR post-processing needs OCR text aligned word by word with its ground truth. Data sets rarely provide that mapping, and a monotone aligner can lose its place when zoning errors permute the reading order. We revisit this problem, the Zoning Problem of our 2019 paper, with a protocol that tests correctness under such permutations. We apply OCR-side reading-order permutations with construction-time word-level gold and score correct-match F1, cascade length and recovery. We compare the 2019 location-aware aligner, a re-anchoring extension of it, a RETAS-style baseline (RETAS-lite), a multi-region aligner that combines unique-word and unique n-gram anchors with chunk-to-window assignment, genalog's RETAS and the original Java RETAS. Experiments use synthetic character noise with block moves, paragraph swaps and column interleaving, historical newspaper reading-order permutations, geometry-derived gold for Tesseract OCR, and a small word-box set. Synthetic tests show where local tracking fails after large displacements and where unique-anchor methods collapse when unique anchors are scarce. On our synthetic boilerplate page the 2019 method had the highest F1 among those tested. Historical newspaper and geometry-derived evaluations carry the limitations of approximate gold and the article-order convention. The first multi-region version has a quadratic scaling cost. Local tracking remains fragile under large reading-order changes; the tested anchor-based methods handle many of those cases but fail when unique anchors are scarce. Approximate gold, the article-order convention, missing residual annotation, and the lack of modern multi-column word-level ground truth remain limitations.

</details>


### [120] [Visual Similarity in Adult Word Extension and Artificial Word Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14116)
*Patricio M. Vera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 用冻结的 DINOv2 编码器度量测试图像与教学示例的视觉距离，解释成人与人工学习者的词汇过度扩展现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两个对象共享名称时，视觉相似性能解释多少泛化，以及教学示例的构成是否提供距离之外的信息。

Method: （1）重新分析 Lewis & Frank (2018) 的成人词义扩展实验逐试次数据（520 人），以 DINOv2 表征计算测试图到示例的距离并做混合效应回归，控制分类层级与教学条件；（2）构建使用同一编码器的人工学习者，学习合并两个类别的词，考察类别间距与命名准确率的关系，并与三个人类相似度/混淆数据集对齐；（3）用基于单名儿童经验训练的第二编码器做稳健性检验。

Result: 距离与扩展显著相关（每标准差 log odds -3.23）；示例构成影响巨大：三个下位示例后成人仅扩展 1.1% 上位项，上位示例后达 77.5%。人工学习者近/中/远类对的命名准确率为 84.7%/65.4%/53.0%；编码器距离与人类相似度相关 rho=.501-.795，但未能确证人类距离可预测学习者难度，第二编码器仅复现层级排序而不复现配对级效应。

Conclusion: 视觉相似性部分解释了两种系统中的泛化组织方式，而示例构成贡献了所用距离度量无法捕捉的信息。

Abstract: When two objects share a name, how much does their visual similarity explain, and what do the teaching examples add? First, we reanalyzed trial-level data from a large adult word-extension study (Lewis & Frank, 2018), computing each test image's distance to the examples shown in the space of a frozen self-supervised visual encoder (DINOv2). Distance was associated with extension (log odds -3.23 per standard deviation; 520 people), and an exploratory post hoc analysis suggested that this association persisted within the taught domain after controlling for taxonomic level and teaching condition. Example composition also mattered: after three examples, adults extended a word to 1.1% of superordinate items when the examples were subordinate and to 77.5% when they were superordinate. Second, in an artificial learner using the same encoder, words joining two categories were learned less accurately the farther apart the categories were (84.7%, 65.4%, and 53.0% naming accuracy for near, mid, and far pairs). The encoder's distances correlated with human similarity and confusability measures from three datasets (rho = .501-.795), but this representational alignment did not translate into conclusive evidence that human-derived distances predict learner difficulty, and a second encoder trained on one child's experience reproduced the tier ordering but not the pair-level effect. The secondary human data were analyzed under an internal plan fixed before the confirmatory analyses (not externally preregistered). Visual similarity accounts for part of how generalization is organized in both systems, while the composition of examples contributes what the distance we used does not capture.

</details>


### [121] [Mechanistic Interpretability Needs a Logic of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14483)
*Zhimin Hu,Chen Jiang,Hanbo Xie,N.Apurva Ratan Murty,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出"机制推断"的方法论框架：借鉴神经科学与机制可解释性（MI）的平行经验，识别从复杂神经系统中推断机制时的共同推断瓶颈，并给出五条原则组成的递归逻辑，用于更严谨的机制推断流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI 已能定位神经元、特征、电路、群体结构与动力学模式，但学界关注点正从"能观察到什么"转向"观察如何支撑机制性解释"；缺乏对机制推断过程本身的规范性约束，导致局部发现难以累积。

Method: 概念性/方法论论文，无新方法或实验：对比神经科学与 MI 的推断实践，归纳反复出现的推断瓶颈，提炼五条原则并组织为递归式的机制推断逻辑框架。

Result: 提出一个可操作的推断框架，支撑两类科学发现：将局部可解释性发现转化为模型内机制的累积性知识；使机制足够精确以生成可检验的领域外假设。并强调最终仍需 MI 之外的证据来确定模型机制对现实世界的揭示力。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性需要规范化的推断逻辑而非仅更多观察工具；模型机制的外部有效性必须由模型外证据确立。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly studied not merely as predictive systems, but as complex systems whose internal computations are themselves objects of scientific inquiry. Recent progress in mechanistic interpretability (MI) has made it possible to identify neurons, features, circuits, population structures, and dynamical patterns associated with model behavior. As these tools improve, attention is shifting from what can be observed inside a model to how those observations support mechanistic explanations. We examine the parallel efforts of neuroscience and MI to address this question and identify recurring inferential bottlenecks when inferring mechanisms from complex neural systems. We propose five principles and organize them into a recursive logic for a more rigorous procedure of mechanistic inference. These principles provide a framework that supports scientific discovery in two senses: it helps turn local interpretability findings into cumulative knowledge of mechanisms within models, and it makes those mechanisms sufficiently precise to generate testable hypotheses about domains beyond the models. Evidence beyond MI is ultimately required to establish what model mechanisms reveal about the world.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Oscillatory Processing Units as a computing substrate for biomimetic intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13840)
*Alexander A. Wolf*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出"振荡处理单元"(OPU)——一组共享节律的合成神经元,其有向交互将输入活动与先前接收状态映射为"振荡占用空间"(OOS),并以此作为可复用的计算基元;作者构建关系图谱(Atlas)支持前瞻性组合,并演示了在固定突触权重下完成逻辑/数值运算、联想回忆与闭环决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物智能源于连接感知、记忆与行动的神经活动,突触可塑性、神经元集群与振荡原理为仿生计算提供启示,但要将其转化为可复用的算子,需要一个明确定义的计算单元。作者希望建立一个既能承载经验、又能被组合复用的仿生计算基底。

Method: 定义OPU为共享节律的合成神经元群;OPU 间的有向交互把输入活动与既有接收状态变换到振荡占用空间OOS,该空间可被后续计算访问。系统刻画源-接收条件与振荡组织对变换的几何、持久性与历史可及性的影响,并用实测关系构建Atlas以支持按预测响应类别的前瞻组合;在固定突触权重下配置电路执行逻辑、数值运算与闭环决策。

Result: 源-接收条件与振荡组织共同塑造OOS变换的几何结构、持续性与历史可及性;Atlas能够支持带预测响应类别的组合式电路设计;配置后的电路在固定权重下实现逻辑与数值运算、联想式回忆以及闭环决策。

Conclusion: OPU与OOS构成面向仿生智能的计算基底,使"经验"始终保留在状态之中,新输入经由该状态转化为行动;该抽象层面的框架为组合式、可解释的神经形态计算提供了可复用的基础单元。

Abstract: Biological intelligence emerges from neural activity that connects sensation, memory and action. Synaptic plasticity, neuronal assemblies and oscillations provide principles for biomimetic computing, but translating them into reusable operations requires a defined computational unit. We introduce Oscillatory Processing Units (OPUs), groups of synthetic neurons sharing a rhythm. Their directed interactions transform incoming activity and prior receiving state into an Oscillatory Occupancy Space (OOS), which becomes available to subsequent computation. We show that source--receiver conditions and oscillatory organization shape these transformations, including their geometry, persistence and historical accessibility. An Atlas of measured relationships supports prospective composition with predicted response classes. Configured circuits perform logical and numerical operations, express associative recall and generate closed-loop decisions with fixed synaptic weights. OPUs and OOS establish a computing substrate for biomimetic intelligence in which experience remains part of the state through which new input becomes action.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [GARD: Gradient-Guided Adversarial Representation Defense against Backdoors in Multimodal Contrastive Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14143)
*Yuhong Wang,Yujun Cai,Lei Feng,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出GARD，一种目标与触发器无关的事后防御方法，通过全局+局部对抗探针探测表征敏感度并重新对齐干净锚点，修复被后门污染的CLIP类多模态对比编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等多模态对比模型易受后门攻击，触发器从局部 patch 到全局分布式扰动不等，且新型攻击在表征空间优化触发器。现有事后防御中，干净微调可能残留后门，触发器反演法则依赖对攻击目标与触发器的准确估计，因此需要一种不依赖目标/触发器先验的通用防御。

Method: 观察到后门编码器具有结构依赖的表征敏感性（全局扰动更能暴露分布式后门，局部 patch 更能暴露局部后门）。据此优化互补的全局与局部对抗探针，使干净图像表征最大程度偏移，再用 detached 的干净锚点将扰动后表征拉回，同时保持图文对齐；推理阶段零额外开销。

Result: 在6种视觉后门攻击与2种CLIP视觉骨干共12个组合上，攻击成功率全部降至1%以下，同时保持有竞争力的干净准确率。

Conclusion: 对抗表征探测可有效修复被后门注入的多模态对比编码器，为后门防御提供了一种目标/触发器无关、推理零成本的通用思路。

Abstract: Multimodal contrastive learning models such as CLIP are vulnerable to backdoor attacks with diverse trigger structures, ranging from localized patches to globally distributed perturbations, with recent attacks increasingly optimizing these triggers in the representation space. Existing post-hoc defenses based on clean fine-tuning may leave backdoors active, while trigger-inversion methods depend on accurately estimating the attack target and trigger. We observe that backdoored encoders exhibit structure-dependent representation sensitivity: global perturbations preferentially expose distributed backdoors, whereas local patch perturbations are more effective for localized backdoors. Based on this observation, we propose Gradient-Guided Adversarial Representation Defense (GARD), a target- and trigger-agnostic post-hoc defense. GARD optimizes complementary global and local adversarial probes to maximally displace clean-image representations, and then realigns the perturbed representations with detached clean anchors while preserving image-text alignment. The resulting model requires no additional computation at inference time. Across six visual backdoor attacks and two CLIP visual backbones, GARD reduces the attack success rate below 1% in all 12 evaluated attack-backbone combinations while maintaining competitive clean accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of adversarial representation probing for repairing backdoored multimodal contrastive encoders.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [D-TINs for Track-Sampled Ice Sheet Surface Reconstruction from Satellite Altimetry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12894)
*Xiaoyu Xia,Noah Bergam,Abani Patra*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 针对卫星测高沿轨密、跨轨稀的不规则采样问题，提出直接在观测点上做Delaunay三角剖分与重心插值的D-TIN方法，并给出固定权重的多尺度扩展MS-D-TIN，精度接近普通克里金而中位耗时降低约252倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星测高的空间覆盖呈"沿轨稠密、跨轨稀疏"的条带结构，常规模格化重建（克里金、分块多项式）在此类不规则支撑上误差大或计算昂贵，需要一种既适应轨迹几何又计算高效的重建方法。

Method: 提出D-TIN：直接以观测位置构建Delaunay三角网并做重心坐标插值，不做格点重采样；设计空间留出评测，移除连续轨迹区间及邻近同轨观测以模拟真实空缺；对比普通克里金与基于5 km分块的多项式插值工作流；进一步以固定权重方式融合多尺度三角网得到MS-D-TIN。

Result: 在ICESat-1七个campaign上，D-TIN相比独立的5 km分块多项式重建将RMSE降低12.1–23.6 m；总体精度接近GStatSim平台1 km格网上普通克里金工作流，而中位运行时间低252倍；MS-D-TIN取得最低总体RMSE 67.5 m，同时保持低计算成本。

Conclusion: 对条带式不规则采样数据，直接在观测几何上做三角剖分插值可在几乎不损精度前提下大幅降低计算量，且易于通过多尺度加权扩展进一步提精，适合大尺度冰盖高程重建的大规模批处理场景。

Abstract: Satellite altimetry samples ice sheets densely along tracks but sparsely across tracks, with frequent gaps in spatial coverage. We study the impact of this irregular support on surface reconstruction and introduce a new method, D-TIN, that applies Delaunay triangulation and barycentric interpolation directly to observed locations. A spatial holdout removes contiguous track intervals and nearby same-pass observations. We compare D-TIN with two common workflows, kriging and tile-based polynomial interpolation, to evaluate its accuracy and computational efficiency. Across seven ICESat-1 campaigns, D-TIN reduces RMSE by 12.1 m to 23.6 m relative to independent 5 km tile and polynomial based reconstruction. Its aggregate accuracy is close to the ordinary kriging workflow using a 1 km grid on the commonly used GStatSim platform while its median runtime is 252 times lower. A fixed-weight multiscale extension of D-TIN, called MS-D-TIN, gives the lowest aggregate RMSE, 67.5 m, while retaining the low computational cost of the D-TIN family.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Is a CNN-Transformer Ensemble Worth It When a Chest X-Ray Foundation Model Is Available?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13014)
*Aditya Seelaboyina,Irfan Khan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 在NIH ChestX-ray14患者级划分上，单一胸片预训练基础模型EVA-X-S（宏AUROC 0.828）显著优于5个ImageNet预训练骨干的集成（0.814）及与EVA-X-S的混合集成，且成本仅约1/7.8延迟、1/10参数；配平整定（full-stacking）未超过校准平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签胸片分类中，ImageNet预训练CNN/ViT集成被当作常规提点手段；但当日志预训练（chest radiograph foundation model）可用时，集成是否仍具成本效益尚不明确，需要严格的统计检验来验证。

Method: 在NIH ChestX-ray14官方患者无重叠划分（25,596测试图像，2,797患者）上，用仅验证集选模训练5个ImageNet预训练骨干与EVA-X-S；比较5种融合规则；所有差异用患者级配对bootstrap估计置信区间；并进行配方交叉实验（ImageNet骨干改用EVA-X-S训练配方、EVA-X-S改用ImageNet配方）与三随机种子稳定性测试。

Result: EVA-X-S单模型0.828 vs 5模型集成0.814（差-0.0136，95% CI -0.0178~-0.0098），后者延迟约7.8倍、参数约10倍；与EVA-X-S平均融合为-0.0056（-0.0095~-0.0021）；ImageNet骨干用EVA-X-S配方训练后集成0.817，仍低-0.0109（-0.0142~-0.0076）；EVA-X-S三种子波动≤0.001 AUROC；但换用ImageNet配方EVA-X-S降至0.798；full-stacking从未超过校准平均。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，ImageNet集成未能在单一胸片基础模型（按其自身配方微调）之上带来增益；预训练域匹配与训练配方对域内性能影响显著，集成收益依赖于骨干配方一致性。

Abstract: Ensembles of ImageNet-pretrained convolutional networks and Vision Transformers are a common way to improve multi-label chest X-ray classification. We ask whether such an ensemble is still worth its cost when a model pretrained on chest radiographs is available. On the official patient-disjoint split of NIH ChestX-ray14 (25,596 test images, 2,797 patients), we train five ImageNet-pretrained backbones and the EVA-X-S foundation model with validation-only model selection, compare five fusion rules, and estimate every difference with a patient-level paired bootstrap. EVA-X-S alone reached a test macro-AUROC of 0.828; the five-model ensemble reached 0.814 (difference -0.0136, 95% CI -0.0178 to -0.0098) at about 7.8 times the summed backbone latency and ten times the parameters. Averaging the five ImageNet models with EVA-X-S changed AUROC by -0.0056 (-0.0095 to -0.0021). With all five ImageNet backbones trained with the EVA-X-S recipe, their ensemble scored 0.817, still -0.0109 (-0.0142 to -0.0076) below EVA-X-S, and across three seeds EVA-X-S varied by at most 0.001 AUROC. Under the ImageNet models' recipe, however, EVA-X-S fell to 0.798. Our full-stacking configuration never outperformed calibrated averaging. In this setting, the tested ImageNet ensembles did not improve on a single chest X-ray foundation model fine-tuned with its own recipe.

</details>


### [126] [Counterfactual Analysis of Extent Leakage from Annotation-Derived Prompts in Vision-Language Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13970)
*Niran Nataraj,Ankita Das,C. Krishna Mohan,Kenji Kawashima*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文发现文本引导医学分割中，标题（caption）通过"尺寸/位置词"泄漏掩码范围信息，称为 extent leakage；仅保留这些词即可恢复大部分收益，且该泄漏可从训练标题中学到，并提出将标题语义与标签信息解耦的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准上文本字幕显著提升分割精度，但结肠镜与乳腺超声的字幕尺寸/数量/位置词由后续用于评分的掩码计算而来，胸片字幕由标注者撰写，存在标签泄漏；作者想检验模型究竟从字幕中学到了什么。

Method: 在已发表的两个文本引导分割模型上做消融：只保留尺寸词或肺区分区短语、去除该词、篡改病种名称（将息肉称为"石头"）；在三个数据集上重训练以考察习惯来源；并用仅字幕词预测病灶面积（无模型方法）量化泄漏程度，再以无测试掩码的图像估计范围方法做对照。

Result: 结肠镜与胸片上收益主要来自病灶范围：仅保留尺寸词/肺区短语可恢复空字幕相对提升的84–98%，删除则损失52–77%；其余文本几乎无用，错误病种命名不影响分割。乳腺超声中近半数字幕含诊断信息并贡献部分收益。重训练显示模型部分从训练字幕习得该习惯，并削弱了仅凭图像的分割能力。仅字幕词预测病灶面积在7个测试集中的6个上R²为0.54–0.77。无测试掩码时最佳方法在结肠镜上Dice 74.59，低于发表的77.23。

Conclusion: 提示文本引导医学分割基准存在严重extent leakage，字幕的增益多为对标签的复制；主张评测协议应区分字幕对图像的描述与对标签的复制，并支持无模型的事前泄漏检测。

Abstract: Text-guided segmentation models receive an image and a short caption about the lesion, and on public benchmarks the caption improves accuracy considerably. On colonoscopy and breast ultrasound, however, the size, count and location words of each caption were computed from the mask that later scores the prediction, and the chest X-ray captions were written by benchmark annotators. We tested what two published models take from them. On colonoscopy and chest X-ray, it is mostly the lesion's extent. Keeping only the size word or the lung-zone phrase recovers 84 to 98 percent of the improvement over an empty caption, and removing it loses 52 to 77 percent. The rest matters little, and a polyp called a rock is segmented just as well. We call this extent leakage. On breast ultrasound, nearly half the captions also name the diagnosis, which carries part of the benefit. Retraining on three datasets shows that models pick up this habit from their training captions, though only partly on breast ultrasound, and that it weakens their segmentation from the image alone. The leak can be measured without training a model, since caption words alone predict lesion area (coefficient of determination 0.54 to 0.77 on six of seven test sets). Without the test mask, the best method we tested, which estimates extent from the image, reaches 74.59 Dice on colonoscopy against the published 77.23. We propose an evaluation protocol that keeps what a caption says about the image apart from what it copies from the label.

</details>


### [127] [TransGHOST: Real-Time, Synthesis-Free Reconstruction of Instrument-Occluded Regions in Ophthalmic Microsurgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13971)
*Niran Nataraj,Maina Sogabe,Masamichi Yamashita,Norihiko Ito,Kenji Kawashima*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TransGHOST：面向眼科/显微手术器械遮挡的因果、参考引导非生成式修复流水线，仅在可见区域拟合几何变换并重采样历史帧，逐像素给出置信度、溯源与弃权标记，安全模式可实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手术高倍视野中器械遮挡同时影响外科医生与机器人感知模块；生成式修复以合成内容填补缺失区域，无法保证视觉溯源，且多数视频修复器依赖未来帧而无法在线运行。

Method: 因果参考引导重建：所有恢复像素均由已记录帧经几何变换重采样得到，变换只用可见区域拟合；提供逐像素置信度与溯源信息，对不支持像素显式弃权；含区域感知的参考帧选择（替代最近帧策略）。

Result: 犬白内障手术新临床语料（像素级器械与解剖标注）上，安全模式 masked PSNR 31.89 dB、masked SSIM 0.915，中位延迟 51.7 ms、95分位 85.5 ms；预声明留出队列 29.83 dB；匹配掩码评估较 LaMa 高 3.45 dB；区域感知参考选择较最近帧提升 3.60 dB（参考帧本身可被遮挡时）。

Conclusion: 以溯源安全的非生成式修复替代合成填补，可在低延迟约束下完成短时重复遮挡下的在线重建，为手术机器人视觉提供可信输入；但评测限于兽医学细分领域，缺乏机器人任务级下游验证。

Abstract: Intraoperative scene perception in ophthalmic microsurgery is limited by instrument occlusion: when a tool enters the high-magnification field, it conceals tissue from both the surgeon and the perception components of robot-assisted systems that process the video. Generative inpainting can remove the occluder but replaces the missing image content with synthesis, which is unsuitable when visual provenance matters. Many video inpainters also depend on future frames and cannot operate online. We present TransGHOST, a causal reference-guided reconstruction pipeline whose non-generative safe mode operates in real time. Every restored value is obtained by resampling a recorded frame using geometric transforms fitted only to visible regions. The system provides per-pixel confidence and provenance and marks unsupported pixels as abstentions. We evaluate TransGHOST on a new veterinary clinical corpus of canine cataract surgery with pixel-level instrument and anatomy labels. Only the occluded region is scored, and each scoring target is verified to differ from the reference supplied to the system. Safe mode reaches 31.89 dB masked PSNR and 0.915 masked SSIM at 51.7 ms median and 85.5 ms 95th-percentile latency. On a pre-declared held-out cohort using instrument cutouts excluded from segmentation training, it reaches 29.83 dB masked PSNR, while matched-mask evaluation on validation shows a 3.45 dB advantage over LaMa. Region-aware reference selection improves masked PSNR by 3.60 dB over recency when candidate references can themselves be occluded. Reference-age analysis further defines the operating interval for the brief, repeated occlusions targeted by TransGHOST.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [A probabilistic model of visual segmentation explains early visual cortical dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13316)
*Tridib K. Biswas,Ruben Coen-Cagli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出一个生物合理的皮层计算模型，将特征推断与分割（特征如何组织为有意义部分）的推断耦合，通过递归与反馈迭代产生神经表征动力学，并对自然图像的诱发响应做出理论精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉皮层神经动力学（尤其在静态刺激下）的功能作用尚不清楚；现有预测早期视觉皮层放电的模型多假设神经元表征潜在图像特征的后验推断，却忽略了推断过程本身的动态性。作者希望检验"图像中的推断动力学"能否解释视觉皮层动力学。

Method: 构建生物合理的皮层计算模型，假设特征推断与分割推断相互耦合；对给定输入图像，模型在特征与分割之间通过递归与反馈迭代，从而在特征表征中诱导动力学，并给出理论解析和精确预测。

Result: 模型预测自然图像诱发响应的增益与变异性，复现增益动力学、刺激呈现期间变异性衰减等经典单神经元实验发现；并预测这些动力学围绕推断的分割结构组织、比以往认为的更异质，反映自然场景推断的复杂性；解析形式提供清晰解释。

Conclusion: 推断动力学可解释视觉皮层动力学；该规范化模型的洞见可推广到其他实验模态的分割问题，并有助于理解迭代计算在人工网络中的推断代价。

Abstract: Neural dynamics are a central feature of biological neural systems. In the visual cortex, the functional role of neural dynamics is poorly understood, particularly for static stimuli. The statistics of still natural images have long been used by models that successfully predict firing in early visual cortex; those models assume that neurons represent inferences about latent image features, but often ignore the dynamics of said inference. Here, we test if inferential dynamics in images explain visual-cortical dynamics. To do so, we present a biologically plausible model of cortical computation which assumes that the inference of features is coupled with inference about how those features are organized into meaningful parts, a function also known as segmentation. Given an input image, our model iterates between inferring the features and the segments, through recurrence and feedback, thus inducing dynamics in the neural representation of features. For these neural dynamics, we derive theoretical and exact predictions. Our model predicts both gain and variability in evoked responses to natural images, and we show that it captures classical single-neuron experimental findings such as gain dynamics and variability decay during stimulus presentation. The model also predicts that those dynamics are organized around inferred segment structure and more heterogeneous than previously thought, reflecting the complexity of inference for natural scenes. Finally, our analytical formulation provides a clear interpretation of our results. Insights from our normative model could generalize to segmentation problems in other experimental modalities, and help address the cost of inference in artificial networks with iterative computation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [EarthRecord: From Pixels to Land Change with Multimodal Agents over Four Decades of Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12934)
*Haoran Zhang,Huizhen Sun,Hanbin Wen,Jie Leng,Jiaxin Zhu,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EarthRecord 基准：基于 56 个站点四十年原始 Landsat/MODIS/VIIRS/Sentinel-2 档案（云掩膜与跨传感器协调由智能体自行完成），238 个土地变化问题覆盖 10 类任务，并设计"闭卷"与"仪器配对"两种对照实验，评估 12 个多模态智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体已能用工具做复杂数据分析，但其将数十年卫星影像转化为可靠土地变化分析的能力尚未被度量；现有基准或用精选影像提问，或直接给出预处理输入，绕开了真实分析起点——原始多传感器档案。

Method: 构建含四十年、多传感器原始影像的 56 站点数据集，不提供云掩膜和跨传感器协调，交由智能体处理；设计 238 个跨 10 类分析任务的变化问答，以全球产品留出参考、多方法一致性、目视解译三种方式评分；引入两个控制实验（移除数据的闭卷、只改动单一卫星数据而地面不变的仪器配对）诊断答案来源。

Result: 十二个多模态智能体中，领先者能端到端完成多数分析，但在需要从影像做映射时表现下降；记录使答案从先验知识转向像素，却未真正对齐地面真实变化——单颗卫星数据被篡改时，约一半正确答案跟随篡改。

Conclusion: EarthRecord 提供了可诊断智能体"从像素到土地变化"能力的严谨平台，揭示当前智能体仍依赖先验与表面统计而非真实地面证据，为后续改进指明方向。

Abstract: While multimodal agents now carry out complex data analysis with tools, their ability to turn decades of satellite imagery into reliable land-change analysis, crucial for Earth observation science, remains unmeasured. Existing benchmarks fall short: they pose questions on curated images or give agents preprocessed inputs, leaving out the raw multi-sensor archives where such analysis begins. We introduce EarthRecord, a benchmark built on the raw record of 56 sites: four decades of Landsat, MODIS, VIIRS, and Sentinel-2 data, with cloud masking and cross-sensor harmonization left to the agent. It poses 238 land-change questions across ten analysis tasks, scored against withheld references from global products, agreement among methods, and visual interpretation. Two controls test what answers rest on: the closed book removes the record, and instrument pairs alter one satellite's data while nothing changes on the ground. Evaluations of twelve multimodal agents reveal that leading agents complete most analyses end to end but falter when an answer requires mapping from imagery. Further analysis shows that the record moves answers from prior knowledge to the pixels but not yet to the real change on the ground: when one satellite's data are altered, half of the right answers follow the alteration. EarthRecord thus provides a principled platform for measuring and advancing agents from pixels to land change.

</details>


### [130] [Seeing Is Not Believing: Counterfactual Lesion Erasure Teaches a Small Medical Vision-Language Agent to Report Only What the Image Shows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13006)
*Bhaskar Gurram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出证据敏感性差距（ESG）指标，通过擦除/对照编辑的反事实视图量化医学VLM是否"因问题命名而报告病灶"；MedScope-4B在内部507例达ESG 0.758，但外部中心与罕见病变（气胸48例全漏）表现差，且不提升感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型常因提问中已包含病名而"附和式"报告发现（sycophancy/提示依赖），而标准基准无法暴露这一问题；同时缺少无需人工标注即可度量"模型是否真正依据图像证据作答"的手段。

Method: 从公开分割数据集构造三类反事实视图（inpainting擦除病灶、异位同形对照编辑、未编辑无病灶图），定义ESG=擦除后"不存在/拒绝"回答率−对照编辑后该率；以Qwen3.5-4B+LoRA+图像工具训练MedScope-4B（标签派生轨迹、预注册选型规则、单次测试），对9个前沿与6个开源VLM比较，并用四项预注册检查探究机制（去掉反事实视图、MedGemma 1.5 4B复制、两种未见inpainting工具、与基于移除的DPO及配对视图一致性目标的对照）。

Result: 内部507例、5种影像：MedScope-4B ESG 0.758、误称已擦除病灶率9.6%，前沿模型为0.27–0.47与41–62%（p<0.001），真病灶准确率无显著损失；无对照视图时ESG降至0.279±0.107（3种子）；在MedGemma 1.5 4B及两个未见inpainting工具上复现。四个外部中心优势保持但前沿模型检出更多真病灶；全新外部测试中48张气胸片检出0例，净效用低于gemini-4-argon（−0.36 [−0.50,−0.23]）。未证明"匹配对照编辑"关键；三种子下相对移除式DPO未建立外部ESG优势；配对视图一致性目标未胜等算力对照，但该续训本身在各随机种子下都降低了未见工具上的误称率（一个种子以灵敏度为代价）。

Conclusion: 用擦除/编辑图像训练可赋予小模型"证据门控"倾向，这是提示工程无法让前沿模型获得的行为；但该方法不改善感知能力，且在罕见关键病变上需专门覆盖，泛化到真实外部分布仍受限。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often report a finding because the question names it; standard benchmarks miss this. From public segmentation datasets we build counterfactual views without manual annotation: the lesion erased by inpainting, a same-shape control edit elsewhere, and unedited lesion-free images. The evidence sensitivity gap (ESG) is the rate of absent-or-refer answers after erasure minus the rate after the control edit; it is zero for a model that ignores the image or reacts to both edits alike. MedScope-4B (Qwen3.5-4B, LoRA, image tools) was trained on label-derived trajectories, selected by a pre-registered rule and tested once against nine frontier and six open VLMs; four pre-registered checks then probed the mechanism. On 507 internal cases across five imaging types, MedScope-4B reached ESG 0.758 and claimed an erased lesion in 9.6% of cases, against 0.27-0.47 and 41-62% for the frontier models (all p<0.001), without significant loss of accuracy on real lesions. Without counterfactual views ESG fell to 0.279+-0.107 (three seeds); the effect replicated on MedGemma 1.5 4B and two unseen inpainting tools. At four external centres the advantage held but frontier models detected more real lesions; on a fresh external test MedScope-4B detected 0 of 48 pneumothorax films, with net utility below gemini-4-argon's (-0.36 [-0.50, -0.23]). Matching the control edit was not shown to matter; over three seeds no fresh-external ESG advantage over removal-based DPO was established; a paired-view consistency objective did not beat a matched-compute control, while that continuation alone cut erased-lesion claims on unseen tools at every seed, at a cost in sensitivity at one. Training on erased and edited images gives a small model an evidence-gated disposition that prompting does not give frontier models; it does not improve perception, and rare critical findings need coverage.

</details>


### [131] [Towards Reliable VLM Judges: State-Conditional Invariance and Presentation-Aware Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13596)
*Qinan Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Qihang Jin,Tianyuan Wang,Hongbing Luo,Pengfei Li,Wenjun Lv,Yu Kang,Jian Di*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出State-Conditional Invariance (SCI)可靠性准则，并构建GridWM-Judge基准（基于确定性MiniGrid轨迹，含Full/证据移除/反事实变体及呈现与表征探针），系统诊断视觉语言模型作为多模态与具身任务自动评判者的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs越来越多被用作多模态与具身任务的自动评判器，但单一聚合准确率或一致性指标无法揭示评判器是否因“正确的原因”改变判定：既可能在语义保持的呈现变化下不稳定，也可能对改变任务结果的因果干预不敏感。需要区分“表面稳定”与“因果敏感”的诊断原则与基准。

Method: 提出SCI准则：要求评判器在语义保持的呈现变化下保持稳定，同时对改变任务结果的因果干预作出响应。将其实例化为GridWM-Judge：由确定性MiniGrid轨迹生成的仿真接地诊断基准，对齐构造Full、证据移除与反事实变体，并加入呈现与表征探针。五项诊断任务分别检验局部感知、动作条件转移、呈现稳定性、表征可交换性与Full-CF配对因果判别；并做感知控制分析以剥离视觉基础能力的影响。

Result: 诊断评测揭示异质失效模式：对证据呈现方式与编码敏感、在成功轨迹上激活度偏低、配对反事实判别能力弱。感知控制分析表明视觉接地与目标恢复是显著且模型依赖的瓶颈。单一分数无法刻画评判器可靠性。

Conclusion: 作为VLM自动评判器的可靠性应按正确性、呈现稳定性与因果敏感性三方面联合报告，而非依赖单一聚合分数；SCI与GridWM-Judge为这一诊断提供了可分析、可控反事实的基准框架。结论主要建立在仿真网格环境与MiniGrid任务上，向真实机器人/开放多模态的迁移尚待验证。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for multimodal and embodied tasks, yet aggregate accuracy or consistency alone does not reveal whether a judge changes its verdict for the right reason. We propose State-Conditional Invariance (SCI), a reliability principle requiring a judge to remain stable under semantics-preserving changes in presentation while responding to causal interventions that change the task outcome. We instantiate SCI in GridWM-Judge, a simulator-grounded diagnostic benchmark built from deterministic MiniGrid trajectories with aligned Full, evidence-removal, and counterfactual variants, plus presentation and representation probes. Across five diagnostic tasks, GridWM-Judge tests local perception, action-conditioned transitions, presentation stability, representation exchangeability, and Full-CF causal discrimination. Our diagnostic evaluations reveal heterogeneous failure modes, including sensitivity to evidence presentation and encoding, low activation on successful trajectories, and weak paired counterfactual discrimination. Perception-controlled analyses further indicate that visual grounding and objective recovery are substantial, model-dependent bottlenecks. These results motivate jointly reporting correctness, presentation stability, and causal sensitivity instead of relying on a single score.

</details>


### [132] [A computational framework for understanding embodied plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13669)
*Aalap D. Shah,Basil Saeed,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Tobias Gerstenberg,Hyowon Gweon,Ilker Yildirim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenEA 提出一种多层状态空间生成模型，耦合刚体生物力学、物体交互与仿真直觉物理，对多步长时程具身计划进行目标-计划联合概率推理，在物体操作与导航任务上优于TAMP、启发式和SOTA视觉语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人的行为推断其计划与目标，是人类社会认知与协作型具身AI的基础能力；但如何在自然、具身情境中形式化该能力，长期是难题。现有启发式、标准任务-运动规划或大视觉语言模型难以同时刻画长时程多步计划中的物理可行性与意图结构。

Method: 将机器人学中的任务-运动规划（TAMP）框架与基于仿真的直觉物理结合，构建耦合"关节体粗粒度生物力学"与"邻近物体交互"的多层级状态空间；再把该状态空间嵌入生成模型，得到目标与具身计划的联合概率分布，并通过对该联合分布的简单变换（如条件化、边缘化）实现多种推理模式。

Result: 在物体操作与导航这两个性质不同的域、多类任务与多种行为指标上，GenEA能复现人类对具身计划的推理模式，并稳定优于强基线（合理启发式、标准TAMP、以及一个SOTA视觉语言模型）。

Conclusion: 该工作为人类推理与社会认知的架构提供新视角，主张追求与人无缝交互的具身智能体应采用GenEA式的生成式动作规划模型；但实验主要为与人类行为数据的拟合对比，尚缺真实机器人闭环部署证据。

Abstract: Reasoning about people's plans and goals based on their actions is fundamental to human social interaction and essential for cooperative, embodied artificial intelligence. Formalizing this capacity in naturalistic, embodied settings has been a longstanding challenge. Here, we present GenEA, a novel agent representation capable of reasoning over multi-step, long-horizon embodied plans. At the heart of GenEA lies a multilevel state-space that couples the coarse biomechanics of an articulated body and its interactions with nearby objects by extending the task-and-motion planning framework from robotics with simulation-based intuitive physics. By embedding this state-space in a generative model, GenEA yields a joint probability distribution over goals and embodied plans. Across several experiments spanning the qualitatively distinct domains of object manipulation and navigation, featuring diverse tasks and multiple behavioral measures, we show that GenEA, through simple transformations of this joint distribution, recapitulates how humans reason about embodied plans. Crucially, GenEA consistently outperforms strong alternative models, including plausible heuristics, standard task-and-motion planning, and a state-of-the-art vision-language model. GenEA thereby offers a new perspective on the architecture of human reasoning and social cognition, suggesting that embodied artificial intelligence systems seeking to seamlessly interact with humans should adopt GenEA-like generative models of action planning.

</details>


### [133] [Taxonomy-Agnostic Neurosymbolic Grounding of Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13850)
*Bastien Dussard,Aur{é}lie Clodic,Guillaume Sarthou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: URDF2Ontology：用LLM+神经符号护栏把URDF转成可推理的OWL本体，含形态感知双轨（自底向上Tree / 自顶向下Chain）与迭代图剪枝防上下文饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URDF只含低层运动学，缺乏任务分配符号推理所需的语义与能力grounding；手工编写领域本体开销大。

Method: 自动化、taxonomy-agnostic神经符号框架：LLM做定向语义推理，迭代序列化图剪枝控制上下文窗口；形态感知双轨架构处理分支装配与串联肢链；神经符号护栏约束抽取功能子图并对齐用户给定分类体系。

Result: 在多机异构机器人队上验证：schema对齐良好、结构抽取有效、显著降低人工本体编写开销。

Conclusion: 可作为机器人能力grounding与任务分配的中间件，但缺少真实部署与任务级量化证据，偏工具性贡献。

Abstract: While symbolic reasoning for task allocation requires grounding a robot's physical capabilities, standard Unified Robot Description Format (URDF) files provide only low-level kinematics without the formal semantic context needed for reasoning. We present URDF2Ontology, an automated, taxonomy-agnostic neurosymbolic framework that transforms raw URDF files into populated, grounded OWL ontologies. It leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) for targeted semantic reasoning, coupled with an iterative sequential graph-purging mechanism to prevent context window saturation. To process complex kinematic structures, it employs a morphology-aware dual-track architecture featuring a bottom-up Tree Track for branching assemblies and a top-down Chain Track for serial limbs. Guided by neurosymbolic guardrails, the framework extracts functional sub-graphs and asserts populated ontologies aligned with arbitrary user-provided taxonomies. Empirical evaluation across a diverse fleet of heterogeneous robots demonstrates strong schema alignment, valid structural extraction, and a significant reduction in manual domain authoring overhead.

</details>


### [134] [Personalized Affective Perception via Continual Learning in Human-Agent Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14247)
*Mehmet Onur Keskin,Berk Buzcu,Nikhil Churamani,Reyhan Aydogan,Hatice Gunes,Catholijn Jonker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将持续学习+想象的表情识别模型CLIFER嵌入人形机器人谈判策略，用差分反馈把效价-唤醒变化转成目标效用调整，66人实验显示个体效用与让步率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用面部情感模型跨个体、跨情境泛化差，导致自利智能体无法在实时人机谈判中准确解读非言语信号并相应调整行为，形成泛化鸿沟。

Method: CLIFER：以持续学习+想象机制个性化效价-唤醒回归；差分反馈将效价-唤醒方向与强度变化映射为智能体目标效用的调整量；异步架构在真实交互中后台增量更新模型。采用66人平衡化被试内设计，与FaceChannel基线对比。

Result: 持续学习条件下个体效用显著提高、被试让步率显著更高；平均唤醒估计更高、输出动态范围更宽；被试效用与Nash距离无显著差异。

Conclusion: 将持续性情感个性化与差分反馈耦合，可有效提升实时人机谈判性能；但缺乏交叉情境泛化验证与公平性（Nash距离不变）的深层分析。

Abstract: Self-interested intelligent agents that negotiate with humans should interpret nonverbal expressions and adapt their behavior accordingly. However, generic facial-affect models degrade across individuals and contexts, creating a generalization gap. We introduce an affect-adaptive negotiation strategy that incorporates Continual Learning with Imagination for Facial Expression Recognition (CLIFER) to personalize valence-arousal perception in real time. Differential feedback converts changes in valence-arousal direction and intensity into adjustments of the agent's target utility. An asynchronous architecture updates CLIFER in the background during live interaction. In a counterbalanced within-subject study in which 66 participants negotiated with a humanoid robot, the CL-enabled agent achieved significantly higher individual utility and elicited higher participant concession rates than the FaceChannel baseline. The CL condition yielded significantly higher mean arousal estimates, alongside a broader output range. Participant utility and Nash distance did not change significantly between conditions. These outcomes support the practical value of coupling continual affective personalization with differential feedback for real-time negotiation.

</details>


### [135] [Multimodal Abstention That Preserves Acquired Correct Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14311)
*Jinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReFrain：在对已适配的多模态模型加入弃答能力时，以"新丢失正确答案数"为预算约束、最大化不可答问题的正确弃答率；方法为对首个token logits 的线性探针 + 留出图像簇上的阈值校准 + 确定性否决（用"none of the above"替换）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给任务适配后的多模态模型加弃答会误伤已获得的回答能力（丢失正确答案），现有弃答方法普遍以回归代价换取弃答率，缺乏显式的"零/低代价"约束与度量。

Method: 将弃答修复形式化为带预算约束的最大化问题；线性探针作用于首 token logits 判别可否答；在留出图像簇上校准阈值以控制损失；再用确定性 veto 机制把被拒输出替换为屏幕上可见的"none of the above"选项，同时不改变其他任何回答。对比实验统一从同一 answering-SFT 检查点出发，分别追踪丢失正确数、修复错误数、保留的 SFT 增益。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-3B：零预算下保留全部 57 项 SFT 增益、无损失，正确弃答率 +7.30pp；0.5% 校准预算下达 74.05% 正确弃答、8 损失、保留 55 增益；CLIP-UP EmbLoRA 为 64.65% / 62 损失 / 41 修复 / 40 增益；同一探针默认阈值损失 298 个正确答案。冻结模型与阈值后在 228 个独立问题簇上确认：66.05% 正确弃答、零损失，较 EmbLoRA 高 32.72pp；普通拒答 SFT 弃答率更高但回归代价更大。

Conclusion: 弃答可作为"低回归代价"的轻量后处理问题被有效解决；ReFrain 在几乎零损失前提下显著提升弃答率，并强调应将"丢失正确答案"作为弃答方法的一等评测指标。

Abstract: Adding abstention to a task-adapted multimodal model can discard correct answers, including capabilities acquired during adaptation. We formulate abstention repair as maximizing correct abstention on unanswerable questions under a budget on newly lost correct answers. We propose ReFrain (Regression-Free Abstention), which combines a linear probe on first-token logits, threshold calibration on held-out image clusters, and a deterministic veto that replaces rejected outputs with the displayed "none of the above" option while preserving all other responses. "Regression-free" denotes zero loss on the calibration set at the zero-budget target. All abstention methods in our main comparison start from the same answering-SFT checkpoint; we separately track lost correct answers, repaired errors, and retained SFT gains. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B, ReFrain at zero budget retains all 57 SFT-gained answers with no losses and improves correct abstention by 7.30 percentage points. At a 0.5% calibration budget, it reaches 74.05% correct abstention with 8 losses and retains 55 gains; CLIP-UP EmbLoRA reaches 64.65% with 62 losses, 41 repairs, and 40 retained gains. The same probe at its default threshold loses 298 correct answers. With models and thresholds frozen, confirmation on 228 independent question clusters yields 66.05% correct abstention and no losses at the 0.5% point, exceeding EmbLoRA by 32.72 percentage points. Ordinary refusal SFT achieves higher correct abstention at greater regression cost. Code and reproduction materials: this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [TypedInk: Knowing When Not To Draw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13027)
*Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TypedInk 从可能不完整的科研记录（提纲/段落/表格/单句意图）推断图类型（示意图/统计图/图版），并仅当对应"证据契约"可满足时才分派渲染器，否则拒绝生成，避免缺表时凭空编造数值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学图表生成器假设输入完整（完整方法段或完整表格），但真实研究记录常不完整；在缺表时用图像模型"画出测量值"属于幻觉结果，需要让生成器具备"证据不足即拒答"的能力。

Method: 构建 TypedInk：先由图类型路由器在 {diagram, plot, plate} 中判定类型，再将类型映射到可兑现的证据契约并分派给相应渲染器（代码渲染/示意图渲染/图版），契约不可满足时拒绝分派；在 TypedFigBench 的 6 个金标准图表（5 种视图，n=30）上与无门控的 always-draw 编码器对比，并做类型路由器的字段消融（16 图 × 5 视图，n=80）。

Result: 观测到表格时 TypedInk 代码路径覆盖全部金标准表格 token（12/12），对 12 个隐藏表格视图拒绝而非编造数值，6 个仅意图视图标记为示意性，无执行失败；always-draw 基线生成 30/30 图（含 18 个无表格者）却在无表格图上恢复 0 个金标准数值，两个视觉语言评分器仍判定其 17/36 次"可提交"，而 TypedInk 的拒绝为 0/36。消融显示：仅给提纲时类型准确率 0.31 且所有金标准图表触发完整性违规，聊天模型规划器将总体准确率提升至 0.85 但仍有 25% 提纲完整性失败；图为标签召回 0.47（完整记录）/0.19（仅提纲）；金标准图版在分派时被正确识别（oracle 违规 0.00）并以静帧渲染。

Conclusion: 证据契约驱动的"类型路由 + 可拒绝分派"是抑制科学图表数值幻觉的有效机制，且比单纯增强规划器更关键；类型推断在稀疏输入下仍是主要瓶颈，评估依赖模型评分器、缺乏人工复现。

Abstract: Can a scientific figure generator know when it does not have enough evidence to draw? Complete-source illustrators map a finished methodology section to a schematic, or a table to plotting code, and are never asked this question because their input is assumed complete.
