The research record is rarely that complete: an outline, a draft paragraph, a table, or a one-sentence intent, in any combination, and painting measured numbers with an image model when the table is missing is a hallucinated result. TypedInk infers a figure type from {diagram, plot, plate} from a possibly incomplete source and dispatches to the renderer that can honor that type's evidence contract, refusing dispatch when the contract cannot be met. On the six gold plots in TypedFigBench (5 views, n=30), TypedInk's code path inks every gold table token when a table is observed (12/12), refuses the 12 hidden-table views instead of inventing numbers, and marks 6 intent-only views illustrative, with no execution failures. An always-draw coder with no such gate inks 30/30 charts, including all 18 with no table, and recovers zero gold numbers on any of them; two vision-language raters (not humans; human replication is future work) still judge those always-draw charts submittable 17/36 times, against TypedInk's refusal 0/36 times. A diagnostic field ablation of the type router (16 illustrations, 5 views each, n=80) locates where this failure would originate if unchecked: an outline alone yields type accuracy 0.31 and sends every gold plot into an integrity violation, and a chat-model planner narrows but does not close the gap (overall accuracy 0.85, still integrity-failing on 25% of outlines). Diagram-label recall is 0.47 on the full record and 0.19 on an outline. Gold plates are typed at dispatch (oracle integrity-fail 0.00) and rendered as stills.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Maze Runner: Autonomous Maze Navigation with Line-Based Localization and Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12949)
*Alvaro J. Gaona,Lucas Gómez-Velayos,Peter Pasuy-Quevedo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Maze Runner：基于ROS 2/C++的迷宫自主导航全栈系统，RANSAC+TLS直线特征驱动EKF定位与EKF-SLAM，A*/Hybrid A*规划，NMPC+CBF安全控制，仿真中135 m行程定位RMSE 1.0 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迷宫场景同时要求稀疏重复几何下的定位、狭窄走廊中的规划，以及估计或地图出错时仍保持安全控制，现有工作少有端到端统一处理。

Method: Gazebo中Husarion ROSbot XL + 2D LiDAR仿真；每帧用RANSAC与总体最小二乘提取带不确定度的墙体直线；同一前端分别接入基于网格地图的EKF与在线建图的EKF-SLAM；在膨胀占据栅格上以A*+视线捷径或前向单步Hybrid A*在线规划；非线性MPC跟踪轨迹，叠加世界系LiDAR点上的离散时间CBF约束；算法置于无ROS依赖、单元测试的核心库。

Result: 135 m迷宫路线中已知地图定位RMSE 1.0 cm、SLAM模式24 cm；规划耗时6 ms，NMPC平均11.7 ms且不超80 ms预算。

Conclusion: 提供可复现、模块化、实时性满足的经典几何SLAM+MPC/CBF迷宫导航基线栈；但仅在仿真验证，安全与定位鲁棒性未经真实机器人检验。

Abstract: Maze navigation requires localization from sparse, repetitive geometry, planning through narrow corridors, and control that stays safe when the estimate or the map is wrong. We present Maze Runner, an open autonomy stack in C++ and ROS 2 that addresses these needs end to end on a simulated Husarion ROSbot XL with a 2D LiDAR in Gazebo. Perception extracts wall lines from each scan with RANSAC and total least squares, with per-line uncertainty. The same line front end feeds two estimators: an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) that localizes against a line map built from the maze mesh, and an EKF SLAM variant that builds the line map online. Planning runs online on an inflated occupancy grid with A* plus line-of-sight shortcutting, or with a forward-only Hybrid A*. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) tracks the plan while discrete-time Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) on world-frame LiDAR points keep the robot clear of walls and unmapped obstacles. The algorithms live in a ROS-free, unit-tested core library, and the whole stack builds and runs from a single reproducible environment. In simulation, the robot drives a 135 m route through the maze with 1.0 cm localization RMSE against the known map and 24 cm in SLAM mode, the planner returns a path in 6 ms, and the NMPC solves in 11.7 ms on average, never exceeding its 80 ms budget.

</details>


### [138] [CAFE: Closed-Loop Autonomous Task Formulation and Execution without Instance-Specific Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12983)
*Mingjia Chen,Xuewen Zhao,Philip Dames,Qi Mao,Fei Xie,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAFE框架用规则触发的Trigger-LLM在预定义清洁/安全/收纳类别下，从在线记忆（物体、功能区、执行状态）中发现任务，并通过Draft-Complete-Audit三阶段LLM流程生成目标、补全执行细节、校验依赖，结果回写记忆以纠错和后续发现；在AI2-THOR 12个场景、3种骨干、3档复杂度上取得更高GCR，配LimoCobot真机案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景驱动的机器人自主性需要同时解决"从观测中选任务"和"执行任务"两个问题，而现有工作多依赖实例级目标或预设动作序列，缺乏在给定抽象类别下自主形成任务并闭环执行的能力。

Method: 维护物体、功能区和执行状态的在线记忆；规则引导的Trigger-LLM依据累积证据判断何时触发任务形成；Draft/Complete/Audit三个角色分别提出目标、解析执行细节、检查依赖与约束；执行结果反馈更新记忆用于纠正与后续发现。在AI2-THOR 12个场景上以coverage、constraint、execution指标对比3个骨干和复杂度分层，并有消融控制实验与LimoCobot真机轨迹。

Result: Tri-LLM配置在三个骨干与各复杂度层级上GCR分数均优于所评估基线；消融覆盖补全、审计、功能区、反馈、触发、prompt示例各模块；LimoCobot轨迹展示了收纳、清理及抓取失败后的恢复。

Conclusion: 共享场景状态可有效连接任务发现与执行，形成"发现—执行—结果驱动修订"的闭环；但证据以仿真基准为主，真机仅为定性案例，尚缺部署级定量验证。

Abstract: Scene-driven robot autonomy requires selecting tasks from observations as well as executing them. We study scene-driven task formulation and execution under predefined cleaning, safety, and storage/organization categories, without instance-specific goals or action sequences. CAFE maintains online memory of objects, functional zones, and execution states. A rule-guided Trigger-LLM admits formulation from accumulated evidence; Draft, Complete, and Audit propose goals, resolve execution details, and check dependencies. Outcomes update memory for correction and subsequent discovery. Evaluation in 12 AI2-THOR scenes compares three backbones and task-complexity tiers using coverage, constraint, and execution metrics. Tri-LLM records higher GCR scores than the evaluated baselines across three backbones and task-complexity tiers. Controls examine completion, auditing, zones, feedback, triggering, and prompt examples. LimoCobot traces illustrate storage, cleanup, and recovery from a failed grasp. The study examines how shared scene state connects task discovery to execution and outcome-driven revision.

</details>


### [139] [RoboACE: Adversarial Co-Evolution for Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13034)
*Delin Zhao,Zhengrong Yue,Junlin He,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboACE提出课程与执行脚手架协同演化框架：在冻结基础模型外围，用能力证据驱动的课程生成有信息量的挑战，执行端给出可复用的策略修订，交替更新并通过固定快照比较执行修订，LIBERO-PRO达74.0%（+15.0pp）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体可从执行反馈中改进技能、规划与恢复策略，但练习来源固定，无法持续发现新弱点；有效练习依赖智能体当前能力，因此选择练习的机制本身也必须可演化。

Method: 在冻结VLA/基础模型上构建双重脚手架：(1) 课程脚手架依据累积的能力证据提出有信息量的挑战；(2) 执行脚手架输出可复用的修订以扩展执行能力。两者交替更新（一侧固定时另一侧变化），被接纳的挑战形成固定快照，用于公平比较不同执行修订。以紧凑LIBERO练习条件集为起点。

Result: 在四个LIBERO系列评测面板上，冻结部署优于所评估的控制器与智能体系统；LIBERO-PRO成功率74.0%，较初始部署提升15.0个百分点，较其VLA控制器直接执行提升13.5个百分点；案例研究展示了证据引导的练习选择、抽屉技能接地与控制器介入的变化。

Conclusion: 将课程选择与执行改进联合共演化、并以快照机制保证可比较性，是提升冻结基础模型智能体能力的有效范式；但证据完全来自LIBERO仿真基准，缺乏真机部署验证，实践来源仍受初始练习条件集约束。

Abstract: Robot agents can improve reusable skills, planning procedures, and recovery strategies from execution feedback, yet a fixed practice source leaves the search for new weaknesses unchanged. Useful practice depends on the agent's current capabilities, so the procedure that chooses practice must also be revisable. We present RoboACE (Robot Adversarial Co-Evolution), a framework that jointly evolves curriculum and execution harnesses around frozen foundation models. The curriculum harness uses accumulated capability evidence to propose informative challenges, while the execution harness responds with reusable revisions that expand execution capabilities. Alternating updates hold one side fixed as the other changes, and admitted challenges form fixed snapshots for comparing execution revisions. Starting from a compact set of LIBERO practice conditions, the resulting frozen deployment outperforms the evaluated controllers and agent systems across four LIBERO-family panels. On LIBERO-PRO, it reaches 74.0% success, exceeding the initial deployment by 15.0 percentage points and direct execution by its VLA controller by 13.5 points. Case studies document changes to evidence-guided practice selection, drawer-skill grounding, and controller intervention.

</details>


### [140] [Robo-MLT: Dual-System Robotic Manipulation for Long-Horizon Medical Laboratory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13039)
*Zequan Zeng,Kai Wang,Delvin Ce Zhang,Huaping Liu,Zhipeng Yuan,Junqin Yang,YunLong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robo-MLT：双系统框架，用视觉-语言规划器生成原子技能序列、多帧执行监视器判断技能完成/不可行并推进或重规划、技能条件VLA策略生成轨迹，异步运行时协调二者；在血气分析与试管分拣上成功率82%/70%，比π0.5高30/22个百分点，并支持样本被移除后重规划继续执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗实验室长程自动化中，单步动作正确但整体流程可能失败：混淆视觉相似的阶段，或人工干预后停滞。需要显式的流程级监控来保证按协议顺序完成并具备可恢复性。

Method: 双系统：①视觉-语言规划器依据协议与初始场景观测合成有序原子技能序列；②多帧执行监视器检测当前技能完成或不可行，进而推进流程或触发重规划；③技能条件的视觉-语言-动作策略生成操作轨迹；④异步运行时协调策略与监视器。真机评测对比4个策略基线与1个VLM监视器基线，每方法50个标称配置+20个移除样本配置。

Result: 血气分析成功率82%、试管分拣70%，较π0.5分别提升30与22个百分点；操作员移除正在处理的样本后系统能修订剩余计划并继续处理其他样本。

Conclusion: 在所评估条件下，显式流程监控可提升长程实验室操作任务的可靠性与可恢复性；方法核心为监控+重规划，而非新的操作策略或数据增强。

Abstract: Reliable medical laboratory automation requires robots to complete entire workflows in protocol order while handling changes during execution. A robot may execute each motion correctly yet fail the workflow by confusing visually similar stages or stalling after human intervention. We present Robo-MLT, a dual-system framework that couples explicit workflow monitoring with learned action generation. A vision--language planner composes an ordered atomic-skill sequence from the protocol and the initial workspace observation. A multiframe execution monitor detects when the active skill has completed or become infeasible, then advances the workflow or triggers replanning. A skill-conditioned vision--language--action policy generates the manipulation trajectories, and an asynchronous runtime coordinates it with the monitor. We evaluate Robo-MLT against four policy baselines and a VLM-based monitor baseline on blood gas analysis and blood sample tube sorting, with 50 nominal configurations per method and 20 sample-removal configurations. Robo-MLT achieves task success rates of 82\% and 70\%, exceeding $\pi_{0.5}$ by 30 and 22 percentage points, respectively. After an operator removes the sample being handled, the system revises the remaining plan and continues processing the other samples. These results indicate that, under the evaluated conditions, explicit workflow monitoring makes long-horizon laboratory manipulation more reliable and recoverable.

</details>


### [141] [ASPIRE: An Agentic System-Identification Platform for Iterative Reasoning and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13114)
*Erik Derner,Jiří Kubalík,Robert Babuška*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASPIRE：由大语言模型智能体自主选择并执行非线性系统辨识方法，从实测数据构建可解释的闭式非线性静态/动态模型，并在三个真实系统上全部产出有效模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统辨识依赖专家决策（选择方法、融入物理先验、模型验证）；希望借助 LLM 智能体自动化这一流程，降低对领域专家的依赖并保证物理约束可验证。

Method: 构建 LLM agent 框架：输入测量数据，可选补充系统描述、模型用途、形式化或自然语言的物理要求；智能体选择并执行辨识步骤，生成闭式非线性静态/动态模型，附参数、估计记录、各项解释及在智能体不可见的测试数据上的误差；形式化物理要求由外部独立校验；支持自主或交互式（提问、追加分析轮次）运行。

Result: 在移动机器人、含干摩擦的旋转系统和实验室发酵罐三个实测系统上，每系统在有/无描述两种条件下各 5 次，共 30 次运行全部得到有效模型，单次成本 0.96–3.38 美元；作者评估中，30 个推荐模型的 150 项评估条目里 132 项完全满足、18 项部分满足，最常见缺陷（9 个模型）为模型结构选择相关论断不准确或缺乏依据。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体可端到端承担非线性系统辨识的方法选择与执行，产出可解释、经独立物理校验的模型且成本低；但结构选择理由的可追溯性与准确性仍是主要弱点。

Abstract: System identification requires expert decisions on the identification method, incorporation of prior physical knowledge, and model validation. We propose ASPIRE, a framework for nonlinear system identification in which a large language model agent selects and executes the identification method. ASPIRE constructs closed-form, nonlinear static or dynamic models from measured data, optionally supplemented by a system description, the intended use of the model, and physical requirements expressed formally or in natural language. Each model includes its parameters, an estimation record, an interpretation of its terms, and an error evaluated on test data inaccessible to the agent. Formal physical requirements are verified independently. ASPIRE operates autonomously or interactively, allowing the agent to ask questions and the user to request further analyses and identification rounds. We evaluate ASPIRE on three measured systems: a mobile robot, a rotational system with dry friction, and a laboratory fermenter. Each system is tested with and without a description, in five runs per task. All 30 runs delivered valid models, at a cost of 0.96 to 3.38 US dollars per run. An assessment by the authors found that, for the 30 models recommended by the agent, one per run, the interpretation of the model and the documentation of its structure selection and parameter estimation fully satisfied 132 of 150 assessed items and partly satisfied 18. The most frequent discrepancy, found in nine models, concerned inaccurate or unsupported claims about model structure selection.

</details>


### [142] [DOBB: Dynamic Oriented Bounding Boxes for Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance under State Uncertainty and Heterogeneous Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13142)
*Pedro Antonio Alarcon Granadeno,Jane Cleland-Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态有向包围盒（DOBB），将状态估计、不确定性与个体响应特性映射为方向相关安全裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式去中心化避碰需在状态估计不确定性与异构动力学下保持可靠，而现有方法仅靠训练域变化或将不确定性/能力估计暴露给策略，使策略需自行推断这些量如何改变安全间距。

Method: 在线几何表示DOBB：由当前状态估计、不确定性与智能体特定响应特性生成动态方向相关安全裕度；推导刻画DOBB间相对构型与交互严重度的几何特征，并设计保持这些交互物理可解释性的奖励函数。

Result: 四个评估环境中平均总失败率最低为2.6%；在驱动动力学与状态估计不确定性的分布外偏移下仍保持，各测试条件下碰撞类失败≤2.3%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性来自安全裕度的几何抽象，其与训练中遇到的特定不确定性与驱动机制耦合更弱。

Abstract: Learning-based decentralized collision avoidance must remain reliable under state-estimation uncertainty and heterogeneous agent dynamics. Existing methods typically address these factors through training-domain variation or by exposing uncertainty and capability estimates to the policy at runtime. However, both leave the policy to infer how these quantities should alter the physical separation required for safe interaction, which can couple collision-avoidance behavior to relationships learned from the training distribution. We introduce the Dynamic Oriented Bounding Box (DOBB), an online geometric representation that maps the current state estimate, uncertainty, and agent-specific response characteristics into a dynamic, direction-dependent safety margin. We derive geometric features that jointly characterize the relative configuration and severity of interactions between agents' DOBBs, along with a reward function that preserves the physical interpretation of those interactions. Across four evaluation environments, DOBB achieves the lowest mean total failure rate of 2.6%. This performance persists under out-of-distribution shifts in actuation dynamics and state-estimation uncertainty, with collision-based failure remaining at or below 2.3% in every tested condition. We attribute this robustness to the geometric abstraction of the safety margin, which is less coupled to the specific uncertainty and actuation regimes encountered during training.

</details>


### [143] [How Much of Your VLA Is kNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13160)
*Denis Grachev,Damien LaRocque,Omar Moured*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Paper finds that retrieving nearby demos and reusing their action chunks scores 91.7% on LIBERO without training; fine-tuned VLAs mostly learn better retrieval keys, and introduces Local Action Consistency (LAC), an offline metric correlating with retrieval success.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIBERO success exceeds 95%, but it is unclear how much of this reflects true generalization versus memorization/reuse of training demonstrations, so a diagnostic baseline of demonstration retrieval is needed.

Method: Training-free demonstration retrieval with action-chunk reuse as a baseline; analysis of nine public VLAs under fine-tuning to test whether the VLM backbone acts mainly as a retrieval key; proposed Local Action Consistency (LAC) offline metric measuring whether representation-space neighbors share similar future actions, also usable as a training signal for the retrieval key.

Result: 91.7% on LIBERO without policy training; smallest degradation under LIBERO-Plus perturbations among evaluated policies; fine-tuned VLAs' actions stay close to demonstrations; LAC tracks retrieval success.

Conclusion: Current VLA benchmarks are largely saturable by retrieval; LAC offers a cheap diagnostic and guidance signal, suggesting benchmarks and training should better stress genuine generalization.

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models now exceed 95% success on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, yet it remains unclear how much of this performance requires generalization beyond the training demonstrations. In this work, we show that retrieving nearby demonstrations and reusing their action chunks achieves a success rate of 91.7 % on LIBERO without policy training. Under LIBERO-Plus perturbations, retrieval shows the smallest drop of all evaluated policies. Across nine publicly available VLAs, fine-tuning mostly makes the vision-language backbone a better retrieval key, and the actions of the VLAs remain close to the demonstrations. In addition, we introduce Local Action Consistency (LAC), an offline metric that measures whether nearby observations in representation space are also associated with similar future actions. We show that LAC tracks the success rate of retrieval and can guide the training of a retrieval key. Our evaluation and source code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control for Underactuated Upright Physical Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13204)
*Darren Biskup,Jeonghan Yu,Zibo Yu,Sunil K. Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRF-MPC：将实时地面反力(GRF)与压力中心(CoP)测量嵌入人体质心动力学模型，构建滚动时域QP优化辅助力/力矩，在欠驱动4缆RobUST上与8缆全驱动阻抗基线对比，扰动下峰值CoM误差降19.6%，轨迹跟踪误差降24.1%，净机械功少28.7%，自主稳定域扩大45.9%，QP求解<1ms（100Hz重规划）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有康复机器人多用反应式跟踪控制执行预设轨迹，未显式纳入用户瞬时人-地交互信息，导致辅助不透明度高、需要更多执行器才能实现同等或更差的稳定/跟踪表现。

Method: 以人体质心动力学为预测模型，将在线GRF与CoP测量作为模型输入/约束，构造实时二次规划(receding-horizon MPC)同时优化辅助力与力矩；部署于4缆欠驱动站立训练平台RobUST，与8缆全驱动笛卡尔阻抗控制做对照实验（6名健康被试、近300次试验，覆盖脉冲扰动、弓步轨迹、自主稳定极限三类任务）。

Result: 脉冲扰动下峰值平面CoM误差降低19.6%；弓步轨迹跟踪误差降低24.1%；净机械功减少28.7%；归一化自主偏移面积增大45.9%；QP求解时间<1 ms，可支持100 Hz重规划。

Conclusion: 将实时人-地交互量纳入预测控制可获得更强、更"透明"（低阻抗、低能耗）的物理辅助，并以半数控执行器配置超越全驱动阻抗基线，为人机耦合物理辅助控制提供了可实时部署的范式。

Abstract: Many rehabilitation robots execute prescribed motions using reactive tracking controllers that do not explicitly incorporate the user's instantaneous human-ground interaction. This paper presents Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control (GRF-MPC), a framework for upright physical assistance. By integrating live GRF and center of pressure (CoP) measurements directly into a human centroidal dynamics model, GRF-MPC formulates a real-time quadratic program (QP) that optimizes assistive forces and moments over a receding horizon. We deployed GRF-MPC on the Robotic Upright Stand Trainer (RobUST) in an underactuated 4-cable configuration and evaluated it against a fully actuated 8-cable Cartesian impedance baseline in nearly 300 trials across six healthy participants. Across tasks spanning impulsive perturbations, lunge trajectories, and voluntary limits of stability, GRF-MPC reduced peak planar center-of-mass (CoM) error by 19.6% during perturbations, lowered trajectory tracking error by 24.1%, required 28.7% less net mechanical work, and permitted a 45.9% greater normalized voluntary excursion area. The underlying QP solves in under 1 ms, comfortably enabling 100 Hz replanning and demonstrating that incorporating live human-ground interaction into predictive control yields stronger, more transparent physical assistance with half the actuators.

</details>


### [145] [Latent Barrier Steering: Hierarchical Safety for Generative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13227)
*Renhao Zhang,Mingzhe Li,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LBS提出层次化"语义→路径"安全框架：先在行为潜空间将生成器潜变量朝高安全裕度方向引导，再对解码轨迹用路径空间CBF-QP仅修复残余违反，并给出有限时间恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向扩散/流匹配规划器的CBF安全过滤器只在采样中或预测后对生成样本做局部修补，当新约束直接封锁整个被选中的行为模式（而非局部扰动）时，局部修复容易失效。

Method: 将行为选择与路径认证解耦：(1) 在生成器的行为潜空间进行引导（latent steering），把潜变量推向安全裕度更大的轨迹；(2) 对引导后潜变量解码得到路径，再施加路径空间CBF二次规划以处理残余违反。理论上证明潜空间引导可增大路径级修正器可用的安全裕度，从而获得有限时间恢复保证并降低修正负担。

Result: 在导航与机器人末端执行器规划任务上，相比路径级安全过滤器，LBS在保持安全性的同时提升了目标到达率并降低了修正负担（以仿真/任务级实验为主，未见真机验证描述）。

Conclusion: 分层安全干预（潜空间粗引导 + 路径空间精细CBF修正）比纯路径级过滤更有效，为流匹配/扩散规划器提供了带理论保证的安全生成范式；可作为具身规划器中安全层的可组合模块。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Barrier Steering (LBS), a hierarchical semantic-to-path framework for safe flow-matching-based generative planning. Existing control-barrier-function (CBF) safety filters for diffusion and flow-matching planners act locally on generated samples, either during sampling or after prediction. Such repair is effective for small violations, but can struggle when a new constraint blocks the whole selected behavior mode rather than merely perturbing it locally. LBS decouples behavior selection from path certification through two coordinated interventions: it first steers the generator's behavior latent toward rollouts with larger safety margin, then decodes the steered latent and applies a path-space CBF quadratic program (QP) only for residual violations. We show that latent steering increases the margin available to the path-space corrector, yielding finite-time recovery guarantees and reduced correction burden. Experiments across navigation and robot end-effector planning tasks show that LBS preserves safety while improving goal reaching and reducing correction burden compared with path-level safety filters.

</details>


### [146] [Incorporating World Models in Decision Making for Next Best View Selection in Active Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13229)
*Nikita Sarawgi,Omey M. Manyar,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用少量本地标定数据学习一个"传感器回波适配器"，在冻结预训练世界模型的前提下估计预测表面的测量丢包，从而改进下一最佳视角选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练世界模型可指导视角选择，但预测表面在实际传感器工作条件下未必产生有效深度测量，导致名义选择反复瞄准却测不到的区域。

Method: 保持预训练预测器固定，仅学习一个轻量适配器，基于少量本地标定数据对预测表面逐点估计测量丢包（dropout）概率；将该概率纳入NBV评分函数，优先选取可填补实测重建空隙的视角。

Result: 真实机器人+结构光传感器实验：实测表面覆盖率更高，拍摄次数减少60%；且成功恢复名义方法反复瞄准却测不到的表面区域。

Conclusion: 预训练预测模型可通过少量数据适配到部署端条件，实现数据稀缺环境下的高效主动三维重建。

Abstract: Efficient robotic 3D reconstruction requires selecting views that contribute new surface measurements with few physical captures. Pretrained world models can guide this choice, but a predicted surface may not produce valid depth measurements under the operating conditions of the deployed sensor. We present a deployment adaptation methodology for next-best-view selection that learns a sensor-return adapter from limited local calibration data while keeping the pretrained predictor fixed. The adapter estimates measurement dropout over each predicted surface, allowing the selection method to favor views likely to fill gaps in the measured reconstruction. In a real-robot experiment with a structured-light sensor, the adapted method achieves higher measured surface coverage with 60% fewer captures than nominal selection. The adapted method recovers a surface region that the nominal method repeatedly targets but fails to measure. These results suggest that robots can reuse pretrained predictive models while adapting their decisions to local operating conditions in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [147] [Curie's Jarvis: Autonomous Robotic Chemistry Experiments from Printed Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13251)
*Rumi Loghmani,Bozhang Song,Victor Alvarado,Aliyah Redding,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Curie's Jarvis lets a Franka Panda arm execute chemistry protocols from a photo of a printed instruction sheet, using label reading across four camera views to distinguish identical unmarked containers on an ordinary bench, with per-step re-localization and content-conditioned execution parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类做台面实验时依赖印刷步骤单、手写标签和反复目视确认，而现有实验室自动化要求脚本化流程、固定工位或基准标记，难以迁移到为人布置的普通实验台。作者希望机器人以「像人一样」的方式完成化学实验。

Method: 系统流水线：(1) 从印刷步骤单照片中解析实验步骤；(2) 在无固定位置、无 fiducial 标记的普通台面上检测容器；(3) 通过四个相机视角读取标签，区分外观相同的容器；(4) 每一步执行前重新定位目标；(5) 依据被处理对象的内容推断执行方式（如移动速度等参数）。硬件为 Franka Panda 机械臂。

Result: 儿童化学套件的两套协议中分别完成 5/5 与 4/5 次全自主运行，无人工干预；在印刷与手写标签上的 160 次定位识别全部正确。

Conclusion: 该工作将「读取步骤—标签消歧—逐步重定位—内容自适应执行」这一人类式工作流引入机器人台面化学实验，向真实实验室场景的可迁移自动化迈进了一步；当前评测规模仍较小，主要局限在标签可靠性而非任务级闭环。

Abstract: A person running a bench experiment works from a printed instruction sheet, tells identical containers apart by the labels written on them, and looks at the bench again before each step. Lab automation usually works the other way around: the procedure is written as a script, and containers stand at fixed positions or carry fiducial markers. We present Curie's Jarvis, a system that lets a robot arm carry out a chemistry experiment on a bench set up for people. It reads the steps from a photo of the printed sheet, finds containers on an ordinary bench with no fixed positions or markers, tells identical-looking containers apart by reading their labels in four camera views, locates its target again before every step, and sets how each step is carried out, including its speed, from what is being handled. On a Franka Panda arm, Curie's Jarvis completed 5 of 5 runs of one protocol from a children's chemistry kit and 4 of 5 runs of another, with no human intervention, and in 160 locates on printed and handwritten labels it identified every container correctly. This brings robots a step closer to doing bench chemistry the way people do.

</details>


### [148] [Force-Closure Condition for Cooperative Multi-robot Pushing of Rectangular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13268)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 给出矩形物体在四个无摩擦推挤接触点下平面力封闭的充要闭式条件 (l1+l3-b)(l2+l4-a)<0，与质心无关，并据此构建几何可行性层嵌入全位姿轨迹跟踪控制，显著降低狭窄通道跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作推挤操作中接触点布置是否可行（能否达成力封闭、产生任意力与力矩）缺乏针对常见矩形物体的简洁判据，导致接触力分配前缺少快速几何校验手段。

Method: 对长度 b、宽度 a 的矩形在四条边上各一个无摩擦推挤接触点，解析推导平面力封闭的充要条件，得到闭式不等式判据；给出不满足时按两因子符号定位需调整的对偶接触对的规则；将判据作为几何可行性层嵌入全位姿轨迹跟踪框架，先校验接触构型再做接触力分配。

Result: 静态力螺旋可行性测试验证判据正确；狭窄通道浮动体仿真中，保持力封闭构型将 x、y 与展开偏航角 RMS 误差从 12.05 m、18.11 m、535.96° 降至 2.24 m、2.66 m、2.69°。

Conclusion: 矩形四接触推挤的力封闭可用极简闭式不等式判定，且与质心无关，可作为轻量几何层提升协作推挤控制鲁棒性；但验证限于仿真，尚缺实机与多物体/非矩形推广。

Abstract: In cooperative object manipulation, the placement of pushing contacts determines whether force closure can be achieved and agents can generate arbitrary forces and torques on the object correspondingly. This study derives a necessary and sufficient closed-form condition for the planar force closure of a rectangular object, a common object shape in everyday settings, under four frictionless pushing contacts. For a rectangle of length $b$ and width $a$, force closure holds if and only if $(l_1+l_3-b)(l_2+l_4-a)<0$, where $l_1,l_2,l_3,l_4$ are the four edge contact locations. This condition is independent of the object's center of mass and is used to verify contact configurations before contact force allocation. When the condition is not satisfied, the signs of the two factors indicate which opposing contact pair should be adjusted. The condition and adjustment rule are then integrated into a full-pose trajectory-tracking framework as a geometric feasibility layer to demonstrate their use in control. Static wrench feasibility tests validate the proposed condition, while narrow-passage floating-body simulations show that maintaining a force-closure configuration reduces the RMS tracking errors in $x$, $y$, and unwrapped yaw from $12.05$ m, $18.11$ m, and $535.96^\circ$ to $2.24$ m, $2.66$ m, and $2.69^\circ$, respectively.

</details>


### [149] [RaMOD: Moving-Object Detection from Raw mmWave Radar Cubes under Ego Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13271)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Lingzi Xing,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RaMOD：从车载单芯片毫米波雷达原始RAD立方体出发，显式估计自车运动以预测并抑制世界静态结构的Doppler区域，再以多尺度时序特征预测RAD空间3D框与类别，并联合预测径向/前向速度及多普勒解缠概率，实现实时运动目标检测与测速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车载感知多将毫米波雷达输出压缩为稀疏点云或2D投影，浪费了原始range-azimuth-Doppler（RAD）立方体信息；同时自车运动使静态结构产生多普勒响应，且多普勒卷绕（wrapping）造成径向速度混叠，使运动目标检测与测速困难。

Method: 1) 逐帧显式估计自车雷达平移速度；2) 用该速度预测世界静态结构的多普勒区间并在RAD立方体中抑制其回波；3) 对抑制后立方体做时序编码，构建多尺度特征，预测带类别的RAD空间包围框；4) 对每个目标在原始多普勒与解缠两种假设下预测径向/前向速度，并输出多普勒卷绕概率；5) 端到端实时框架，无需点云中间表示。

Result: 在自建BRIDGE-Liv数据集上，RAD-cube、RA、RD三种检测设置下均优于所有对比的原始雷达方法；RAD-cube下IoU 0.30 mAP达80.8%，比最强基线高15.7个百分点；速度MAE 1.128 m/s；多普勒卷绕判定准确率99.87%。

Conclusion: 直接在原始RAD立方体上建模、并显式处理自车运动与多普勒卷绕，是提升雷达运动目标检测与测速的有效路径；该工作给出了实时可部署的单芯片雷达感知方案。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides weather-robust sensing and direct Doppler measurements, yet automotive perception pipelines often reduce its output to sparse point clouds or two-dimensional projections, leaving raw range-azimuth-Doppler (RAD) cubes underutilized. Ego motion induces Doppler responses from world-static structures, while Doppler wrapping aliases radial velocities, complicating moving-object detection and velocity estimation. We present RaMOD (Radar-based Moving-Object Detection), a real-time framework for moving-object detection and velocity estimation from raw RAD cubes acquired by a vehicle-mounted single-chip radar. RaMOD explicitly estimates frame-wise ego motion and uses the estimated radar translational velocity to predict the Doppler region of world-static structures and suppress returns within it. It temporally encodes the suppressed cubes into multi-scale features to predict RAD-space boxes with class labels. For each detected object, it predicts radial and forward velocities under raw-Doppler and Doppler-unwrapped hypotheses, together with Doppler-wrapping probabilities. On our BRIDGE-Liv dataset, RaMOD outperforms all compared raw-radar methods across RAD-cube, RA, and RD detection settings, achieving 80.8% cube mAP at IoU 0.30, 15.7 percentage points above the strongest RAD-cube baseline. Velocity MAE is 1.128 m/s, with 99.87% Doppler-wrapping accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [Large Language Model Explainability for Planning Lunar Rover Trajectories under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13292)
*Michelle Ho,Maximilian Adang,Zachary Booth,David E. Astle,Margaret Hansen,Edward Balaban,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的交互式助手，在规划完成后解释基于采样的行星漫游车轨迹规划器的输出，并可按用户条件重跑规划器以比较方案；采用ReAct框架调用动作工具与检索函数，接线NASA VIPER风格模拟器；在查询库上90.9%回复忠实于规划器输出，较无工具的零样本提示提升近65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星漫游车在概率不确定性下的轨迹规划是多目标优化问题，科学团队目前需手工迭代求解与验证，任务规划期时间线与约束频繁变动时不可行；采样式规划器（如MCTS）虽能加速，但其输出缺乏可追溯到科学目标的可解释性，团队无法验证。已有工作把LLM放入规划决策回路，本文转而探索规划后解释，因不直接影响决策而更易被信任。

Method: 框架由两部分组成：受NASA VIPER任务启发的模拟器，以及可ReAct查询的接口，后者调用经过策划的动作工具（重新运行规划器、设定条件）与检索函数。助手用领域词汇解释规划器轨迹，支持方案比较、可行性与查表类查询。

Result: 在包含方案比较、可行性与查表查询的查询库上，90.9%的回复忠实于规划器输出，相比同一LLM无工具的零样本提示提升近65%。

Conclusion: 规划后解释（而非介入决策）是LLM在不确定性感知规划中建立可信可解释性的可行路径；工具化ReAct接口显著提升忠实度。局限在于依赖模拟器、缺乏真实部署与真实机器人验证，评估规模较小。

Abstract: Planning planetary rover trajectories under probabilistic uncertainty is a multi-objective optimization problem that science teams currently iteratively solve and verify by hand. This process becomes impractical when timelines and constraints frequently shift throughout the mission planning phase. Sampling-based planners such as Monte Carlo tree search can speed up this process, but their outputs do not preserve traceability to the science objectives that justify each plan, which the science team needs to verify. We propose an interactive assistant based on a large language model (LLM) that can explain planner trajectories using domain vocabulary and re-run the planner under user-specified conditions to compare alternatives. Previous work has used LLMs inside planner decision-making loops, but we explore using them for post-planning explanation, where they are more likely to be trusted since they do not directly influence planner decisions. Our framework pairs a simulator inspired by the NASA Volatiles Investigating Polar Exploration Rover (VIPER) mission with a ReAct queryable interface that calls curated action tools and retrieval functions. We evaluate the assistant on a query bank consisting of plan comparisons, feasibility, and lookup queries. Experiments show that 90.9% of the assistant's responses are faithful to the planner's outputs, a nearly 65% improvement over zero-shot prompting of the same LLM without tool access, demonstrating the potential for trustworthy explainability of uncertainty-aware traverses with an LLM-based system.

</details>


### [151] [Does Haptic Connection to a Healthy Partner Improve Motor Learning in Chronic Stroke? A Pilot Study of Robot-Based Dyadic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13307)
*Erica L. Waters,Natalia Stroutinsky-Sobotka,Reem M. Azar,Frances S. Shofer,Pamela Z. Cacchione,Rochelle J. Mendonca,Michelle J. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一项针对慢性卒中患者的机器人触觉双人（dyad）配对训练先导研究：在单独与配对条件下学习复杂追踪任务，配对组训练期表现、努力与运动复杂度更高，但两组均未呈现明确学习与保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉双人交互被认为可通过促进运动变异性、提供隐式沟通通道与增强社交互动来提升运动学习，但针对神经损伤人群（如卒中）的研究稀少，缺乏完整临床评估。

Method: 慢性卒中个体先导实验，训练前完成认知、运动、感觉评估；在单独与双人条件下载复杂追踪任务；以追踪误差、运动距离、平均绝对功率、样本熵等运动学指标评估变化，并联结临床评分与基线追踪误差做相关分析。

Result: 基线追踪误差与较低认知分数、较高运动分数强相关（r>0.6）；仅单独组在评估时追踪误差下降（Δ=-5.33，95%CI[-10.25,-0.40]，p=0.027），但去除一离群被试后结论减弱；两组均无明确保持效应；双人组训练期表现、努力与运动复杂度显著更高。

Conclusion: 所选任务对两组均过难，可能源于高认知负荷，未能揭示学习；样本量与卒中异质性为局限。配对训练提升运动复杂度与努力（与更好运动学习相关），病变部位、认知、感觉与病程等临床特征可能影响配对训练获益。

Abstract: Purpose: Robot-based haptic dyads, where two users exchange forces through robots, have been proposed for robot-assisted neurorehabilitation. This intervention could increase motor learning by promoting motor variability, providing an implicit communication channel, and facilitating social interaction. Despite the potential benefits, few studies have investigated robot-based haptic dyads for neurological populations, and none have included a comprehensive clinical assessment. Design/Methodology/Approach: We present a pilot study investigation of individuals with chronic stroke learning a complex tracking task in solo and dyad conditions. Prior to training, participants completed a battery of cognitive, motor, and sensory assessments. We assessed changes in key kinematic outcomes including tracking error, distance traveled, mean absolute power, and sample entropy. We also performed a correlation analysis of clinical scores with baseline tracking errors. Findings: Baseline tracking error was strongly correlated (r > 0.6) with lower cognitive scores and higher motor scores. Only the solo group demonstrated lower tracking error at assessment ({\Delta} = -5.33, 95%CI [-10.25, -0.40], p = 0.027), but this result was tempered by the removal of an outlier participant. Neither group demonstrated clear retention. The dyad group showed significantly elevated performance, effort, and movement complexity during training. Research Limitations/Implications: The selected task was too difficult to reveal learning for either group, which may be related to its high cognitive demand. Sample size and stroke heterogeneity were also limitation. Originality/Value: Dyadic training increased movement complexity and effort, which are associated with better motor learning. Clinical characteristics such as lesion location, cognition, sensation, and chronicity may influence potential benefits of dyadic training.

</details>


### [152] [PneuGen: Task-Driven Agentic Co-Design of Soft Pneumatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13344)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Perla Maiolino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PneuGen：用LLM智能体（提议设计程序的designer + 分析仿真诊断并给出修改建议的critic）在软体机器人设计空间上高效协同设计，结合参数化组件库与可复用的"模块响应模型"物理先验，自动将设计程序校验并转成有限元仿真迭代评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人任务驱动设计需同时考虑几何、形态、材料与驱动，设计空间庞大；传统计算优化方法在优化与仿真两侧计算负担高、探索效率低。

Method: Agentic co-design框架：designer智能体从参数化组件库生成设计程序；critic智能体解析仿真诊断并推荐定向修改；拟合模块响应模型提供几何—压力—变形的可复用物理先验；设计程序自动校验并转为有限元仿真，形成迭代闭环（含消融）。

Result: 在基本形状与挑战物体的抓取任务、以及需多方向平面与3D形变的形状匹配任务上显著优于先前计算优化基线；消融显示critic智能体与模块响应模型均有贡献；并展示真实世界形状匹配与抓取。

Conclusion: LLM智能体+物理先验+仿真反馈的协同设计范式可在昂贵仿真下高效探索软体机器人设计空间，并具备真机可行性。

Abstract: Task-driven design of soft robots requires the consideration of factors such as geometry, morphology, material properties, and actuation, resulting in a large and complex design space that is challenging to explore. Computational optimisation methods leverage signals from simulation to iteratively improve designs to achieve task objectives. However, these methods typically demand high computational load for both optimisation and simulation. We present PneuGen, an agentic co-design framework for soft robots that leverages Large Language Model (LLM) agents to search over the soft robot design space efficiently. A designer agent proposes design programs from a parametric component library, and a separate critic agent analyses simulation diagnostics and recommends targeted revisions. Fitted module response models provide reusable physical priors relating geometry and pressure to deformation. Design programs are automatically validated and converted into finite-element simulations, enabling iterative evaluation. Across grasping tasks on primitive shapes and challenging objects, as well as shape-matching tasks that require multi-directional planar and 3D deformation, the proposed framework significantly outperforms prior computational optimisation baselines. Ablation experiments also highlight the utility of the critic agent and the module response model in facilitating efficient design. We additionally demonstrate the agent-generated soft robot designs in real-world shape-matching and grasping tasks.

</details>


### [153] [AHEADance: Anticipatory Humanoid Dance from Streaming Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13370)
*Tianhu Peng,Yukun Wang,Lingfan Bao,Yiyang Jia,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHEADance：机器人原生流式舞蹈生成器，含 ListenAhead（用冻结音乐模型预测未来音乐并与已听音乐按时对齐、按可靠度加权）与 Commit Forcing（从自身已提交的 motion chunk 训练延续），在 Unitree G1 实机上随流式音乐跳舞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式音乐下的类人舞蹈要求每段动作在被听到伴奏音乐之前就提交，且必须从已提交动作继续，存在"动作提前提交 vs. 音乐同步"与"自回归误差累积导致低活动"两大矛盾。

Method: (1) ListenAhead：以已听音乐加冻结音乐模型对后续音乐的预测为条件，按目标时间对齐并按可靠度加权融合；(2) Commit Forcing：以模型自身已提交 chunk 为历史训练延续，同时更新潜变量历史与下一 chunk 的解码边界；(3) 三个经真实 G1 舞蹈验证的音乐评估器分别测同步、歌曲匹配、风格；由全身 tracker 在实机上执行。

Result: 预测信号在节拍时序上接近真实未来，生成动作与音乐同步更好、运动能量更集中于歌曲节拍频率；Commit Forcing 将低活动比例从 83.5% 降至 10.3%，接近真实舞蹈水平；G1 实机随音乐起舞、音乐停止即静止、无跌倒。

Conclusion: 以"预测未来音乐 + 强制自一致延续"可解决流式生成下的同步与活动崩塌问题，方案可实机部署于类人机器人，形成端到端音乐驱动全身舞蹈闭环。

Abstract: A humanoid that dances to streamed music must commit each motion chunk before it has heard the music that chunk accompanies, and must continue from motion it has already committed. We present AHEADance, a robot-native streaming dance generator executed on a humanoid robot by a whole-body tracker. ListenAhead conditions each chunk on heard music and a frozen music model's forecasts of the coming music, aligned to their target times and weighted by reliability. The forecasts carry the timing of upcoming beats almost as well as the actual future; with them, the generator is better synchronized with the music and concentrates more motion energy at its song's beat frequency. Commit Forcing trains continuation from the model's own committed chunks, updating both the latent history and the decoded boundary of the next chunk; it reduces low activity from 83.5% to 10.3%, the level of real dance. Three music evaluators, each validated on real G1 dance, test synchronization, song and style. On a physical Unitree G1, it dances while the music plays and comes to rest when it stops, without a fall. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [154] [Fastener-centric acquisition and AND/OR graph representation of state-dependent disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13379)
*Shiki Iwase*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以紧固件为中心的虚拟拆卸框架，从CAD装配体自动获取状态相关拆卸操作，并组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph用于拆卸规划与可拆性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计阶段的装配体可拆性评估需要产品结构与各装配状态下的可用操作知识，但CAD装配体并不直接给出状态相关的拆卸操作，现有方法缺乏此类知识获取途径；同时逐一遍历单个紧固件顺序会导致组合爆炸。

Method: 1）将共同释放同一约束的紧固件聚合为操作级集合，避免任意单紧固件顺序枚举；2）通过状态相关的可见性与工具可达性检查及扭矩驱动的虚拟旋拧试验评估可拆卸性；3）紧固件移除后，用图推理识别候选运动单元，再经初始运动筛选、路径生成与基于物理的验证判断其可分离性；4）将操作、状态、依赖关系、验证路径与仿真导出属性组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph。

Result: 在受控玩具装配体上的穷举验证中，对引入的工具可达性与优先约束未发现分支违规；54部件OpenManipulator Chain夹爪实验显示出状态相关的紧固件拆卸与零件/子装配分离操作；可见性与工具可达性评估剪枝了51.4%的紧固件集合候选评估，且移除阻挡组件可解锁原先不可达的紧固件操作。

Conclusion: 结果表明可在制造前从CAD派生的虚拟装配中获取产品特定的拆卸知识，操作级分组与筛选将详细物理验证限制在机械上合理的候选上，为后续拆卸推理与面向可拆性的设计评估提供支撑。

Abstract: Design-stage disassemblability assessment requires knowledge of product structure and the operations available in each assembly state, but CAD assemblies do not directly specify state-dependent disassembly operations. This paper proposes a fastener-centric virtual-disassembly framework that acquires such knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies. Fasteners jointly releasing the same constraint are grouped into operation-level sets, avoiding enumeration of arbitrary individual-fastener orders. Their removability is evaluated through state-dependent visibility and tool-access checks and torque-driven virtual-removal trials. After fastener removal, graph-based reasoning identifies candidate moving units, whose separation is evaluated by initial-motion screening, path generation, and physics-based validation. The acquired operations, states, dependencies, validated paths, and simulation-derived attributes are organized into a Transformed AND/OR Graph for subsequent planning and design evaluation. Exhaustive verification with a controlled toy assembly found no branching violations for introduced tool-accessibility and precedence conditions. Evaluation with a 54-component OpenManipulator Chain gripper demonstrated state-dependent fastener-removal and part- or subassembly-separation operations. Visibility and tool-accessibility evaluations pruned 51.4% of fastener-set candidate evaluations, while removal of obstructing components enabled previously inaccessible fastener operations. Operation-level grouping and screening thereby limited detailed physical validation to mechanically plausible candidates. The results demonstrate the feasibility of acquiring product-specific disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies before manufacture, supporting subsequent disassembly reasoning and design-for-disassembly evaluation.

</details>


### [155] [Discrete Model-Based Diffusion for Combinatorial Optimization in Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13382)
*Haejoon Lee,Thomas Marshall Vielmetti,Xinyi Wang,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Discrete Model-Based Diffusion (Discrete MBD)，一种免训练的采样式优化方法，通过蒙特卡洛采样近似类别反向转移分布，直接在离散空间上做扩散优化，并给出有限步找到全局最优解的概率下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体/多机器人系统含离散决策变量，形成需秒级求解的 NP-hard 组合优化问题。现有扩散方法存在取舍：Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) 无需训练但仅限连续空间；离散扩散组合求解器支持离散空间但需离线训练。

Method: 构造 Discrete MBD：将 MBD 的采样式优化框架推广到离散域，用蒙特卡洛采样估计类别（categorical）反向转移分布，从而免训练地直接优化离散变量；理论上建立有限去噪步数下找到全局最优的概率下界，并证明该界随步数增加收敛到 1。

Result: 在通用整数规划以及异构多机器人联盟形成与调度任务上，几乎所有设置下优于 SBO 基线；解质量与混合整数规划、强化学习方法相当，但计算开销仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 免训练离散扩散可作为多机器人组合决策的实用求解器，兼顾解质量与近实时计算预算；理论下界为去噪步数的选择提供了依据。

Abstract: Decision making in multi-agent and multi-robot systems often involves discrete decision variables, leading to NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems that must be solved within seconds for near real-time implementation. Denoising diffusion has emerged as a powerful tool for such problems, but existing approaches face a trade-off: Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) incorporates diffusion in sampling-based optimization (SBO) without training but only over continuous spaces, while existing diffusion-based combinatorial solvers operate over discrete spaces but require offline training. To this end, we propose Discrete Model-Based Diffusion (Discrete MBD), a training-free SBO method that directly optimizes discrete variables by approximating categorical reverse transition distributions through Monte Carlo sampling. We establish a finite-step lower bound on the probability of finding a global minimizer and show that this bound converges to one as the number of denoising steps increases. Extensive experiments on generic integer programs and heterogeneous multi-robot coalition formation and scheduling demonstrate that Discrete MBD outperforms SBO baselines in nearly all settings while achieving solution quality competitive with that of mixed-integer programming and reinforcement learning methods at a fraction of their computational cost.

</details>


### [156] [Rhythmic-Semantic Co-Speech Gestures for Emotional Dialogue in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13472)
*Christian Arzate Cruz,Edwin C. Montiel-Vázquez,Stefanos Gkikas,Miguel Mejia Tobar,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出情感感知的节奏-语义协同语音手势生成系统，从文本与机器人内部情感状态实时生成标志性(iconic)手势与节拍(beat)手势，并在社交机器人Haru上完成60人用户研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的机器人系统通常要么只生成节奏性beat动作，要么只生成语义性iconic手势，难以兼顾参与度与语义可懂度；同时缺少将机器人情感状态纳入手势生成的统一形式化框架。

Method: 将beat-iconic协同手势生成任务以情感为条件形式化；采用轻量模型先预测iconic手势的时间与强度，再在剩余时间跨度内以合适强度填充情感条件化的beat动作；在BEAT2数据集上训练，部署于社交机器人Haru进行在线用户研究。

Result: 60名参与者的在线用户研究表明，该方法相比基线方法具有更强的语音-手势对齐性，并更受被试偏好。

Conclusion: 情感条件化的节奏-语义联合手势生成可实时运行于社交机器人，优于仅生成单一类型手势的基线，适用于人机交互场景。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures can increase engagement and improve speech understanding. Yet, most data-driven robot systems generate either rhythmic beat-like motion or semantically meaningful iconic gestures, but not both. We present an emotion-aware rhythmic-semantic co-speech gesture system that generates iconic and beat behaviors in real time from text and the robot's internal emotional state. We formalize the task of beat-iconic gesture generation using emotion and propose lightweight models that predict iconic timing and intensity, then fill the remaining spans with emotion-conditioned beats at an appropriate intensity level. We train on the BEAT2 dataset and deploy the system on the social robot Haru. In an online user study with 60 participants, our method shows stronger speech-gesture alignment and is preferred over baseline methods.

</details>


### [157] [ExRL: Sample-Efficient Online Adaptation of Long-Chunk Policies via Execution Length Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13482)
*Xu Luo,Jiaying Yang,Hao Wu,Junlin Xie,Zijin Hong,Ji Zhang,Lianli Gao,Jingkuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExRL 将长动作块策略的强化学习微调从"优化动作"改为"优化每个预测动作块的执行长度",用轻量适配器输出低维执行长度、以 off-policy RL 训练,从而在在线样本极少的情况下完成适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长动作块(数十步)策略已成为高层机器人策略的关键组成,但直接对高维动作块做在线 RL 微调需要大量交互样本,在真实世界中数据采集成本极高,导致适配不可行。

Method: 在基座策略上附加一个轻量适配器,预测每个动作块的执行长度(即在切换下一个块之前执行多少步);仅对该低维执行长度参数做 off-policy RL 优化,适配空间维度固定而表达能力随块长增长;任务特定超参极少。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实机器人任务上,对多种长动作块策略实现样本高效的 RL 适配,且所需任务特定调参很少。

Conclusion: 把 RL 适配的变量从动作本身转移到动作块的执行时长,是一种低成本、通用性强的长块策略在线改进范式;代码/项目细节与真实任务的量化对比需阅读正文确认。

Abstract: Predicting long action chunks, often spanning dozens of timesteps, has become a key ingredient in increasingly capable robot policies. In this paper, we find that adapting these long-chunk policies with existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods can require many online samples due to the high dimensionality of the action chunks being optimized, making such adaptation impractical in real-world scenarios where interaction data are costly to collect. Our key insight is to shift adaptation from optimizing actions to optimizing how long to execute each predicted action chunk. This keeps the adaptation space low-dimensional while its expressivity grows with the chunk size. We thus introduce Execution at the Right Length (ExRL), a simple approach which augments the base policy with a lightweight adapter that predicts the execution length of each chunk and is trained using off-policy RL algorithms. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic tasks, ExRL enables sample-efficient adaptation of a diverse range of long-chunk policies with minimal task-specific hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [158] [Route-Time Conflict Maps for Cooperative End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13494)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Tianle Zhu,Zhipeng Bao,Yiping Li,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: When2Go 将协同端到端驾驶从 SAE J3216 Class A 升级到 Class B：车辆广播带预测运动的 LiDAR 实例 token、自车规划与意图轨迹，接收方构建"路线-时间冲突图"，规划器在其上给候选速度曲线打分，并用由路权规则蒸馏出的顺序门（order gate）在轨迹冲突时决定通行次序。单模型在三个 CARLA 闭环基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同端到端驾驶只停留在状态共享（Class A）层面：车辆交换的是"感知到什么"而非"意图做什么"，各自独立决定何时通行，导致双方同时让行时死锁、同时抢行时碰撞，合作能力受限。

Method: (1) 意图共享：每车广播 LiDAR 检测及其预测运动为实例 token，并附带自车 plan 与意图轨迹；(2) 接收端将融合检测、预测与伙伴 plan 渲染为沿自车路线的 route-time conflict map（规划时域内的占用栅格）；(3) 规划器在该图上对候选速度剖面打分；(4) 冲突时由 order gate——从路权规则蒸馏出的 precedence network——裁决谁先行，双方基于相同消息得到一致顺序；(5) 模仿 V2Xverse 中可见仿真真值的规则专家，并把 2s 规划时域外不可观测的停车重新标注，避免学习"无因等待"；(6) 评测改动：基准 PID 用 When2Go 路点而非路线转向，关闭长等待车辆的强制推动。

Result: 单模型在三项闭环 CARLA 基准上均为最优：InterDrive 无/有交通 97.8 / 90.7（此前最佳 90.1 / 78.9）；公开 V2Xverse 协议 70.0（16 个复跑方法最佳 59.1）；MDrive 88.2（复跑最佳 85.1）；95.0% 车辆无碰撞（对比 87.7%）。

Conclusion: 意图级（Class B+）协同共享配合 route-time 冲突图与规则蒸馏的顺序裁决，可解决 Class A 协同中的死锁与冲突问题，并在多个闭环基准上超越状态共享方法。评测中修改了控制器接口与强制推车机制，说明部分增益可能依赖评测配置，跨协议鲁棒性仍待验证。

Abstract: Existing cooperative end-to-end driving works at Class A (status-sharing) of the SAE J3216 classes of cooperative driving automation (CDA): vehicles exchange what they perceive, not what they intend, so each plans alone when to go, which limits cooperation and leads to deadlock when both vehicles yield or to collisions when both go. We present When2Go, a cooperative end-to-end driving system that learns when to go by sharing intent under CDA Class B or higher. Each vehicle broadcasts its LiDAR detections with forecast motion as instance tokens, together with its plan and intended trajectory. The receiver draws the fused detections, forecasts and partner plans into a route-time conflict map, the occupancy along its route over the planning horizon, on which a planner scores candidate speed profiles. Where two intended trajectories conflict, an order gate, a precedence network distilled from a right-of-way rule, decides who goes first; both vehicles evaluate it on the same messages and so reach the same order. When2Go imitates the rule-based expert of V2Xverse, which sees the simulator's ground truth; stops that When2Go cannot observe within its 2 s planning horizon are relabelled, so that it does not learn to wait without an observable cause. For our runs only, the benchmarks' PID controller steers by When2Go's waypoints instead of the route, and its forced push of long-waiting vehicles is off. With one model, When2Go sets the state of the art on three closed-loop CARLA benchmarks: 97.8 and 90.7 driving score on InterDrive without and with traffic (best published 90.1 and 78.9), 70.0 on the public V2Xverse protocol (best of sixteen rerun methods 59.1) and 88.2 on MDrive (best rerun 85.1), with 95.0% of its vehicles collision-free (87.7%).

</details>


### [159] [DeRep: Learning Depth Representations for End-to-End Navigation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13499)
*Hanyu Jin,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeRep: 面向导航的视觉表征预训练方法，结合局部鸟瞰图(BEV)可达性监督与深度重建，冻结编码器后在多种下游策略学习器上验证表征质量，并实现无人机零样本sim-to-real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端杂乱环境导航的表征多与策略联合学习或依赖观测重建，未显式规定表征应保留何种信息；且通常仅用单一策略学习器评估，无法判断表征收益是否依赖策略学习方式。因此需在受控条件下系统比较不同表征学习目标对下游导航性能与鲁棒性的影响。

Method: 提出DeRep，将局部BEV可达性监督(可通行区域预测)与深度重建联合作为预训练目标；所有编码器采用统一架构分别预训练后冻结，下游分别用线性策略探针、flow-matching模仿学习与PPO强化学习进行评测，从而隔离表征目标与策略学习器的影响。

Result: DeRep在线性策略探针上取得最低预测误差；在全部六种PPO条件下优于纯重建目标；模仿学习中两个含可达性目标的方法平均成功率均高于纯重建，仅可达性与联合目标的平均成功率相近。冻结DeRep编码器的PPO策略在Gazebo与真实四旋翼上零样本迁移，无需微调。

Conclusion: 显式可达性监督能稳定提升深度表征在多种下游学习范式与评测条件下的导航表现，且具备良好的sim-to-sim与sim-to-real零样本迁移能力；冻结式受控评测流程为表征目标比较提供了可复现范式。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation in cluttered environments relies on compact visual representations to support collision avoidance and goal-directed motion. Existing methods typically learn these representations jointly with the policy or through observation reconstruction, which does not explicitly specify what information the representation should preserve. Moreover, they are usually evaluated with a single downstream policy learner, although the benefit of a representation may depend on how the policy is learned. It therefore remains unclear how different representation-learning objectives affect downstream navigation performance and robustness. This work proposes DeRep, a navigation-oriented representation-learning method that combines local bird's-eye-view reachability supervision with depth reconstruction. To compare representation objectives under controlled conditions, we pretrain encoders with a common architecture and freeze them during downstream learning. We evaluate the representations using a linear policy probe, flow-matching imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with PPO. DeRep achieves the lowest linear policy probe prediction error and outperforms reconstruction alone across all six tested PPO conditions. In imitation learning, both reachability-based objectives achieve higher mean success rates than reconstruction alone, with similar mean success rates between the reachability-only and joint objectives. These results demonstrate consistent benefits of incorporating reachability supervision into depth representation pretraining across different downstream learning methods and evaluation conditions. We further deploy the PPO policy with the frozen DeRep encoder in Gazebo and on a physical quadrotor without fine-tuning, demonstrating its capability for zero-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [160] [Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13517)
*Yaru Gu,Ting Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DFIO 将误差状态卡尔曼滤波（ESKF）的更新阶段嵌入计算图，实现端到端可微滤波，联合优化位移与协方差输出头，显著降低行人惯导里程计的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TLIO 等方法中神经伪测量与下游滤波器独立训练，网络目标与滤波后位姿误差不一致，导致积分噪声与零偏引起的无界漂移无法被有效抑制。

Method: 在 TLIO 式网络独立预训练后，把 ESKF 的测量更新步骤写为可微软更新，用融合后的后验位置作为监督信号，联合优化位移头与协方差（不确定性）头，使网络输出适配滤波器行为。

Result: 在公开行人运动数据集上，相对 TLIO 基线平均绝对平移误差与 1 秒相对平移误差分别降低 13.2% 和 39.6%；相对预训练模型分别降低 7.7% 和 17.4%。在未标定智能手机惯性数据上有效抑制平移漂移并保持轨迹对齐。

Conclusion: 可微滤波是一种将滤波先验与学习方法耦合的有效范式，可在噪声大、未标定的传感器条件下提升惯性里程计的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Accurate inertial odometry is critical for autonomous systems but suffers from unbounded drift caused by the integration of sensor noise and biases. In approaches such as TLIO, neural pseudo-measurements are trained independently of their downstream filter. This paper introduces Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry (DFIO), a learning framework that embeds the update stages of an error-state Kalman filter directly into a computational graph. Following standalone pre-training, DFIO jointly optimizes the displacement and covariance output heads using posterior-position supervision through differentiable measurement updates. The proposed system is validated on public pedestrian motion datasets featuring both calibrated and uncalibrated inertial measurements. Experimental results on a pedestrian dataset demonstrate that DFIO reduces mean absolute and one-second relative translation errors by 13.2% and 39.6% relative to the TLIO baseline, and by 7.7% and 17.4% relative to its pre-trained counterpart. Furthermore, evaluations on uncalibrated smartphone inertial data indicate that the proposed method effectively mitigates translational drift and maintains trajectory alignment under highly noisy sensor conditions. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [161] [GEMOR-GS: Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13528)
*Yuchen Yang,Ming Ding,Xinyu Wu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEMOR-GS 提出一个 LiDAR-惯性-视觉 SLAM 框架：先离线用直接法标定 LiDAR-相机外参，再在门控的可微渲染+LiDAR 投影目标下交替优化关键帧位姿与 3DGS 参数，最后基于精化后的关键帧结构重建全局高斯地图，并借助 XFeat 旁路地图 + RANSAC-PnP + 高斯 RGB-D warp 实现跟踪丢失后的在线重定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在连续运行中需要光度真实且几何度量一致的世界表示；3DGS 虽提供了显式、可渲染、可查询的场景模型，但现有 LiDAR-inertial-visual 高斯 SLAM 的地图几何精度、全局一致性、以及跟踪丢失后可重进入性不足，限制了其作为机器人世界模型的可靠性。

Method: 1) 离线在专用标定段上用直接法对齐估计 LiDAR-相机外参，供后续所有在线/离线流程复用；2) 在标定几何下，以门控的可微渲染损失与 LiDAR 投影损失联合交替精化关键帧位姿与高斯参数；3) 解除在线约束后，从精化关键帧模式重建最终全局地图；4) 重定位流程：基于 3DGS 地图的 XFeat 旁路地图检索，RANSAC-PnP 粗定位，再用高斯 RGB-D warp 精化。

Result: 在自采集 1 个与 5 个公开序列上：离线重建对所有成对关键帧均优于在线地图（14.3–19.9 dB）；PSNR 相比 Gaussian-LIC2 提升约 9 dB；留出集重定位成功率达 86–100%，且与标定质量相关。

Conclusion: GEMOR-GS 提升了 3DGS 地图的几何精度、外观保真度与可重进入性，可作为面向 SLAM 与下游任务的可靠机器人世界模型。

Abstract: Photorealistic and metrically consistent world representations are a primary requirement for LiDAR--inertial--visual SLAM systems that serve robots over continuous operation. 3D Gaussian splatting makes such representations attainable by supplying an explicit, renderable, and queryable scene model. We present GEMOR-GS (Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM) to make the Gaussian map geometrically accurate, globally refined, and re-enterable, providing a better appearance for robot perception and decision-making. To improve the 3DGS map, the LiDAR--camera extrinsic is first estimated offline by direct-method alignment over dedicated calibration segments and reused by all following online and offline processes. Under the calibrated geometry, keyframe poses and Gaussian parameters are refined alternately under a gated differentiable-rendering and LiDAR--projection objective. The final global reconstruction is then rebuilt from the refined keyframe schema with all online constraints lifted. For the robot SLAM lifecycle, the method recovers the pose from the 3DGS map after tracking loss through an XFeat bypass map, RANSAC-PnP coarse localization, and Gaussian RGB-D warp refinement. Experiments on one self-collected and five public sequences quantify each element: the offline reconstruction wins all paired keyframes against the online map by 14.3--19.9 dB, improves PSNR by approximately 9 dB over Gaussian-LIC2, and achieves 86--100% held-out relocalization success tied to extrinsic quality. The results show that GEMOR-GS improves the geometric accuracy, appearance fidelity, and re-enterability of the 3DGS map, serving as a dependable robot world model for SLAM and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [162] [Few-Shot Residual Adaptation of Pretrained Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13592)
*Jakub Suliga,Pankhuri Vanjani,Nisarga Nilavadi,Wolfram Burgard,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 冻结预训练潜在世界模型，用少量交互学习自监督残差修正（同时作用于潜在预测器内部与预测潜状态），在真实机器人上从质心/摩擦改变中恢复控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练潜在世界模型可支撑视觉规划与控制，但部署时物理动力学改变会导致预测失效；作者希望在不改预训练权重、不辨识具体物理参数的前提下，仅用少量交互实现自适应。

Method: 基于潜在预测误差的自监督残差适应：学习一个修正模块，双站点注入——(1) 潜预测器内部特征处，(2) 直接叠加到输出的预测潜状态；预训练世界模型全程冻结。仿真与真实机器人在质心偏移与摩擦变化下评测，并做单站点/双站点消融与诊断分析。

Result: 冻结模型在两类偏移下成功率降至接近0；仅用3个交互回合适应后，真实机器人上质心偏移场景成功率恢复至90%，摩擦改变场景恢复至100%。消融显示双站点优于单站点；诊断表明大部分学习到的修正是在弥合通用的预测失配，而成功控制还依赖适应过程中捕获的偏移特异性信息。

Conclusion: 少量交互、冻结骨干的残差式潜空间适应是部署期动力学偏移的实用方案；修正位置与偏移特异性信息的结合对闭环控制成败至关重要。

Abstract: Pretrained latent world models can support visual planning and control, but their predictions may fail when physical dynamics change at deployment. We study whether such models can adapt from only a few interactions without modifying their pretrained weights or identifying the changed physical parameters. We introduce a few-shot residual adaptation approach that learns self-supervised corrections from latent prediction errors. The corrections are applied both inside the latent predictor and directly to its predicted latent state, while the pretrained world model remains frozen. We evaluate the approach in simulation and on a real robot under center-of-mass and friction shifts. These changes reduce the success of the frozen model to near zero. Using three interaction episodes for adaptation, our dual-site approach recovers 90\% success under the center-of-mass shift and 100\% under the modified friction on the real robot. Ablations show that correcting both locations outperforms single-site adaptation. Our diagnostics further suggest that much of the learned correction reduces a general prediction mismatch, while successful control also depends on shift-specific information captured during adaptation.

</details>


### [163] [From Egocentric Human Video to Robot Manipulation in the Era of Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13610)
*Yi Wang,Jingqiu Wu,Lei Yao,Zhaoshuo Li,Haoyu Zhu,Yirui Zhou,Huaiyuan Xu,Ying Zheng,Chuang Yang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统梳理"Ego2Robot learning"——从第一人称人类视频（EHV）中学习机器人操作策略的四类范式：显式具身迁移、显式动作与任务监督、自我中心视频表征预训练、隐式动作与动力学建模，并综述相关数据集与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作数据采集昂贵且难以规模化，而第一人称人类交互视频可提供近距手-物交互与日常活动时序结构，潜在可作为可扩展经验来源；但缺少机器人动作标注，且存在形态、视角、动作空间差异，因此需要系统性厘清如何从中提取可用监督。

Method: 综述/概念框架构建：提出Ego2Robot learning定义，按"从人类视频中获取什么"划分为四种范式（机器人格式示范、可解释监督、视觉表征、动作与动力学预测模型），逐一追踪所提取的EHV信息、机器人策略的使用方式、额外数据需求，并梳理涵盖EHV、机器人示范、合成与仿真资源的基准数据集。

Result: 形成了以"EHV数据贡献—任务信息保留程度—机器人特定grounding（训练或部署阶段）"为主线的分析框架，并系统整理了跨范式的数据集资源。

Conclusion: 指出该方向在物理可行性、智能体协同、上下文内适应、受控评测与预测控制五方面的挑战；本身不提出新方法，属路线图性质的综述。

Abstract: Egocentric human video (EHV) records close-range hand-object interaction (HOI) and the temporal structure of everyday activities, offering a potentially scalable source of experience for robot manipulation. Using this experience for robot control requires recovering useful supervision without recorded robot actions and accounting for differences in morphology, viewpoint, and action space. We define Ego2Robot learning as the process of learning or adapting robot manipulation policies from EHV, organized into four learning paradigms: explicit embodiment transfer, explicit action and task supervision, egocentric video representation pretraining, and latent action and dynamics modeling. The four paradigms differ in what they derive from human video: robot-format demonstrations, interpretable supervision, visual representations, or predictive models of actions and dynamics. For each paradigm, we trace what EHV information is extracted, how robot policies use it, and when the additional data is required. Besides, we review the benchmark datasets that cover EHV, robot demonstrations, and synthetic and simulated resources. This paper links EHV data's contribution to the task information preserved and the robot-specific grounding used during training or deployment. Finally, we identify challenges in physical feasibility, agent coordination, in-context adaptation, controlled evaluation, and predictive control.

</details>


### [164] [An IoT Based Approach for Platform Independent Positioning Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13634)
*Anh-Van Vu,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KAILOS的IoT扩展架构：用专用IoT设备替代移动App来实现WiFi室内定位服务，并给出设备软硬件设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAILOS 2014年基于WiFi+手机App的室内定位方案受操作系统权限限制较多，希望通过专用IoT设备绕过App层障碍，便捷地提供定位服务并拓宽应用场景。

Method: 在KAILOS原有WiFi定位基础上设计IoT设备端架构，包含硬件（WiFi扫描/通信模块）与软件（定位数据采集与位置估计、与后端服务交互）两部分的设计方案；属系统架构设计与原型实现。

Result: 摘要未给出量化精度、时延或对比实验，仅以演示/定性方式论证IoT路线可行、有前景。

Conclusion: IoT化是室内定位服务实用化的一条可行路径，可解除App平台限制；但缺少精度基准与规模部署证据。

Abstract: KAIST Indoor Localization System (KAILOS) was introduced in 2014 and became one of the world-first complete indoor positioning solutions. It is mainly based on the WiFi signal to estimate location and provide positioning service via mobile application that has many restrictions by the Operating System. This study proposes an extended architecture of KAILOS to make use of the IoT device for removing the barriers of the mobile-app-based approach and conveniently providing indoor positioning service. Design of the IoT device that contains hardware and software is also presented. Via this study, we prove that the IoT-based approach is promising and can be a crucial feature to widen applications of indoor localization services.

</details>


### [165] [NIF-VIO: Visual--Inertial Odometry Across Inertial and Non-Inertial Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13662)
*Zichao Shu,Lijun Li,Zhuochen Li,Zetao Chen,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NIF-VIO，针对非惯性载体（加速/旋转平台）内部相机与IMU观测不一致问题，基于测量一致性自适应切换融合策略，实现跨惯性/非惯性参考系相对局部环境的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VIO假设相机与IMU观测在同一惯性系下联合一致；但在加速/旋转载体内部，视觉观测反映载体相对运动，IMU含额外载体诱导分量，二者不一致导致常规融合失效。

Method: 按测量一致性自适应调整视觉-惯性融合：惯性条件变化时，先进行早期约束隔离（early constraint isolation）并配合局部重优化，在不一致被确认前抑制误差传播并修复受影响局部几何；进入非惯性状态后，引入时间共享参数的紧凑模型显式建模载体运动，同时保留与视觉互补的惯性信息。

Result: 在公开数据集与自采数据集上，惯性条件下性能具竞争力；非惯性条件下定位精度与可用性均提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将"参考系一致性"显式纳入VIO建模，为非惯性平台（如车载/机载/转台内感知）提供可行定位方案，但方法细节（状态估计形式、一致性判据、实验规模）在摘要中信息不足。

Abstract: Conventional visual--inertial odometry (VIO) assumes that observations from cameras and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) are jointly consistent in an inertial reference frame. However, this assumption breaks down under non-inertial conditions. Inside accelerating or rotating carriers, visual observations of the interior reflect carrier-relative motion, while IMU measurements contain additional carrier-induced components, which can compromise conventional fusion. We therefore present NIF-VIO for localization relative to the local environment across inertial and non-inertial reference frames. It adapts visual--inertial fusion based on measurement consistency. As inertial conditions change, early constraint isolation is combined with local reoptimization to limit error propagation before inconsistency confirmation and repair the affected local geometry. Under non-inertial conditions, a compact model with temporally shared parameters is introduced to account for carrier motion while preserving inertial information that complements visual constraints. Experiments on public benchmarks and a self-collected dataset demonstrate that NIF-VIO achieves competitive performance under inertial conditions and improved localization accuracy and availability under non-inertial conditions.

</details>


### [166] [Ego Guidance: Enhancing Robot Policy Learning with Action-Free Human Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13685)
*Lin Long,Tianren Ma,Zhong Chu,Di Wu,Maosong Cao,Qixiang Ye,Junbo Zhao,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于预训练VLM提出SPAR（Shared Prediction on Action and Representation），一种MoT架构，使未来视觉状态预测与动作预测并行提供独立互补的学习信号，从而在无动作标注的人类自我中心视频上学习机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作标注数据稀缺，而人类自我中心视频规模庞大但缺乏动作监督，如何利用这类无动作视频进行可扩展的机器人策略学习。

Method: 在预训练VLM之上构建Mixture-of-Transformers架构：共享表征下设置动作预测头与未来视觉状态预测头两个目标，视觉预测作为独立且互补的自监督信号；并系统分析目标函数选择与数据筛选（curation）策略的影响。

Result: 从自我中心视频学到的表征在分布外（OOD）机器人任务上提升下游性能，且随视频数据规模增大收益进一步提升；给出目标设计与数据整理的经验性洞察。

Conclusion: 视觉状态预测与动作预测的联合建模是有效利用无动作视频的途径，为可扩展的机器人学习提供了目标设计与数据整理方面的指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate how human egocentric videos can benefit robot policy learning without action supervision. Building on a pretrained VLM, we propose Shared Prediction on Action and Representation (SPAR), an MoT architecture in which future visual state prediction can provide independent and complementary learning signals alongside action prediction. We find that representations learned from egocentric videos improve downstream performance on out-of-distribution robot tasks, with further gains as video data scales. We also study the effects of objective choices and data curation, providing insights into how to leverage action-free videos for scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [167] [Joint-State Inference for Autonomous Excavators Using Cab-View Vision-Control Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13690)
*Junwei Li,Jian Pan,Mengchi Cai,Shuai Zhao,Xizhuo Jiang,Zheng Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"少传感器"闭环自主挖掘框架：用车载驾驶室视角图像+摇杆指令时序信息连续推断挖掘机关节状态（含遮挡下的铲斗滚动状态缓冲与机械关节行程约束），并接入ACT策略在线生成动作，仿真20次试验成功率95%、单步延迟10.11ms、铲斗角MAE 0.965°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有挖掘机前端机构状态反馈依赖加装关节传感器、外部相机或视觉标记，改造成本与维护负担高；自主挖掘需要可靠的状态反馈而又希望降低对外部传感基础设施的依赖。

Method: (1) 从当前驾驶室视角图像编码结构线索；(2) 以历史摇杆指令建模短期运动趋势（控制相关时序先验）；(3) 为铲斗关节引入滚动状态缓冲以在严重遮挡下保持时序连续性；(4) 将机械关节行程约束并入学习目标以抑制不可行估计；(5) 推断状态与当前图像输入基于ACT（Action Chunking with Transformers）的控制策略做在线动作生成。

Result: AGX Dynamics for Unity 仿真中多工况实验：20次自主挖掘试验任务完成率95%，关节状态推断+控制平均单步延迟10.11 ms，铲斗关节推断MAE 0.965°。

Conclusion: 验证了一种轻传感、计算高效的关节状态推断方法可支撑闭环自主挖掘，降低对额外传感设施的依赖；但验证仅限仿真，未见实机部署证据，且属特定工程机械场景而非通用具身智能方法。

Abstract: Reliable state feedback of excavator front-end mechanisms is essential for autonomous excavation, yet retrofitting existing machines with dedicated joint sensors, external cameras, or visual markers can increase deployment and maintenance burdens. This paper proposes a closed-loop autonomous excavation framework for continuous inference of excavator joint states using onboard visual observations and control-related temporal information. The framework encodes structural cues from the current cab-view image, models short-term motion trends from historical joystick commands, and introduces a rolling state buffer for the bucket joint to preserve temporal continuity under severe occlusion. Mechanical joint-range constraints are incorporated into the learning objective to suppress infeasible estimates. The current cab-view image and inferred states are supplied to an Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT)-based control policy for online action generation. Experiments in an AGX Dynamics for Unity simulation environment under diverse operating conditions evaluate joint-state inference and end-to-end task execution. Across 20 autonomous excavation trials, the framework achieved a 95% task-completion rate, with an average joint-state-inference-and-control latency of 10.11 ms per step. The bucket-joint inference achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.965 degrees. These results demonstrate a sensor-light and computationally efficient joint-state-inference approach that can support closed-loop autonomous excavation with reduced dependence on additional sensing infrastructure.

</details>


### [168] [CIALLO: Learning Shared Intent Representations for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13732)
*Choo Sin Wai,Dake Zhong,Jingye Zhang,Zhifang Liu,Siyao Xiao,Zirong Ding,Yuhong Zhang,Kangjie Chen,Dongbin Zhang,Guorui Song,Guocun Wang,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CIALLO: a shared "behavioral intent" action codec aligning actions, visual changes and language; embodiment-conditioned decoder plus 10s-calibration enables cross-embodiment transfer; Qwen-CIALLO reaches 76.7% real-robot success vs 70.0% for finetuned pi0.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示蕴含可复用的操作知识，但跨本体迁移需要能桥接不同控制接口的表示。单纯的动作重建损失并不保证不同机器人的相似行为共享同一表示，因此需要一个显式对齐的共享动作空间。

Method: 提出 CIALLO 动作编解码器：在短时域行为意图空间中联合对齐动作、视觉变化与观测接地的语言；本体条件化解码器将意图映射回本体特定动作。八本体预训练后，仅更新本体嵌入、用≤10秒单条录制完成新机器人标定并冻结编解码器；策略（Qwen3.5-4B，骨干无需大规模机器人动作预训练）从观测与指令预测意图。仿真中额外微调解码器。

Result: 真实 SO-101 与单臂 Piper 上每任务20次演示，平均成功率76.7%（全量微调π0为70.0%）；仿真 LIBERO 97.3%、RoboTwin 2.0 52.7%；LIBERO 策略零样本迁移 LIBERO-Plus 达80.8%。

Conclusion: 共享动作编解码器可作为可迁移的控制接口，将本体标定与任务学习解耦，为新机器人提供低成本适配路径。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations contain reusable manipulation knowledge, but transferring this knowledge across embodiments requires representations that bridge differences in control interfaces. Action reconstruction alone does not ensure that similar behaviors across robots share a representation. To address this gap, we introduce CIALLO, an action codec that aligns actions, visual changes, and observation-grounded language in a shared space of short-horizon behavioral intent. An embodiment-conditioned decoder maps this intent back to robot-specific actions, allowing a shared representation to support different control interfaces. After pretraining on eight embodiments, CIALLO adapts to a new robot from one recording of at most 10 seconds by updating only its embodiment embeddings. The calibrated codec then remains frozen while a policy learns to predict intent from observations and instructions. We instantiate this policy with Qwen3.5-4B to form Qwen-CIALLO, whose backbone requires no large-scale robot-action pretraining before policy finetuning. With 20 demonstrations per task, Qwen-CIALLO achieves 76.7\% average success across real SO-101 and single-arm Piper robots, compared with 70.0\% for fully finetuned $\pi_0$. In simulation, we additionally finetune the action decoder, achieving 97.3\% success on LIBERO and 52.7\% on RoboTwin 2.0. The LIBERO policy also achieves 80.8\% on LIBERO-Plus without further finetuning. Together, these results support a shared action codec as a transferable control interface that separates embodiment calibration from task learning.

</details>


### [169] [FAME-QP: A GPU-Accelerated Quadratic-Programming Solver for Real-Time Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13766)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Debdipta Goswami,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME-QP：用算子库缓存+融合内核+CUDA Graph 回放+设备标定的 GPU QP 求解器，服务线性 MPC，降低延迟且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性模型预测控制中的稠密 QP 求解是实时控制回路的瓶颈，现有 GPU QP 求解器未充分结合约束结构、内核融合与执行配置自动化，难以在固定控制周期内稳定达到低延迟。

Method: 提出 FAME-QP：约束相关（constraint-dependent）算子表示；融合的单/双 kernel 迭代并通过 CUDA graphs 重放以消除启动开销；通过设备标定与问题级计时自动选择执行配置；用缓存的算子库（operator bank）探测选择初始 penalty，并在问题矩阵变化时惰性更新算子。

Result: 数值基准测试与仿真人形/四旋翼控制实验中，相比同类 GPU 求解器降低求解延迟，同时保持解精度与控制性能。

Conclusion: 结构化算子复用+执行流水固化+自动配置选择能有效提升 GPU 上稠密 QP 的实时求解效率，适用于高频 MPC；但实验限于仿真，缺少真实硬件部署验证。

Abstract: We present FAME-QP, a GPU-accelerated solver for dense quadratic programs arising in linear model predictive control. FAME-QP combines constraint-dependent operator representations with fused single- and two-kernel iterations replayed through CUDA graphs, automatically selecting the execution configuration through device calibration and problem-specific timing. It probes a cached operator bank to select the initial penalty and updates operators lazily when problem matrices change. Numerical benchmarks and simulated humanoid and quadrotor control experiments demonstrate reduced latency relative to competing GPU solvers while maintaining solution accuracy and control performance.

</details>


### [170] [From Depth to Safe Depth: Uncertainty-Aware Monocular Perception for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13769)
*Saeid Firouzi Daghigh,Saeed Ayat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用一个仅0.58M参数的插件头在冻结的单目深度模型上预测逐像素log深度修正μ与不确定度σ，训练用β加权高斯负对数似然整定，导航栈消费单侧下界d·e^(μ−kσ)（k由共形校准确定），并使不确定度决定哪些像素可视为地面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动机器人上以单目RGB相机替代深度传感器很有诱惑力，但现成深度基础模型（如Depth Anything V2 metric）系统性高估近处障碍距离，直接驱动Nav2在仿真室内导航120个episode中碰撞率达63%，暴露了"开箱即用"的不安全性。

Method: 1) 冻结深度基础模型，仅训练0.58M参数的轻量头，逐像素输出log深度修正μ和不确定度σ；2) 损失为β加权高斯负对数似然；3) 导航使用保守单侧界d_safe = d·e^(μ−kσ)，k由在留出世界上的共形校准确定；4) 用σ判定哪些像素可作为可通行地面；5) 仿真室内导航基准（Nav2栈）对比真值深度与恒定安全边距基线。

Result: 深度误差减半（AbsRel 0.262→0.134），近距高估比例从59.8%降至12.0%；导航成功率由37%升至70%，碰撞率由63%降至3%（接近真值深度的2%）；同像素覆盖率的恒定安全边距基线成功率仅30%（因使机器人冻结），不确定性感知方法在56个不一致配对episode中赢下52个；在玻璃、无纹理墙面和暗区上增益仍成立。

Conclusion: 开箱即用的单目深度基础模型用于机器人导航不安全；通过共形校准的不确定性感知单侧安全深度界，可低成本地把深度预测转为可安全消费的几何量，且优于固定安全边距这种"冻结式"保守策略。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models make it tempting to replace depth sensors on mobile robots with a single RGB camera. We show that, used off the shelf, this is unsafe: in a simulated indoor navigation benchmark, a standard Nav2 stack driven by Depth Anything V2 (metric) collides in 63\% of 120 episodes, because the model systematically over-estimates the distance to nearby obstacles. We propose a lightweight plug-in module that turns predicted depth into \emph{safe depth}. A 0.58\,M-parameter head on top of the \emph{frozen} depth model predicts, per pixel, a log-depth correction $\mu$ and an uncertainty $\sigma$, trained with a $\beta$-weighted Gaussian negative log-likelihood. The navigation stack consumes the one-sided bound $\hat d_{\text{safe}}=d\,e^{\mu-k\sigma}$, where $k$ is set by conformal calibration on a held-out world, and uncertainty also decides which pixels may be treated as floor. The correction halves the depth error (AbsRel $0.262\rightarrow0.134$) and reduces near-range over-estimation from 59.8\% to 12.0\%. In navigation, safe depth raises the success rate from 37\% to 70\% and lowers collisions from 63\% to 3\%, close to the 2\% of ground-truth depth. A constant safety margin calibrated to the same pixel coverage reaches only 30\% success because it freezes the robot; uncertainty-aware safety wins 52 of 56 discordant paired episodes against it. Gains hold on glass, texture-less walls and dark regions.

</details>


### [171] [Softness is something you also need! Why compliance helps control: information metrics of a passive walker with compliant ankle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13779)
*Fabio Bonsignorio,Elisabetta Barletta,Sorin Dragomir,Francesco Esposito,Enrica Zereik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇技术札记，回顾信息度量与动力学关联的数学结果，并给出倒立摆与被动动态行走模型的相空间体积计算方法，用状态依赖蒙特卡洛与RKHS方法估计可达相空间体积 W(γ)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为机器人（刚性与软体）与生物在适应性、柔顺性与灵巧性上存在鸿沟，根源在于"笛卡尔式"自上而下控制与心身分离的设计范式；希望以形态计算、涌现行为等生物学原则重构建模与控制方法，并量化"物理智能"。

Method: 综述性整合：回顾将信息度量与智能体动力学联系起来的经典数学结果，结合作者及社区的近期方法；技术部分从McGeer罗盘步态出发，逐步引入膝-踝柔顺、足部摇杆效应与Ruina式分布腿部惯量罗盘步态，推导标准足跟触地映射，并以状态依赖蒙特卡洛和再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）方法估计可行相空间体积 W(γ)。

Result: 给出了若干被动动态行走变体模型的相空间体积计算流程与结果，并表明基于RKHS的方法能够提供更深入、更细致的分析；全文以技术札记形式呈现，主要结论偏方法论层面。

Conclusion: 相空间体积估计可作为衡量柔顺/欠驱动行走智能体可行性与鲁棒性的定量工具；作者指出未来方向是面向完全或部分柔顺人工体的可落地建模与控制方法，以及对自然物理智能的更深入理解。

Abstract: A great gap in adaptivity, flexibility, and dexterity still exists between intelligent robots, whether they are made of rigid or (partially) compliant components, and their naturally evolved animal and vegetal counterparts. One reason could be of `paradigm' nature. On the one hand, most robots (both rigid and soft) are still designed by following a `Cartesian' approach, with top-down control and a clear mind-body separation. Designing artificial agents on the basis of the principles that we observe in biology is not an easy task. The principles of organization of animals and plants rely on morphological computation and emergence of fit behaviors from loosely coupled networks of embodied agents. Modeling and control require radically new approaches. In this paper we briefly recall and explain some seminal mathematical results linking information metrics and dynamics of the agents, already published years ago; we integrate them with some recent methods developed by us and by the community, we highlight some brief remarks about their relevance and we hint to our current and near future work towards finding concrete and efficient ways for the modeling and control of completely or partially compliant artificial agents and for a better understanding of physical intelligence in nature. We present a technical note summarizing phase-space volume calculations for inverted-pendulum and passive-dynamic walking models, following our proposed framework. Starting from the McGeer compass gait, we introduce knee-ankle compliance, foot-rocker effects, and a Ruina-style compass gait with distributed leg inertia. Standard heel-strike maps are derived and used in the estimation of viable phase-space volumes $W(\gamma)$, based on a state-dependent Monte-Carlo and Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces methods. We show how the methods based on RKHS allow deeper and more subtle analysis.

</details>


### [172] [RiSE-Drive: Risk-Sensitive Long-Horizon Candidate Evaluation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13790)
*Yu Zhao,Hongyi Duan,Ming Yin,Hongyuan Zhang,Fan Yang,Hongzhi Wang,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.RO
