TL;DR: RiSE-Drive 将端到端自动驾驶的轨迹候选打分问题重构为“候选条件潜在未来滚动 + 长时域结果分布估计 + 尾部风险感知与扰动鲁棒的排序准则”的分布化风险评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶普遍采用“生成多候选轨迹再择优”的范式，真正的瓶颈已从候选生成转向在不确定性下评估候选的长时域后果。现有方法仍依赖确定性点分数、启发式子分或单次结果评估，难以刻画延迟失效、尾部风险以及在合理未来扰动下的排序不稳定。

Method: RiSE-Drive 包含三步：先做以候选轨迹为条件的潜在未来滚动（candidate-conditioned latent future rollout）；再估计长时域结果分布而非单一标量分数；最后采用尾部风险感知（tail-risk-aware）且对扰动鲁棒的决策准则对候选进行排序，从而把确定性“oracle 式”打分转为带显式风险控制的分布化结果评估。

Result: 在 NAVSIM-v1、NAVSIM-v2 以及闭环 HUGSIM 基准上取得 SOTA 性能，并显著提升候选选择的鲁棒性；摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Conclusion: 候选评估应从点估计转向分布化结果评估与显式风险控制，使评估器偏好不仅在名义条件下表现强、且在长时域滚动与安全关键偏差下依然可靠的候选；该思想可推广到其他“生成—择优”型端到端系统。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems increasingly rely on generating multiple trajectory candidates and selecting one for execution. In this setting, a central challenge is no longer only candidate generation, but how to evaluate candidates according to their long-horizon consequences under uncertainty. Existing methods still mainly use deterministic point scores, heuristic subscores, or single-outcome evaluations, which makes it difficult to capture delayed failures, tail risk, and ranking instability under plausible future perturbations. We present RiSE-Drive, a framework for risk-sensitive long-horizon candidate evaluation in end-to-end autonomous driving. RiSE-Drive first performs candidate-conditioned latent future rollout, then estimates long-horizon outcome distributions instead of single scalar scores, and finally ranks candidates using a tail-risk-aware and perturbation-robust decision criterion. This reformulates candidate scoring from deterministic oracle-style evaluation into distributional outcome assessment with explicit risk control. As a result, the evaluator is encouraged to prefer candidates that are not only strong under nominal conditions, but also reliable under long-horizon rollout and safety-critical deviations. Experiments on the NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks, as well as the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark, show that RiSE-Drive achieves state-of-the-art performance and significantly improves the robustness of candidate selection.

</details>


### [173] [Camera equalisation of an 11.5x drop in environmental illuminance: four imitation-learning policies on an SO-101 arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13810)
*Simon Cowling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在低成本 SO-101 机械臂上训练四个模仿学习策略（ACT、SmolVLA、Diffusion Policy、pi0.5），将照度从约 520 lux 降至约 45 lux，成功率几乎不变（20/40→19/40），但图像亮度仅下降 3%/19%，说明自动曝光与白平衡吸收了大部差异，房间照度并不等于策略所看到的图像光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实机器人视觉运动策略基准通常只用房间照度（lux）描述光照条件，作者怀疑在该类低成本平台上，房间光照与相机图像光照几乎无关，因此仅报告 lux 不足以刻画策略实际面对的视觉分布。

Method: 在同一 50 条抓取放置演示上训练四种模仿学习策略（ACT、SmolVLA、Diffusion Policy、pi0.5），每种在约 520 lux 与约 45 lux（11.5x 降幅）各执行 10 次 rollout，共 40 次/策略；统计成功率差异与策略间敏感性交互效应，并测量前后腕部相机的图像亮度变化；相机保持自动曝光与自动白平衡，曝光未锁定；另外记录一次意外的重复实验块以核验可复现性。

Result: 正常光下成功 20/40，低光下 19/40，变化 -2.5 个百分点（95% CI -23 至 +19），策略间敏感性无显著差异（交互 p = 0.86）；图像平均亮度低光下前相机仅低 3%、腕部相机低 19%；重复的 10 次 rollout 块成功率复现（7/10），但仅 6/10 的放置位置一致，与随机水平相符。

Conclusion: 仅用 lux 无法描述策略真正看到的视觉输入；应报告并控制相机设置（曝光锁定等）以及相对于训练录像的图像统计量。数据集与代码公开。该工作本质上是指标/评测规范层面的批判性观察，方法学价值在于提醒视觉运动策略基准的可比性缺陷，但缺乏新方法或性能突破。

Abstract: Real-robot benchmarks of learned visuomotor policies often record room illuminance as the lighting condition. We show that, on a typical low-cost rig, room light and image light can be almost unrelated. Four imitation-learning policies (ACT, SmolVLA, Diffusion Policy and pi0.5) were trained on the same 50 demonstrations of a pick-and-place task on an SO-101 arm. Each was run for ten rollouts at about 520 lux and again at about 45 lux, an 11.5x reduction. Success was 20/40 in normal light and 19/40 in low light: a change of -2.5 percentage points (95% CI -23 to +19), with no evidence that the policies differed in sensitivity (interaction p = 0.86). The recorded video shows why: mean image brightness was only 3% lower in low light on the front camera and 19% lower on the wrist camera. Both cameras were set to automatic exposure and white balance, which most likely absorbed the difference; exposure was not locked in this experiment. An accidental repeat of one 10-rollout block reproduced its success count (7/10) but matched the first block at only 6 of 10 placements, consistent with chance. We conclude that lux alone does not describe what a policy sees. Camera settings, and image statistics compared with the training recordings, are the quantities to report and control. Data and code are public.

</details>


### [174] [WAM is capable in BEHAVIOR-1K: the 2nd-place solution to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13818)
*Yelin He,Jiaquan Ye,Xinming Wei,Wenjie Xiao,Gengming Zhang,Guoting Wei,Xianbiao Qi,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cosmos 3 世界-动作模型 + 视觉历史/任务进度条件 + 任务组与技能级 LoRA 适配器 + 进度估计与路由推理，获 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge 第二名（Q-score 0.613，全任务成功率 40.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEHAVIOR 挑战需在 100 项家务任务上做移动操作，要求 ≥1 FPS 实时推理、无闭源模型、不用 RSI 与 agentic harness，需在有限算力下兼顾多任务泛化与部署效率。

Method: 以 Cosmos 3 世界动作模型为骨干：训练端引入更长视觉历史、任务进度条件、任务组与技能特定 LoRA 适配器；推理端用独立进度估计器提供进度上下文，按任务与进度条件化路由选择策略，并叠加动作候选聚合、rolling inpainting、任务特定时序与夹爪控制。

Result: 自评测 3 轮平均 Q-score 0.613、全任务成功率 40.9%；官方私有测试集每任务 20 实例下 Q-score 0.615、成功率 41.2%；100 端口并发、受限硬件下平均端到端 >2 FPS/端口。

Conclusion: 工程化模块化改造（进度条件、MoE 式 LoRA 路由、候选聚合）可在严格实时与开源约束下显著提升世界-动作模型的移动操作表现，验证 WAM 路线的可部署性。

Abstract: This work presents the 2nd-place solution (Team IF1001) to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge, which evaluates mobile manipulation across 100 household tasks. Our method is built on a powerful world action model (WAM), Cosmos 3. We improve Cosmos 3 by using richer visual history, task-progress conditioning, and task-group and skill-specific LoRA adapters. During inference, we apply a separate progress estimator to supply task-progress context, and use task- and progress-conditioned routing to select a task-group or skill-specific policy, complemented by action-candidate aggregation, rolling inpainting, and task-specific temporal and gripper control. To meet the minimum inference requirement of 1 FPS, we use neither recursive self-improvement (RSI) nor agentic harness methods and rely on no closed-source models; during official private-test evaluation, our system handled concurrent requests across 100 evaluation ports with limited hardware resources, sustaining an average end-to-end inference rate above 2 FPS per port. Our method achieves a mean Q-score of 0.613 and a full-task success rate of 40.9%, averaged over 3 self-evaluation rounds across all 100 tasks. On the official private test set, with 20 instances per task, our system achieves a mean Q-score of 0.615 and a full-task success rate of 41.2%.

</details>


### [175] [Game-Enhanced Embodied Learning with Event and Foresight Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13819)
*Lianlei Shan,Han Jin,Yaunchao Ma,Xinpeng Chen,Linhao Zhou,Xin Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InternVLA-A1.5 用 394 段、20 款游戏的人类键鼠数据做"事件预测+历史条件视觉前瞻"的双目标预训练，为机器人策略提供初始化；LIBERO 四套件总成功率从 85.90% 提升到 90.55%（+4.65pp），但任务级结果异质，且归因不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类游玩数据天然提供"观测—动作—视觉后果"配对，可低成本补充机器人策略训练所需的数据规模与多样性；作者希望验证完整游戏预训练阶段能否为 VLA 策略带来可迁移的初始化收益。

Method: 构建 20 款游戏、394 段录制、共 223.36 小时时间跨度的人工游玩语料；采用保留覆盖率的锚点选择构造 1 秒长度目标片段；预训练联合两个目标——(1) 键盘/鼠标事件预测，(2) 以历史为条件、经冻结视频模型提供的视觉前瞻监督；并用注意力掩码阻止 teacher-forcing 的未来动作泄漏进前瞻查询。经 12,400 次游戏预训练更新后，在 LIBERO 四个套件上以统一的机器人微调协议与公开基座对比（共 2,072,084 个训练锚点）。

Result: LIBERO 汇总成功率由 1,718/2,000（85.90%）升至 1,811/2,000（90.55%），增益 4.65pp；分套件增益分别为 Spatial +9.2、Object +6.8、Goal +0.2、Long +2.4。40 个任务中 24 个提升、10 个下降、6 个持平。

Conclusion: 结果支持"完整游戏预训练阶段"在该评测设定下的实践价值，同时暴露了任务级效果的异质性。作者明确指出对比包含额外优化步数，未隔离游戏数据、两个目标各自贡献及训练随机性，故该增益不能归因于单一因素；后续需消融与多随机种子验证。

Abstract: Human gameplay supplies paired observations, actions, and visual consequences that can supplement robot-policy training. We study event-and-foresight pretraining for InternVLA-A1.5, combining recorded keyboard/mouse event prediction with history-conditioned visual supervision through a frozen video model. Coverage-preserving anchor selection constructs one-second targets, and an attention mask prevents teacher-forced future actions from entering foresight queries. The selected corpus contains 394 recordings across 20 games, with 223.36 hours of recorded timestamp span and 2,072,084 training anchors. After 12,400 game-pretraining updates, we compare the resulting initialization with the public base under the same suite-specific robot fine-tuning protocol. Across four LIBERO suites, aggregate success increases from 1,718/2,000 (85.90%) to 1,811/2,000 (90.55%), a gain of 4.65 percentage points. Suite gains are 9.2, 6.8, 0.2, and 2.4 points on Spatial, Object, Goal, and Long. The 40-task breakdown shows 24 improvements, 10 declines, and six ties. These results support the practical value of the complete game-pretraining stage in this evaluated setting, while revealing heterogeneous task outcomes. The comparison includes additional optimization and does not isolate the effects of game data, the two objectives, or training variability.

</details>


### [176] [Learn-To-Propagate: Distributionally Robust Motion Planning for Nonlinear Uncertain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13863)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Adrien Banse,Qi Heng Ho,Raphael Jungers,Morteza Lahijanian,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: L2P-DR-Forest: a distributionally-robust, sampling-based motion planning algorithm for nonlinear stochastic systems with unknown initial-state and process-noise distributions, using optimal-transport ambiguity sets propagated through dynamics.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing stochastic motion planning either assumes linear dynamics with known distributions or lacks formal correctness guarantees; the goal is to plan with high-probability safety under unknown nonlinear stochasticity.

Method: Use optimal transport to learn ambiguity sets containing true distributions with high confidence; develop efficient propagation of these sets through nonlinear dynamics plus safety verification; integrate into a sampling-based planner that grows a forest of trees while mitigating ambiguity-set radius inflation.

Result: Proves correctness (every returned plan satisfies specifications at prescribed confidence) and, under feasibility/distributional assumptions, probabilistic completeness; robotic benchmarks show significant improvement over existing methods with formal high-confidence guarantees.

Conclusion: Distributionally-robust optimal-transport ambiguity sets can be embedded in sampling-based planners to yield guaranteed, scalable stochastic motion planning for nonlinear systems with unknown noise.

Abstract: Motion planning for stochastic systems aims to find a sequence of control inputs that drives a system to a goal set while avoiding unsafe states with high probability. Existing approaches, however, are either restricted to linear dynamics with perfect knowledge of the underlying distributions or lack formal correctness guarantees. To address these limitations, we propose \emph{Learn-To-Propagate Distributionally-Robust Forest (L2P-DR-Forest)}, a distributionally-robust sampling-based planning algorithm for \emph{nonlinear} stochastic systems with \emph{unknown} initial state and process noise distributions. Our approach leverages recent advances in optimal transport to learn ambiguity sets that contain the true system distributions with high confidence. We develop an efficient method for propagating these sets through the system dynamics and for verifying their safety. We then integrate these techniques into a novel sampling-based planning algorithm to grow a forest of search trees in the system's state space while accounting for and mitigating the growth of ambiguity-sets radii. We prove that the algorithm is correct, i.e., every returned plan satisfies the specifications with the prescribed confidence, and, under some feasibility and distributional assumptions, is probabilistically complete. Evaluations on several robotic benchmarks demonstrate that our algorithm significantly outperforms existing methods while providing formal guarantees with high confidence.

</details>


### [177] [ReACT: Reflection-Consistent Modulation and Cross-Load Consistency for Humanoid Velocity Tracking Under Asymmetric Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13929)
*Xianghui Wang,Aoran Fu,Liyou Luo,Shanze Wang,Xiaoyu Shen,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReACT：面向单侧上肢负载下速度跟踪的人形全身运动控制框架，用反射一致扰动调制和跨负载一致性训练减少速度跟踪偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在搬运物体时，单手持物带来持续不对称上身负载，导致速度指令跟踪退化；跟踪偏差累积成位置与朝向漂移，增加碰撞与导航失败风险。

Method: 提出ReACT框架：1）反射一致扰动调制——用估计的对外力/力矩（wrench）调制actor特征以补偿负载，并保证镜像观测下动作对应关系一致；2）跨负载跟踪一致性训练——在相同速度指令下对比带载与空载条件，促使不同负载条件下的速度跟踪表现一致。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人仿真中，不同不对称负载下速度跟踪改善、方向偏差减小；真机实验中，在固定无地图导航策略与单侧手持负载条件下，40次导航试验全部无碰撞到达目标。

Conclusion: 显式建模负载扰动的对称一致性与跨负载一致性训练，可有效缓解人形机器人搬运中的速度跟踪偏差，并在真机上支撑可靠的无地图导航。

Abstract: In recent years, humanoid loco-manipulation has progressed to whole-body grasping, lifting, and transport. A basic requirement underlying these tasks is carrying the object to its destination, which depends on the locomotion controller accurately executing velocity commands from the navigation policy. However, carrying an object in one hand introduces a persistent asymmetric upper-body load that can degrade velocity tracking. Persistent tracking biases can accumulate into position and heading deviations, increasing the risk of collisions and navigation failure. To address this problem, we present ReACT, a framework for accurate velocity tracking under asymmetric upper-body loads. Reflection-consistent disturbance modulation uses the estimated wrench to modulate actor features for load compensation while preserving action correspondence under reflected observations. Cross-load tracking consistency training compares loaded and unloaded conditions under the same velocity commands to promote consistent velocity tracking across loading conditions. Simulation experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot show improved velocity tracking and reduced directional bias under varying asymmetric loads. Real-world experiments demonstrate collision-free goal reaching in all 40 tested navigation trials under unilateral hand loads with a fixed mapless navigation policy.

</details>


### [178] [Optimization-Based Path Planning for Industrial Manipulators: Experimental Validation in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13983)
*Shiva Ganesh Murali,Valentin Bhend,Deepak Ingole,Gabriele Fadini,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出离线优化式机器人增材制造联合路径规划器，将关节路径规整性、自碰撞惩罚与工艺成本/约束统一，支持全长与分块耦合优化及迭代权重调节(IWT)，在碳纤维缠绕与机器人3D打印上于UR10e实机执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人增材制造需要同时满足机器人可行性与工艺几何要求的联合路径，而现有方法难以将新工艺以统一形式纳入优化框架。

Method: 基于优化的离线联合路径规划：以工艺参考轨迹、残差与容差形式接入新工艺；结合关节路径规整性与自碰撞惩罚；针对不同长度参考提供全路径优化与边界耦合的分块优化；用迭代权重调节(IWT)依据KPI与目标差距确定代价权重。

Result: 在碳纤维缠绕与机器人3D打印两项任务中，规划路径满足几何容差与全部KPI目标；均在UR10e上执行（打印不挤出），并记录了关节级跟踪数据。

Conclusion: 该框架为多工艺机器人增材制造提供了可复用的联合路径规划方案，但打印任务未实际挤出，验证偏于运动学层面，工艺稳健性尚待验证。

Abstract: Robotic additive manufacturing requires joint paths that reconcile robot feasibility with process-specific geometry. We develop an offline optimization-based path planner that combines joint-path regularity and self-collision penalties with process costs and constraints, so that a new process enters through its own reference, residuals, and tolerances. Full-path and boundary-coupled chunked optimization handle references of different lengths. Iterative Weight Tuning (IWT) determines the cost weights from the gaps between Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) and their targets. In carbon-fiber winding and robotic 3D printing, the planned paths meet the geometric tolerances and all KPI targets, and both paths are executed on a UR10e, without extrusion for printing, with joint-level tracking recorded during execution.

</details>


### [179] [LaST0.5: A Generalist World Action Model with Interaction-Centric Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14048)
*Xuheng Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Jialin Gao,Hao Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ji Li,Runzhong Huang,Yinxi Wang,Chenyang Gu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Zhonghao Yan,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Peng Jia,Pheng-Ann Heng,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaST0.5 提出以交互为中心物理表征作为世界建模与动作生成之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM（视频优先动作预测、联合视频-动作建模）直接将世界模型的全局物理表征迁移到动作生成，但全局场景表征会弱化机器人-物体局部交互，造成"世界演化优化目标"与"操作任务需求"之间的表征错配。

Method: 在视觉交互线索（机器人与环境交互区域）监督下学习紧凑的交互中心隐空间，并将其实例化为 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构中的"隐专家"，桥接世界专家与动作专家；同时联合预测未来表征与进度值（progress），利用进度一致性自适应调整动作执行并触发未来重采样，实现自适应 world-to-action 迁移；在超 14k 小时机器人数据语料上预训练。

Result: LIBERO 达 99.5%，在 10 个 RLBench 任务上超越此前 SOTA；真实世界实验展现稳健长时序执行与精细灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: 将"交互中心物理推理"确立为可扩展且有效的 WAM 范式，缓解全局表征与操作需求间的错配问题。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, endowing policies with strong capabilities for modeling temporal and physical dynamics. Existing WAMs, encompassing both video-first action prediction and joint video-action modeling, directly transfer global physical representations from world models to action generation. However, global scene representations may underemphasize local robot-object interactions, creating a mismatch between representations optimized for world evolution and those required for manipulation. To bridge this gap, we propose LaST0.5, a novel WAM that introduces an interaction-centric physical representation between world modeling and action generation. Specifically, LaST0.5 learns a compact interaction-centric latent space under the supervision of visual interaction cues between the robot and the environment. This reasoning space is instantiated as a latent expert that bridges the world expert and the action expert within a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. Furthermore, LaST0.5 performs adaptive world-to-action transfer by jointly predicting future representations and progress values, using progress consistency to adjust action execution and trigger future resampling. Finally, we pretrain LaST0.5 on a large-scale robotic data corpus comprising over 14k hours of data, establishing interaction-centric physical reasoning as a scalable and effective paradigm. LaST0.5 achieves 99.5% on LIBERO, surpasses the previous state of the art on ten evaluated RLBench tasks, and demonstrates robust long-horizon execution and precise dexterous manipulation in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [180] [RedirectMimic: Local Motion Content Redaction for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14049)
*Zepeng Wang,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedirectMimic 通过解耦"actor 观察的参考动作"与"训练执行的目标动作"，用规则生成的编辑参考引导 PPO 跟踪奖励，在观测/动作接口不变的前提下实现人形机器人局部动作替换（redaction）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动接口需在保持可用参考行为的同时满足应用特定的运动约束（如局部内容替换/遮蔽）。当控制器持续接收原始参考时，若仅对该部分动作去除跟踪激励，替换目标变得未定义，现有 reward zeroing 与 anti-tracking 效果不佳。

Method: 提出 RedirectMimic：分离 actor 观测的参考与训练执行目标。用规则生成的编辑参考在 PPO 中驱动跟踪奖励，而部署时的观测与动作接口保持不变；基于在 7,509 段运动片段上预训练的 tracker，在 Unitree G1 仿真中评估基于姿态与速度定义的两种手臂编辑任务。

Result: 举臂任务中核心窗口内容出现率降至 2.7%（reward zeroing 58.9%，anti-tracking 32.8%），核心窗口关节成功率 94.2%（兼顾内容抑制、无跌倒与局部保持）；速度定义任务中三种方法快速外摆率均很低。两任务中 RedirectMimic 替换误差最低，且未编辑运动跟踪精度与预训练控制器相当。

Conclusion: 解耦观测参考与训练目标可在不改动部署接口的情况下实现有效且局部的运动重定向，为人形运动接口提供可行方案；但验证仅限仿真与手臂编辑，速度定义任务区分度有限。

Abstract: Humanoid motion interfaces should accommodate application-specific movement constraints while retaining useful reference behavior. Local content redaction requires a controller to replace selected movements even when it continues to receive the original reference. Removing the tracking incentive for those movements leaves their replacement unspecified. We introduce RedirectMimic, which separates the reference observed by the actor from the target used to train its execution. A rule-generated edited reference guides tracking rewards during proximal policy optimization, while the deployed observation and action interfaces remain unchanged. We evaluate pose- and speed-defined arm editing in Unitree G1 simulation using a tracker pretrained on 7,509 motion clips. On the overhead-arm task, RedirectMimic reduces core-window content occurrence to 2.7%, compared with 58.9% for reward zeroing and 32.8% for anti-tracking. It achieves 94.2% core-window joint success, combining content suppression, fall-free execution, and local retention. On the speed-defined task, fast outward arm movements occurred at similarly low rates under all three local editing methods. Across both tasks, RedirectMimic achieved the lowest replacement error among these methods and tracked unedited motions with accuracy comparable to that of the pretrained controller. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [A human-in-the-loop Control Paradigm for Construction Robotics: From VR-Guided Teleoperation to Semi-Autonomous Dual-Arm Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14062)
*Jinhua Chen,Xiaoji Xu,Zhongyuan Liao,Huang Zhenmin,Zhang Ye,Changying Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人机协同控制范式，在VR遥操作、半自主共享控制与规则式自主执行之间结构化切换，用于双臂接触密集型施工任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险非结构化环境中的施工任务既需要人的适应性又需要机器人精度，但现有方法将遥操作与自主性割裂为独立控制模式，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 融合沉浸式VR引导、人工定义的可复用技能原语库、带显式人机角色分配与指令优先级规则的共享自主框架；在双臂平台上执行立面安装任务进行验证。

Result: 标定良好的名义条件下自主执行性能最佳；当环境扰动超出预设自主流程纠偏能力时半自主控制韧性更好；半自主相比全遥操作显著降低操作员主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: 有效的施工机器人控制应按任务与环境不确定性自适应调节人的参与程度：重复性操作交给自主，扰动恢复使用共享控制。

Abstract: Construction tasks in hazardous and unstructured environments require both human adaptability and robotic precision, yet existing approaches often treat teleoperation and autonomy as separate control modes. This paper presents a human-in-the-loop control paradigm that enables structured transitions among VR-guided teleoperation, semi-autonomous shared control, and rule-based autonomous execution for contact-rich dual-arm construction tasks. The proposed system combines immersive VR guidance, a manually defined library of reusable skill primitives, and a shared-autonomy framework with explicit human-robot role allocation and command-priority rules. This integration allows operators to retain supervisory and corrective authority while delegating repetitive and precision-critical actions to the robot. The paradigm is validated on a dual-arm platform through representative facade-installation tasks. Results show that autonomous execution provides the best performance under well-calibrated nominal conditions, whereas semi-autonomous control improves resilience when environmental disturbances exceed the predefined autonomous pipeline's correction capability. Semi-autonomous control also substantially reduces subjective operator workload compared with full teleoperation. These findings demonstrate that effective construction-robot control should adapt human involvement to task and environmental uncertainty, using autonomy for repeatable operations and shared control for disturbance recovery.

</details>


### [182] [DexSeed: Expanding Human Hand-Object Interactions into Robot Demonstrations for Dexterous Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14092)
*Huaijin Pi,Zehong Shen,Yong Wang,Kechun Xu,Changpeng Wang,Dingkun Wei,Xueyang Wang,Wenjia Wang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSeed：以人-物交互序列为源，通过InteractionMesh重定向+手臂轨迹增强+RL手指策略在物理仿真中生成多样且可执行的灵巧机器人示教，用于微调VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧VLA学习需要多样、物理可执行的演示，但遥操作采集昂贵；人-物数据集虽可复用，现有迁移方法仅复现给定交互，缺乏对象/操作位置覆盖以及可执行的动作与视觉观测。

Method: 按抓取模式与共享语言描述组织源序列；用InteractionMesh重定向与手臂轨迹增强，将序列适配到机器人手、不同物体几何与抓放位置，同时保持局部手-物关系；RL手指控制策略生成可执行rollout，手臂跟踪预计算轨迹；将成功rollout的渲染观测与记录动作配以抓取描述，微调VLA。

Result: 重定向的接触保真度优于基线，整臂执行更可靠；仿真评测与真机部署表明，仅用生成示教微调的VLA能执行语言指定的抓取。

Conclusion: 人-手数据可通过“扩展序列+物理仿真落实执行”的低成本流水线转化为可扩展的灵巧VLA训练数据。

Abstract: We study how to generate diverse, physically executable robot demonstrations for dexterous vision-language-action (VLA) learning. Existing demonstration-generation methods typically rely on costly teleoperated examples. Meanwhile, human hand--object datasets provide a reusable source, but existing transfer methods mainly reproduce given interactions. Turning these interactions into VLA training data further requires coverage of different objects and manipulation locations, together with executable arm and finger actions, and visual observations. To achieve this, we present DexSeed, a framework for low-cost generation of dexterous robot data from human hand--object interactions. Our key idea is to expand human sequences while preserving their grasps and realizing the resulting motions under arm-tracking dynamics in physics simulation. Specifically, we organize source sequences by grasp mode with shared language descriptions. InteractionMesh-based retargeting and arm trajectory augmentation adapt these sequences to the robot hand, different object geometries, and pick-and-place locations while preserving local hand--object relationships. An RL finger-control policy produces executable rollouts, while the arm tracks precomputed trajectories. Rendered observations and recorded actions from successful rollouts are paired with grasp descriptions to fine-tune a VLA. Experiments show improved contact fidelity in retargeting and more reliable whole-arm execution than the evaluated baselines. Simulation evaluation and real-world deployment further demonstrate that a VLA fine-tuned solely on the generated demonstrations can execute language-specified grasps. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [183] [Model-Free Runtime Measurement of Task-Relevant Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14111)
*Dimitris Panagopoulos,Adolfo Perrusquía,Yang Xing,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Acquisition-State Telemetry (AST)：无模型架构，将原始观测经领域接口映射为任务解析坐标与归一化对比表示，由固定因果监控器实时输出"前瞻进度"与"信息摩擦"两路信号，用于判断信息获取过程本身是否运作正常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作能规划信息寻求动作、解释观测，却不监控信息采集过程本身是否有效，导致系统可能长期"运行正常"但任务需求始终未解决（无效停滞）。

Method: 模型无关架构：领域接口把观测映射到任务解析坐标与归一化比较表示；固定的因果监控器输出两路连续实时信号——(i) 未解决需求上的前瞻进度，(ii) 重复证据带来极小信息增益时产生的"信息摩擦"。同一套未调参配置跨八个领域测试；并在带提前终止约束的闭环强化学习中惩罚信息摩擦。

Result: 跨搜救对话、空间网格探索、分层能力获取、连续机器人操作、概率意图估计等八领域，摩擦通道能在任务完成前区分无产出停滞与有目的探索，且任务完成后保持静默；闭环RL中惩罚摩擦可维持高任务成功率，而未惩罚策略及等运行成本基线未能完成任务。

Conclusion: 对"信息采集过程健康度"的实时元监控是一种可迁移、免调参的有效信号，可作为进度/停滞判别与RL塑形项，弥补任务级监控的盲区。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly monitor physical kinematics, environmental states and task completion outcomes. While existing work provides methods to plan information seeking actions and interpret acquired observations, current approaches do not monitor whether the live process of information gathering is functioning properly. Consequently, such systems can remain operationally active without resolving outstanding mission requirements. To address this limitation, we introduce Acquisition-State Telemetry (AST), a model free architecture that evaluates ongoing information acquisition from observable evidence relative to declared task requirements. Specifically, a domain interface maps raw observations into task resolution coordinates and normalized comparative representations, which are processed by a fixed causal monitor. This monitor emits two continuous real time signals that quantify (i) prospective progress across unresolved requirements and (ii) informational friction generated when recurrent evidence yields negligible informational gain. We evaluated a single, unadjusted monitor configuration across eight diverse domains including search and rescue dialogue, spatial grid exploration, hierarchical capability acquisition, continuous robotic manipulation, and probabilistic intent estimation. We show that across these domains the friction channel distinguished unproductive stagnation from purposeful exploration prior to task completion, while remaining muted during post completion activity. Furthermore, in closed loop reinforcement learning experiments under early termination constraints, we demonstrate that penalizing informational friction allowed agents to sustain high mission success, whereas unpenalized policies and baselines with matched operational costs failed to complete required tasks.

</details>


### [184] [LEASH: Stay Safe in Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14115)
*Qinfeng Zhu,Weiguang Zhao,Ke Sun,Xihao Guo,Yunxi Jiang,Rui Zhang,Lei Fan,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboRisk 具身智能体安全基准（92 仿真 + 13 真机任务，覆盖任务拒绝、计划修复、执行期危险、注入攻击、序列记忆五类）与 LEASH 安全防护框架（任务/动作/提交三重门控，共享安全策略），实验表明可显著降低六种前沿基座模型的危害率且几乎不损失任务完成率、开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用基座模型（GPT/Claude 等）闭环控制机器人存在严重安全风险，而现有基准最多只覆盖五类风险中的两类，部分仅评估文本输出，缺乏全面的真实闭环安全评估。

Method: 构建 RoboRisk 基准：92 个仿真任务 + 13 个真机任务，闭环执行，分五类（Task-level Refusal / Plan Repair / Execution-time Hazard / Injection / Sequential Memory）；评测 6 个前沿基座模型及两个机器人专用 API；提出 LEASH 框架，用决策模型（Jev 或 OpenAI Decisions API）在视觉上下文中实现任务门、动作门、提交门三重校验，与规划器共享同一安全策略。

Result: 六个 SOTA 基座模型在 RoboRisk 上普遍呈现高危害率；LEASH 在仿真与真机上均显著降低所有六个模型的危害率，基本保持任务完成性能，时间与 token 开销很小。

Conclusion: 具身机器人智能体的安全亟需系统化基础设施；RoboRisk 提供了覆盖最全的闭环安全评测，LEASH 作为可插拔安全层能有效防护且开销可忽略。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude have shown strong capabilities in spatial understanding and embodied intelligence. These advances make agentic robotics a promising path toward artificial general intelligence in robotics. However, we find that using foundation models to control robots poses serious safety risks, and comprehensive safety evaluation is still lacking. We introduce RoboRisk, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating safety in agentic robotics. Its 92 simulated tasks and 13 physical-robot tasks fall into five categories: Task-level Refusal (T), Plan Repair (P), Execution-time Hazard (E), Injection (I), and Sequential Memory (S). Prior benchmarks cover at most two of these categories, and several evaluate only text outputs. In contrast, RoboRisk covers all five and executes every task in closed loop. We evaluate six state-of-the-art foundation models on RoboRisk: GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6 Luna, Claude Opus 5.5, Gemini 4 Argon, and Grok 4.7. These models generally show high harm rates. We also evaluate two robot-specific APIs: the Dream API and PokeBot's RoboAPI. We further propose the Lease-Enforced Agentic Safety Harness (LEASH), which offers essential safety infrastructure for agentic robotics and can be integrated into nearly any agentic architecture. LEASH leverages a decision model (Jev or OpenAI's Decisions API) to implement task, action, and commit gates, which review the task, each action, and each irreversible operation in their visual context. The planner and all three gates share a common safety policy. Experiments on RoboRisk in simulation and on physical robots show that LEASH effectively reduces harm across all six foundation models. It largely preserves their task completion performance and adds little time and token overhead.

</details>


### [185] [Koopman Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14186)
*Wenjian Hao,Paulo C. Heredia,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出K-MBRL：用随机Koopman算子将未知非线性动力学线性化，结合critic估计剩余代价，实现单步预测+解析即时代价的近似策略目标，把模型相关策略梯度简化为常数矩阵乘法，并在四足机器人真机上完成二足平衡行走 maneuvers。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性机器人系统动力学未知时，无模型RL样本效率低，而基于模型RL的长时域rollout会累积预测误差且策略梯度计算依赖模型反传、代价高。作者希望兼得模型线性化带来的计算高效性与误差受控性，同时保留长期代价信息并对真机实时控制友好。

Method: (1) 用随机Koopman算子学习未知非线性动力学的线性提升表示；(2) critic网络估计值函数以补偿单步之外的未来代价；(3) 构造近似策略目标＝解析即时代价＋对一步预测状态的未来代价估计，使策略梯度退化为常数矩阵乘积；(4) 单步模型传播取代长时域rollout以抑制误差累积；(5) 策略为前馈评估，无需在线优化，可实时部署；(6) 在标准随机逼近假设下给出策略学习收敛性保证；在HalfCheetah、四足locomotion与pendulum swing-up上做仿真，含梯度近似精度与消融，并在实物四足上验证。

Result: 仿真中HalfCheetah与四足运动任务样本效率更高、性能优于经典无模型与基于模型RL基线；pendulum任务验证策略梯度近似精度并完成学习机制与关键超参消融；实物四足机器人成功完成由两只圆弧形后足支撑起立、保持平衡的双足行走、以及安全落地的连续动作序列。

Conclusion: Koopman线性化＋critic的混合结构在保证策略梯度计算高效、抑制模型误差累积的同时，给出收敛性保证，并证明可前馈实时执行复杂动态 maneuvers；Koopman线性模型与单步传播的结合是面向未知动力学机器人控制的实用范式。

Abstract: We propose a Koopman model-based reinforcement learning (K-MBRL) algorithm for controlling nonlinear robotic systems with unknown dynamics. The K-MBRL learns a control policy by minimizing an approximate policy objective that combines the analytical immediate cost with an estimate of the future cost evaluated at the one-step predicted state. To this end, we develop a stochastic Koopman operator-based learning method to approximate the unknown nonlinear dynamics in a linear representation, while a critic function estimates the remaining future cost. This formulation offers two key advantages. First, the linear Koopman dynamics simplify the model-dependent policy gradient computation to a constant matrix product, enabling computationally efficient policy improvement. Second, one-step model propagation mitigates the accumulation of prediction errors associated with long-horizon rollouts while retaining long-term cost information through the critic. The learned policy enables real-time control through feedforward evaluation without online optimization. We establish policy learning convergence guarantees under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Simulations on HalfCheetah and quadruped four-legged locomotion tasks demonstrate strong sample efficiency and improved performance compared with classical model-free and model-based reinforcement learning baselines. A pendulum swing-up task is used to assess policy gradient approximation accuracy and conduct ablation studies on the learning mechanism and key hyperparameters. The proposed K-MBRL is further validated on a physical quadruped robot performing a challenging sequence of maneuvers: rising to an upright posture supported by two rounded rear feet, maintaining balance during bipedal walking, and safely returning to the ground, as shown in Fig.1.

</details>


### [186] [Elastic-WAM: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Imagination for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14257)
*Haitian Li,Tianfu Li,Xuxi Zhao,Zizheng Liu,Yangcheng Yu,Yiwen Hou,Zeyu Xiao,Mutian Xu,Ziqian Yang,Haonan Chen,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Elastic-WAM 提出用统一的时空精化需求场（时间基+空间幅度）自适应决定世界模型去噪何时停止、每步推进多远、刷新哪些区域，从而在保证操作成功率的同时降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-Action Model 通过预测未来视觉动态提升机器人控制，但迭代视频去噪推理开销大；现有部分未来去噪方法采用固定精化预算。作者观察到最优精化深度随任务与状态变化，且未来不同区域对动作生成的相关性不同，因此需自适应分配计算。

Method: 构建统一的时空精化需求场，联合控制三件事：去噪何时终止、每步推进幅度、需刷新的未来区域；用共享时间基与空间幅度参数化该场，并通过三阶段动作接地（action-grounded）监督流程学习；推理时用学到的场引导循环稀疏世界精化。

Result: 在多个仿真基准与真实世界长时程操作任务上取得 SOTA 控制性能，同时降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应、时空解耦的世界模型精化预算是在 WAM/VLA 类系统中提升效率-性能权衡的有效途径。

Abstract: World-Action Models improve robot control by predicting future visual dynamics, but iterative video denoising incurs substantial inference overhead. Recent approaches reduce this cost through partial future denoising, yet typically rely on fixed refinement budgets. Our empirical observations reveal that preferred refinement depths vary across tasks and states, while different future regions exhibit varying relevance to action generation. Motivated by these findings, we propose Elastic-WAM, which adaptively allocates world refinement computation across temporal denoising time and spatial future space. A unified spatiotemporal refinement demand field governs when to stop world refinement, how far to advance each denoising step, and which regions to refresh. We parameterize this field with a shared temporal basis and spatial amplitudes, and learn it through a three-stage action-grounded supervised procedure. At inference, the learned field guides recurrent sparse world refinement to improve task success while reducing inference overhead. Evaluations across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world long-horizon manipulation demonstrate state-of-the-art control performance with reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [187] [From Prediction to Action: Learning Physical Context for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14274)
*Chengxiao Dong,Alessio Caporali,Hongyu Lan,Gianluca Palli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Geometry-Pointer-Flow (GPF) 作为平面单次抓取 DLO 形状控制的决策骨干，并对比"预测效用"与"决策效用"：真值物理参数提升预测精度但控制增益有限，交互历史表示在闭环与硬件零样本迁移中更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移动机：现有工作默认"预测越准的表示越适合决策"，作者质疑该假设，希望在可变形线性物体（DLO）的单次抓取形状控制中系统区分 predictive utility 与 decision utility，回答"哪种物理/交互上下文表示真正有助于选动作"。

Method: 方法：构建 GPF（Geometry-Pointer-Flow）几何接地策略，将抓取点选择与抓取条件化运动生成解耦；在此backbone上比较三类物理上下文——真值参数、估计参数、学习到的交互历史表示，分别评估前向预测与执行控制；纯几何变体作为基线对照 DLO 专用扩散策略。

Result: 结果：几何-only GPF 使单步执行形状误差较 DLO 扩散策略降低 32.8%，参数量少 18.7 倍、推理快约 193 倍；预测上真值参数较纯几何降低 next-shape 误差 15.8%，估计参数略优于交互历史；控制上估计参数无显著单步增益，历史条件 GPF 在 470 对仿真试验中执行误差降低 9.9%；闭环仿真严格阈值达成率提升，硬件零样本迁移最优。

Conclusion: 结论：物理上下文应以"所支持的决策质量"而非纯预测精度来评价；轻量几何接地策略在效率上优势显著；交互历史表示在闭环控制与 sim-to-real 零样本迁移中优于精确参数估计，提示表示选择需面向控制任务对齐。

Abstract: A representation that accurately predicts a deformable object's response is not necessarily the one most useful for choosing manipulation actions. We study this distinction between predictive utility and decision utility in planar single-grasp shape control of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs). As a common decision backbone, we introduce Geometry-Pointer-Flow (GPF), a geometry-grounded policy that separates grasp-location selection from grasp-conditioned motion generation. Its geometry-only variant reduces one-step executed shape error by 32.8% relative to a DLO-specific diffusion policy, while using 18.7 times fewer parameters and achieving approximately 193 times faster inference. On this backbone, we compare physical context represented by ground-truth parameters, estimated parameters, and learned interaction-history representations, for both forward prediction and executed control. For prediction, ground-truth physical parameters reduce next-shape error by 15.8% over geometry alone, and estimated parameters are slightly more predictive than interaction history. This ordering does not carry over to control: estimated parameters provide no detectable one-step control gain, whereas history-conditioned GPF reduces executed error by 9.9% relative to geometry-only GPF across 470 paired simulation trials. In closed loop, interaction history improves strict-threshold attainment in simulation and yields the strongest zero-shot performance on hardware. All policies are trained exclusively in simulation and transferred to the physical robot without real-world training or fine-tuning. These results show that physical context should be evaluated by the decisions it supports rather than by predictive accuracy alone. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [188] [ForeseeDrive: Future-Token Conditioning for Anticipatory Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14286)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Yuting Hu,Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Hongtao Lu,Yue Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForeseeDrive 将未来场景演化编码为 query-indexed tokens（由日志中 actor 运动、occupancy 演化、route 交互与 TTC margin 派生），并在部署时从当前上下文预测同一 token 空间，以此在未来轨迹候选生成之前调制几何先验；配合零 token 门控残差路径、候选保持损失与 TTC 边界守卫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式驾驶规划器虽能表达多样 ego 未来，但下游 scorer 只能在已采样候选中挑选，无法补回缺失的机动。作者希望把"未来场景演化"变成可部署的接口，用于塑造候选生成本身而非事后打分。

Method: 从日志数据中的 actor 运动、occupancy 演化、route 交互和 TTC margin 提取 query-indexed token 作为未来表示；部署时用当前上下文预测同构 token，并以之条件化轨迹几何（在候选生成前介入）。引入零 token 门控残差通路与 candidate-preservation 损失抑制无关漂移，并用 TTC-margin guard 针对安全/不安全边界穿越脆弱点。

Result: NAVSIM navtest 89.2 PDMS；nuScenes val 平均 L2 0.41 m、碰撞率 0.05%；Bench2Drive 驾驶得分 76.50。DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM 受控诊断显示：零 token 时精确 no-op、候选保持下 aligned 优于 shuffled 的 selected-PDM 小幅增益、oracle-best 质量参差、TTC 边界失败呈局部化。

Conclusion: 基准结果与有界机制测试共同支持"未来条件化生成"作为一类实用的预见性接口；但机制优势幅度有限、oracle-best 质量不稳定，说明未来条件的收益是条件性的而非普遍的。

Abstract: Generative driving planners can represent diverse ego futures, but a downstream scorer can choose only among sampled candidates; it cannot recover an absent maneuver. We present ForeseeDrive, which turns future scene evolution into a deployable interface for shaping candidate generation. Rather than reconstructing dense future states or scoring a fixed menu, ForeseeDrive derives query-indexed tokens from logged actor motion, occupancy evolution, route interactions, and TTC margins, and predicts the same token space from present context at deployment. The tokens condition trajectory geometry before candidates materialize. A zero-token-gated residual path and candidate-preservation loss limit unnecessary drift, while a TTC-margin guard targets brittle safe-to-unsafe boundary crossings. Under their respective benchmark protocols, ForeseeDrive reaches 89.2 PDMS on NAVSIM navtest, 0.41\,m average L2 and 0.05\% collision rate on nuScenes validation, and a 76.50 driving score on Bench2Drive. Controlled DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM diagnostics show an exact zero-token no-op, a small aligned-over-shuffled selected-PDM advantage under preservation, mixed oracle-best quality, and localized TTC-boundary failures. Together, the benchmark results and bounded mechanism tests support future-conditioned generation as a practical interface for anticipation. Code is included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [189] [Gaussian Process-Augmented Model Predictive Contouring Control for 6-DoF Trajectory Tracking with a Holonomic AUV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14373)
*David Dörner,Giancarlo Troni,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 全6自由度椭球型全向AUV的非线性MPCC控制器，用GP回归学习残余动力学并以零阶鲁棒优化嵌入OCP，保持25Hz实时，仿真消融+水池实验对比nominal MPCC与PID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型全向AUV需在状态与输入约束下做任意视点近距结构巡检，要求精确位姿跟踪；MPCC天然处理约束与轮廓误差，但依赖精确动力学模型，而水下动力学难以精确获得。

Method: 为全向AUV构建6-DoF非线性MPCC（以轮廓控制提升跟踪精度）；用GP回归学习标称模型与真实动力学间的残余项，通过零阶鲁棒优化（基于不确定性的收紧约束）将该残差嵌入最优控制问题，无需学习模型的在线推断开销即可保持25Hz实时性；设计消融实验考察残差项选择的影响。

Result: 仿真消融表明残差建模项的选择对控制器设计至关重要；在MOLA AUV上的仿真与实水实验验证，所提控制器优于标称MPCC与经现场验证的PID控制器。

Conclusion: 将GP学习的残余动力学经鲁棒优化嵌入MPCC，可在模型不精确条件下实现实时、约束满足的6-DoF全向AUV近距巡检控制；该"学习残差+鲁棒MPCC"框架对水下等建模困难平台具有推广价值。

Abstract: Small, holonomic Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) enable close-range inspection of underwater structures from arbitrary viewpoints, requiring accurate position and attitude tracking under state and input constraints. Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) naturally handles these requirements, but relies on an accurate dynamics model, which is difficult to obtain underwater. In this paper, we present a nonlinear MPCC in all six Degrees of Freedom (DoF) for a holonomic AUV and alleviate the inaccuracies in the dynamics by augmenting the nominal model employed with residual dynamics learned via Gaussian Process (GP) regression. The learned residual is embedded in the optimal control problem through a zero-order robust optimization scheme, which keeps the controller real-time capable at 25 Hz. A simulation ablation shows that residual selection is essential when designing the controller, and simulation and in-water experiments with the Multimodality, Observing, Low-cost, Agile (MOLA) AUV validate the resulting controllers against a nominal MPCC and a field proven Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller.

</details>


### [190] [SIGMO: Sigmoid Gated Interaction-Aware Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14414)
*Avinash Singh,Manish Saroya,Fatemeh Cheraghi Pouria,Faizan Tariq,Jovin D'sa,David Isele,Yosuke Sakamoto,Sangjae Bae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SIGMO: 用多项式Sigmoid函数解析估计周边车辆让行分数，并嵌入滚动时域规划器，实现交互感知、轻量化的自动驾驶运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集/拥挤场景（窄通道多智能体、车流变道、无信号路口）需要高度主动且可解释、可快速适应的交互式规划；端到端与纯数据驱动方法在可解释性、可扩展性和适应性上受限。

Method: 1) 基于物理意义的交互特征（体现驾驶直觉）构造多项式Sigmoid函数，解析估计周围智能体的yield分数；2) 将该函数集成进receding-horizon（滚动时域/MPC式）规划器，支持与任意轨迹预测模块配合的交互感知规划。

Result: 在交互密集的仿真与硬件实验中验证，SIGMO在主动与防御行为间取得平衡，计算开销低，可作为移动性技术栈的强基线。

Conclusion: 解析式让行建模+滚动时域规划是一种可解释、可扩展、轻量的交互感知规划范式，适合作为通用mobility stack基线。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of autonomous vehicle technologies, reliable navigation within cluttered environments remains arduous due to the necessity of interaction-aware strategies; example scenarios include: narrow passages with multiple agents, lane changing in dense traffic, and crossing unsignalized intersections. These scenarios often require highly proactive strategies, simultaneously demanding interpretability and quick adaptability, which challenge end-to-end and data-driven methods. An effective strategy requires: scalability to multiple agents, adaptability to changes in other agents' behavior, and harmony with surrounding traffic without freezing or becoming overly conservative. To meet these requirements, we present SIGMO, a sigmoid-gated interaction-aware motion planner, with two main contributions. First, we formulate polynomial sigmoid functions that analytically estimate yield scores of surrounding agents using physics-grounded interaction features that capture driving intuitions. Second, we integrate the polynomial sigmoid functions into a receding-horizon planner, which facilitates interaction-aware planning with arbitrary trajectory predictions. We validate SIGMO in interaction-intensive simulations and hardware experiments. The results show that SIGMO balances proactive and defensive behavior while remaining computationally lightweight, serving as a strong baseline in a mobility stack. The simulation and hardware experiment videos are available here: this https URL .

</details>


### [191] [One Policy for 100 Household Tasks: Our Solution for the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14432)
*Ilia Larchenko,Gleb Zarin,Atindra Nair,Keerti Manney,Vitaly Kleban,Mirko Kiefer,Anjana Yodaiken*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge 第9名方案：单一 π0.5 系 VLA checkpoint 覆盖 OmniGibson 100 项长程家务任务（Q=0.392），核心结论为"训练不足"，以更长训练、方差缩减多样本 flow matching 与动作空间迁移为主；另有 14 个任务路由给 GPT-6 编码智能体（Q=0.639 vs 0.108）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025 年冠军方案（基于 π0.5）的主要瓶颈被诊断为欠训练，而非架构缺陷；同时需从 50 任务扩展到 100 任务，并解决训练步效率与梯度噪声问题。

Method: 1) 任务扩展：由示教技能标注构建语义阶段，新任务/阶段嵌入用相似旧任务热启动，21 维动作空间对齐遥操作；2) 方差缩减的多样本 flow matching，并用简化的 noise-vs-compute 模型自适应选择每个观测的动作专家采样数（未做消融）；3) 200k 步训练，约覆盖 211M 帧示教一轮；4) 云端评测基础设施：带溯源记录的共享结果、episode 诊断、可恢复仿真状态，单 GPU 并行 6 个仿真器且快于实时；5) 混合策略：14 个任务路由至 GPT-6 Astra 编码智能体，通过观测与运动工具控制机器人，每 episode 从其在训练实例上练习会话的冻结副本出发。

Result: 与 2025 共享的 50 任务 Q 从 0.260 提升至 0.371；100 任务整体 Q=0.392。GPT-6 智能体在 14 个任务上平均 Q=0.639，显著高于学习策略的 0.108。公开提交仅含部分 Astra 结果，得分 Q=0.446、全任务成功率 27.3%；最终排行榜第 9，为前十中唯一独立团队。

Conclusion: 长程家务任务上"更多训练 + 更低的梯度方差 + 更快的训练步"仍是 VLA 性能提升的主要杠杆；语言模型编码智能体在特定子任务集上远超端到端策略，提示学习策略与通用智能体的混合路由是有价值的工程方向。局限在于缺少消融、依赖专有智能体、仿真评测为主。

Abstract: We describe our solution for the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge: 100 long-horizon household tasks in the OmniGibson simulator. We placed 9th on the final leaderboard, the only independent team in the top 10. The core of the solution is a single vision-language-action (VLA) model, one checkpoint for all 100 tasks, built on our 1st-place 2025 solution, which is based on $\pi_{0.5}$. On the 50 tasks shared with 2025, its Q rises from 0.260 to 0.371; across all 100 tasks it scores 0.392. Our main conclusion from 2025 was that the model was undertrained, so most of the 2026 work went into training the model for longer, optimizing some training parameters, making each training step slightly faster and reducing the gradient noise: a migration from 50 to 100 tasks (semantic stages built from the demonstrations' skill annotations, task and stage embeddings of new tasks warm-started from similar old ones, and a 21-dimensional action space matching the teleoperation controls); variance-reduced multi-sample flow matching with a simple noise-vs-compute model that picks the number of action-expert samples per observation without ablations; and a 200k-step training run, about one pass over the 211M frames of the demonstrations. A cloud evaluation infrastructure keeps a shared record with provenance, episode diagnostics and restorable simulator states, and runs six simulators on one GPU together faster than real time. On top of the learned policy, 14 tasks are routed to a GPT-6 Astra coding agent that controls the robot through observation and motion tools, starting every episode from a frozen copy of its own practice session on training instances. Across these 14 tasks, Astra's mean Q is 0.639, against 0.108 for the learned policy. The public submission included only part of the Astra results and scored 0.446 Q with 27.3% full-task success.

</details>


### [192] [TanCRAFT: Context-Reasoned Anticipatory Feedback for Assembly Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14470)
*Sassan Mokhtar,Lars Doorenbos,Marius Bock,Sina Mokhtar,Katharina Beckh,Dominik Bach,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TanCRAFT四阶段实时预测式辅助系统：跟踪场景状态、推理当前部分装配、预测操作件的短期运动轨迹，并在预测放置与目标不兼容时提前干预；在七巧板装配任务中通过离线基准与组内用户实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理装配中人为错误需在发生前介入，但用户可自由选择装配顺序、正确性依赖动态演化的装配状态而非固定下一步，使传统逐步式（reactive）指导失效。

Method: 四阶段流程：1) 场景状态跟踪；2) 对当前部分装配结构进行推理与合法性判断；3) 预测被操作件的近未来运动（轨迹/落位）；4) 在预测放置与目标不兼容时触发干预反馈。以七巧板（tangram）装配为受控实例化场景。

Result: 离线基准与组内（within-subject）用户实验显示：相比无引导与反应式（reactive）会话，预测式反馈显著降低错误数，同时平均主观负荷最低、平均完成时间最短。

Conclusion: 预测式反馈在人因错误预防上有效，为面向自动化与人机协作工作单元的代价感知辅助（cost-aware assistance）奠定基础。

Abstract: Preventing human error in physical assembly tasks requires assistance systems that can intervene before mistakes are made. This is especially challenging in settings where users are free to choose the construction order and correctness depends on the evolving assembly rather than a fixed next step. We present TanCRAFT, a four-stage real-time predictive assistance system that tracks the scene state, reasons about the current partial assembly, forecasts the near-future motion of manipulated pieces, and intervenes when the predicted placement is incompatible with the target. We instantiate our framework and confirm its effectiveness in a controlled tangram assembly task through offline benchmarks and a within-subject user study. Our findings show that the predictive feedback significantly reduces mistakes relative to unguided and reactive sessions, while yielding the lowest mean perceived workload and shortest mean completion time. Overall, these results demonstrate the value of predictive feedback for error prevention and provide a step toward cost-aware assistance for automation and human-robot workcells.

</details>


### [193] [Is Your Robot's Memory Just a Clock? Temporal Shortcuts in Memory-Augmented VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14488)
*Chubin Zhang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示"时间采样式"记忆的VLA策略存在"幻影进度"(phantom progress)失效：空事件(暂停/抖腕)会被当作已完成工作而提前停止；诊断出时间戳捷径与记忆槽位占用两个根因，提出以事件索引记忆，无需重训即可恢复过半性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强VLA旨在"记住发生过什么"，但多数实现按时间均匀采样帧并打上时间步标签。由于时间在无事发生时也流逝，这类记忆无法区分"等待"与"工作"，需要检验其失效机制并给出修正方向。

Method: 在RoboMME计数任务上注入空事件(null event，如短暂暂停或腕部抖动)作为受控探针；对比"记忆保留"与"同帧不写入记忆"两种条件以隔离记忆的作用；进一步做时间戳重缩放、去除时间编码等消融；并对RoboMME已发布的多套记忆设计按记忆索引方式(时间索引 vs 事件索引)做横截面比较，提出事件索引记忆这一零训练修复，并与"注入暂停的数据增强"训练方式对照。

Result: 空事件使记忆VLA将间隔计为已完成工作并提前停止：单任务成功率由47/50降至16/50，另一任务由44/50降至10/50；同样帧不写入记忆时成功率恢复，故根因在记忆而非中断本身。单独重缩放时间戳即可诱发"快钟早停、慢钟重复"；移除时间编码仍不能修复，因为空事件帧仍占用按时间采样的记忆槽位。脆弱性随记忆索引方式变化。修复效果上，注入暂停的训练无法消除失效且损害计数能力，而事件索引记忆无需重训即可挽回一半以上的损失。

Conclusion: 记忆VLA的失效源于"时间流逝≈任务进度"的时间捷径，加上按时间均匀分配记忆槽位导致空事件仍占据容量。核心结论：记忆应按事件而非时钟计数，事件索引是低成本、可即插即用的修复方向。

Abstract: Memory-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) policies are meant to remember what happened. Many of them, however, fill memory by time: they sample frames uniformly across the episode and stamp each frame with its time step. Because time passes even when nothing happens, such a memory may not tell waiting from working. We show that it does not. On the counting tasks of RoboMME, we make the robot briefly pause or fidget its wrist. Such a null event changes nothing the task depends on, yet a released memory VLA counts the interval as work done and stops early. We call this failure phantom progress. It cuts success from 47 to 16 of 50 on one task and from 44 to 10 on the other. When the same frames are withheld from memory, success returns, so the cause lies in memory rather than in the interruption itself. We trace the failure to a temporal shortcut: because demonstrations proceed at a steady pace, elapsed time predicts progress. Indeed, rescaling the time stamps alone makes the policy stop early when the clock runs fast and repeat when it runs slow. Surprisingly, removing the time encoding does not cure the failure, because the frames of a null event still occupy slots of the time-sampled memory. Across RoboMME's released designs, vulnerability follows how memory is indexed. Of the fixes the diagnosis suggests, training with injected pauses does not remove the failure and impairs counting, whereas indexing memory by events recovers half or more of the loss without retraining. A memory meant to remember what happened should count events rather than clock ticks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Predicting Time-to-conversion for Dementia of Alzheimer's Type using Multi-modal Deep Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2205.01188)
*Ghazal Mirabnahrazam,Da Ma,Cédric Beaulac,Sieun Lee,Karteek Popuri,Hyunwoo Lee,Jiguo Cao,James E Galvin,Lei Wang,Mirza Faisal Beg,Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用401名ADNI受试者的MRI、遗传和认知/人口/CSF三类特征，构建扩展Cox模型的深度学习生存分析模型预测阿尔茨海默型痴呆的转化时间，发现认知类(CDC)特征贡献最大，仅用CDC即可媲美昂贵遗传或CSF数据的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAT受多因素影响，各因素对疾病进展的贡献不明；希望通过系统比较不同模态特征的贡献，获得更准确的转化时间预测，并评估昂贵检测（遗传、CSF）的性价比。

Method: 在ADNI数据库上取401名受试者、63个特征（MRI、遗传、CDC三类模态），采用基于深度学习、扩展经典Cox比例风险模型的生存分析模型预测DAT转化时间，并做模态组合的消融比较。

Result: 遗传特征贡献最小，CDC贡献最大；MRI+遗传优于任一单模态，但加入CDC后效果等同仅用CDC。

Conclusion: 现有临床流程中的认知测验即可达到优于使用昂贵遗传或CSF数据的生存分析效果，提示可降低检测成本而保持预测精度。

Abstract: Dementia of Alzheimer's Type (DAT) is a complex disorder influenced by numerous factors, but it is unclear how each factor contributes to disease progression. An in-depth examination of these factors may yield an accurate estimate of time-to-conversion to DAT for patients at various disease stages. We used 401 subjects with 63 features from MRI, genetic, and CDC (Cognitive tests, Demographic, and CSF) data modalities in the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) database. We used a deep learning-based survival analysis model that extends the classic Cox regression model to predict time-to-conversion to DAT. Our findings showed that genetic features contributed the least to survival analysis, while CDC features contributed the most. Combining MRI and genetic features improved survival prediction over using either modality alone, but adding CDC to any combination of features only worked as well as using only CDC features. Consequently, our study demonstrated that using the current clinical procedure, which includes gathering cognitive test results, can outperform survival analysis results produced using costly genetic or CSF data.

</details>


### [195] [Structured Kernel Surrogate Models for Multimodal Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13198)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Jianglin Lu,Ruoxuan Xiong,Yun Fu,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SKS 提出结构化核代理模型，用组合核刻画"样本×模态"选择状态对预测结果的影响，实现跨样本模态归因，并在多模态数据选择下游任务中提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有影响函数方法只在单样本层面估计数据影响力，无法刻画"某样本的某模态对另一样本多模态学习结果"的跨样本、跨模态交互作用。

Method: 将预测结果建模为选择状态的代理映射；用核岭回归搭配结构化组合核，核函数由"单样本内模态对相似度"与"样本对相似度"相乘构成；并设计基于梯度的估计算法高效拟合该核代理模型。

Result: 在两个多模态基准（卫星图像、路网、文本、音频、表格数据）上，与真实预测结果的相关性较基线提升57.8%；下游数据选择平均精度提升1.93%；所选数据训练出的模型 Hessian 迹更低（损失面更平坦）；用于挑选卫星图像时可定位事故多发的路段而非背景。

Conclusion: 跨样本模态归因可通过结构化核代理模型有效实现，兼具估计精度与下游数据选择收益，并可作为可解释的数据筛选工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of cross-sample modality attribution, which involves measuring how one modality in one sample affects the learning outcome in other multimodal samples. This problem is crucial in a multimodal system because a data modality (such as a text description) may be redundant for one sample (a self-explanatory image), but the same data modality would otherwise be uniquely informative for another sample, or informative when combined with other modalities. Prior work focuses on influence estimation at the level of one sample, and it remains an open question how one should capture the interactions across both samples and modalities. To capture scenarios like the above, we propose structured kernel surrogate models, which are surrogate mappings from both samples and modalities to the prediction outcome when they are trained together. We design an algorithm, SKS, which applies kernel ridge regression with a structured, compositional kernel on the selection states of individual samples and modalities. Specifically, the kernel function multiplies kernel-based similarity scores between every pair of data modalities for one sample and a pair of two samples. Additionally, we design a gradient-based estimation algorithm to efficiently estimate the structured kernel surrogate model. We evaluate our approach on two multimodal benchmarks, spanning a range of data modalities including satellite images, road networks, text, audio, and tabular data. We find that SKS achieves $57.8$% higher correlation with the true prediction outcomes than existing methods. In downstream data selection, SKS improves the average accuracy by $1.93$% over baselines. Models with data selected by SKS have lower Hessian traces, indicating a flatter loss surface. Using SKS to select satellite images identifies road segments, where accident scenes commonly occur, as opposed to background images.

</details>


### [196] [One Trajectory, Two Requirements: Accuracy and Local Stability in Euler Sampling for Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13311)
*Mahindra Rautela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究flow matching机器人策略的Euler采样步数选择：指出轨迹精度与局部Euler稳定性可能给出不同的步数需求，但实测冻结π0.5-LIBERO检查点未出现违反Euler非扩张条件的模式，说明样本总体受精度而非稳定性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: flow matching策略通常离线扫参选定固定Euler步数，但每个输入+噪声采样对应不同ODE轨迹与局部Jacobian；作者质疑名义轨迹精度足够的步数是否在某些强收缩方向仍过粗。

Method: 构造精确二坐标系算例，并对六个已训练flow模型做受控增强实验（保持原学习传输不变）；随后在不改权重与动作动力学的前提下，分析冻结π0.5-LIBERO检查点在10个LIBERO-10任务共30个策略输入上的Jacobian收缩方向与Euler非扩张条件，并做精度扫描。

Result: 动作端点附近存在强收缩方向，但无采样模式违反Euler非扩张条件；5个输入在5%终端误差下需4–9步，1%误差下需87–112步，呈精度受限而非稳定性受限。

Conclusion: 终端精度不能排除独立稳定性需求；该局部准则下机器人策略采样主要受精度约束，但受控实验表明稳定性要求可能在其它情形单独出现。

Abstract: Flow matching robot policies commonly use a fixed Euler step count selected from offline accuracy sweeps. At inference time, however, each policy input (visual observation, robot state, and language instruction) together with a source-noise draw defines a different ODE trajectory and sequence of local Jacobians. We ask whether a step count that accurately follows the nominal noise-to-action trajectory can still be too coarse for strongly contracting directions around it. An exact two-coordinate system and a controlled augmentation of six trained flow models show that trajectory accuracy and local Euler stability can impose different step requirements even when the original learned transport is unchanged. We then study a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$-LIBERO checkpoint without modifying its weights or action dynamics. Across 30 policy inputs from 10 LIBERO-10 tasks, the Jacobian exhibits strong contracting directions near the action endpoint, yet no sampled mode violates the Euler non-expansion condition. On five of those inputs, accuracy sweeps require 4--9 Euler steps at 5% terminal error and 87--112 at 1%. These five paired cases are therefore consistent with accuracy-limited rather than stability-limited sampling under this local criterion, while the controlled experiments show why endpoint accuracy alone cannot rule out a separate stability requirement.

</details>


### [197] [Robust Flow Matching under Target Corruption and Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13324)
*Mert Can Kurucu,Erik Englesson,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将流匹配(Flow Matching)训练视为条件向量场上的时间依赖回归问题，分析目标污染与标签噪声的影响，并提出TRS(时间残差评分)与CCS(对比条件评分)两种"污染特定残差→样本权重"映射以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FM假设目标数据与标签干净，实际中常被破坏；缺乏对两类污染如何在向量场回归目标中传播的机制性理解，因而缺少针对性的鲁棒化手段。

Method: 理论分析：目标污染引入随时间增强的残差项；标签噪声导致更新到错误的类条件向量场。据此设计基于残差分数的样本重加权：TRS在FM中后段时间强调残差幅度（干净与污染样本更可分）；CCS将观测类条件向量场残差与同一轨迹/速度目标下其他类条件向量场残差比较，以识别标签噪声。

Result: 在受控污染设置与真实含噪标签实验中，所提方法在鲁棒性上优于标准FM与鲁棒训练基线。

Conclusion: 污染特定的残差结构可作为鲁棒流匹配训练的实用基础；将鲁棒性问题转化为时间依赖残差回归的加权问题，具有可解释性与通用性。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) is a strong framework for generative modeling due to its stable training and efficient sampling. However, FM assumes clean target data and labels, an assumption often violated in practice. We formulate FM training as a time-dependent regression problem over conditional vector fields and analyze how the two corruption types affect the objective: i) target corruption adds a time-dependent residual term that grows with time, ii) label noise causes updates to the wrong class-conditional vector field. Based on this analysis, we propose to improve FM robustness by mapping corruption-specific residual scores to sample weights. For target corruption, we propose the Temporal Residual Score (TRS), which emphasizes residual magnitudes at intermediate-to-late FM times, where clean and corrupted samples are more separable. For label noise, we propose the Comparative Conditional Score (CCS), which compares the residual of the observed class-conditional vector field against alternative class-conditional vector fields for the same trajectory and velocity target. Experiments across controlled corruption settings and real-world noisy labels show that the proposed methods improve robustness over standard FM and robust-training baselines, supporting corruption-specific residual structure as a practical basis for robust FM.

</details>


### [198] [MouldIt: Data Efficiency of Multi-Task Temporal Representation Learning in Injection Moulding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13646)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将注塑成型过程建模为表征学习任务，提出时序卷积+注意力的变分自编码器MouldIt，统一完成模内信号重构、已知类零件分类与未知类检测，并证明多模态（多种模内信号）可显著提升数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造需要能处理多任务的通用模型，而这类模型必须理解底层物理过程。作者以注塑成型为案例，考察表征学习相对于传统机器学习在数据效率与多模态融合上的优势。

Method: 基于真实Erlanger Träger零件的参考循环合成过程先验数据集；MouldIt由时序卷积与注意力构成VAE结构，重构模内信号并在潜空间上做“合格/多种失效模式”分类。在9个数据规模、单模态与多模态设置下，与accept-all基线及kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、PCA（均改造为支持两任务）对比，任务为已知类分类与未知类检测。

Result: 单模态下从4000个循环起、多模态下从2000个循环起全面超越传统方法；零件分选损失单模态6.0±0.7%、多模态1.0±0.3%，在全部基线与PCA+随机森林组合中排名第一。

Conclusion: 过程感知的时序表征学习在注塑成型监测中兼具数据效率与多任务能力，多模态融合可将所需数据量减半并大幅降低分选损失；但验证主要依赖合成数据，真实产线泛化性仍需实证。

Abstract: Smart industrial solutions that can address multiple tasks require models that understand the underlying processes. We examine representation learning for modeling the injection moulding process as an example of such solutions. We consider its data efficiency and multi-modal benefit compared to classical machine learning alternatives. To this end, we synthesize a process-informed dataset based on a real reference cycle of an Erlanger Träger part. We then introduce MouldIt, a temporal convolution and attention based variational autoencoder that reconstructs in-mould signals, while classifying parts as acceptable or as one of our synthetic failure modes. We evaluate MouldIt on two tasks, known-class classification and unknown-class detection, across nine dataset sizes in single-modal and multi-modal settings. We compare it with an accept-all baseline and the classical methods k-nearest neighbors, logistic regression, random forests, and principal component analysis, which we adapt to support both tasks. MouldIt outperforms classical methods across both tasks from 4000 cycles in the single-modal setting and from 2000 cycles in the multi-modal setting. It achieves part sorting losses of 6.0 $\pm$ 0.7% and 1.0 $\pm$ 0.3% in the single-modal and multi-modal settings respectively, ranking first across all baselines and a combination of principal component analysis and random forests.

</details>


### [199] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Deep Neural Networks: Methods, Frameworks, and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13868)
*Lorenzo Cirillo,Irene Amerini,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述文章：总结深度神经网络机械可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）的方法进展，聚焦于识别其激活对特定行为具因果责任的组件，用于分析网络内部因果推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络虽支撑图像、音频、语言等多域任务，但内部机制难以解释；单神经元常表征多重概念、层间激活存在大量相互依赖关系，因此需要识别具有因果责任的组件。

Method: 综述性梳理，无新方法；按"组件级因果归因"思路整理机械可解释性的代表性技术（如神经元/回路级分析与因果干预类手段）。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验或量化结果，仅为领域概览。

Conclusion: 机械可解释性为理解深度网络内部因果机制提供了路径，该文可作为该方向的入门式综述参考。

Abstract: Deep neural networks underpin fundamental tasks in a wide range of signal domains, such as image, audio, and language processing. However, their internal mechanisms are notably difficult to interpret. Internal activations are structured signals transformed across layers that may encode a multitude of interdependent relationships, as individual neurons frequently represent multiple underlying concepts. Mechanistic interpretability aims to uncover the internal mechanisms of neural networks by identifying components whose activations are causally responsible for specific behaviors. In such a context, this article provides an overview of advances in mechanistic interpretability for the analysis of causal inference in deep neural networks.

</details>


### [200] [RB-LDC: Redundancy-Balanced Latent Coding for Noise-Robust Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13955)
*Hyunseok Jeong,Jaeho Jeon,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在编码器与扩散过程之间插入可逆线性层，按解码器敏感度重新分配各方向的log-SNR，用频谱求解器给出LMMSE风险最优分配，并在VAE潜空间重建上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白化后的前向扩散对每个方向施加相同SNR的噪声，但潜扩散模型的解码器对各方向扰动的敏感度并不相等，这导致图像质量在同等SNR下损失不均。

Method: 在一个能量预算与保证可逆的下界约束下，最小化解码器敏感度加权的LMMSE风险在扩散调度上的积分，得到唯一最优分配；该层与解码器的期望拉回度量对齐，并使各方向的边际风险下降相等，由确定性频谱求解器计算。大规模预算下分配呈敏感度幂律，指数由低SNR端损失加权尾决定（噪声预测1/2、数据预测2/3，截断调度存在交叉）。以Jacobian敏感度估计实例化为RB-LDC。

Result: ImageNet 256×256、SD-VAE与VA-VAE上，RB-LDC在四项指标、五种编码配置、15个SNR与3个随机种子的均值上均优于同等预算的各向同性编码；VA-VAE块局部编码下扰动FID平均降低17.1且保持干净重建；冻结扩散模型下12种采样进度×信道SNR组合的FID点估计均更低。同时报告了扰动FID变差的中间SNR区间。

Conclusion: 解码器敏感度加权可为潜扩散中的各向异性降噪提供闭式最优解，作为轻量可逆层提升噪声潜变量重建与受污染采样下的生成质量。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models decode approximately correct latents, making decoder sensitivity to perturbations relevant to image quality. After whitening, forward diffusion corrupts every direction at the same signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), despite unequal decoder sensitivities. We study an invertible linear layer between the encoder and diffusion process that shifts each direction's log-SNR. Its linear minimum mean-square-error (LMMSE) recovery risk has an exact spectral expression for any whitened latent distribution. Under an energy budget and a floor ensuring invertibility, minimizing decoder-sensitivity-weighted LMMSE risk integrated over the diffusion schedule yields a unique optimal allocation. The layer aligns with the decoder's expected pullback metric and equalizes marginal risk reduction across directions, computed by a deterministic spectral solver. At large budgets, the allocation follows a power law in sensitivity whose exponent is determined by the low-SNR tail of the loss weighting: one half for noise prediction and two thirds for data prediction under the cosine schedule, with a crossover on truncated schedules. We instantiate this principle as RB-LDC using Jacobian-based sensitivity estimates. On ImageNet at 256 x 256 resolution with SD-VAE and VA-VAE, trained RB-LDC improves reconstruction from noisy latents over budget-matched isotropic coding in all four metrics and five coding configurations, averaged over 15 SNRs and three pipeline seeds. It reduces average perturbed FID by 17.1 for VA-VAE block-local coding while preserving clean reconstruction. We also report intermediate SNRs where perturbed FID worsens. With a frozen diffusion model, RB-LDC yields lower FID point estimates in all 12 tested combinations of sampling progress and channel SNR under corruption during sampling.

</details>


### [201] [Structured Confidence Perturbation via Fisher Geometry for Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14046)
*Chenlong Wei,Hanling Wang,Chong Zhang,Qinkai Yu,Fangyu Wu,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对对抗训练中"伪攻击"（提升损失但不改变置信结构）问题，提出混合交叉熵+KL目标与基于置信流形几何的扰动生成（最优方向对齐Fisher信息矩阵主特征向量），并给出高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练常被"伪攻击"困扰：扰动显著提高损失值但并未真正改变模型的置信度结构，说明仅以损失最大化为目标的对抗样本质量不高，鲁棒性提升受限。

Method: 两条互补路线：(1) 在损失最大化框架下引入交叉熵与KL散度的混合目标，使对抗样本同时破坏预测正确性与分布一致性；(2) 几何重构对抗训练，生成使置信流形上分布距离最大的扰动；理论证明最优扰动方向与Fisher信息矩阵主特征向量对齐，并提出避免高维矩阵运算的高效算法。

Result: 多数据集、多模型架构实验表明，方法在对抗鲁棒性与鲁棒迁移性上优于现有方法，尤其在高置信区域的结构性扰动上表现突出。

Conclusion: 以"置信结构变形"而非单纯损失上升作为攻击与防御目标，可缓解伪攻击问题，Fisher几何视角为对抗训练提供了有理论支撑的新方向。

Abstract: Adversarial training is often plagued by "pseudo-attacks", perturbations that significantly increase the loss value but fail to truly alter the model's confidence structure. To address this, we propose two complementary approaches to directly attack and defend against confidence structure deformation. First, building upon the traditional loss maximization perspective, we introduce a hybrid objective that integrates cross-entropy and KL divergence, encouraging adversarial examples to simultaneously disrupt prediction accuracy and distribution consistency. Second, we geometrically reconstruct adversarial training by generating perturbations that maximize the distribution distance on the confidence manifold. Theoretical analysis shows that the optimal perturbation direction aligns with the principal eigenvectors of the Fisher information matrix. We also propose an efficient algorithm to avoid high-dimensional matrix computations. Experiments on multiple datasets and model architectures demonstrate that our method outperforms existing methods in adversarial robustness and robust transferability, particularly excelling in structural perturbations within high-confidence regions.

</details>


### [202] [HaiRuo: Forecasting Ocean Eddies as Fields and Objects with a Satellite-Altimetry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14136)
*Xukun Qin,Haochen Sun,Yuyang Tian,Ge Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HaiRuo 是一个兼具“场预测”与“目标预测”的中尺度涡旋预报框架：基于 30 天卫星海平面图，单次前向输出 15 天海平面与地转流场、逐日涡旋掩膜及每个现存涡旋的轨迹，核心为 1.05B 参数无标签预训练基础模型 HaiRuo-1B 与拉格朗日目标解码器 XuanJi。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中尺度涡旋承载海洋大部分动能并输运热量、盐度与营养盐，但两周后的涡旋位置仍依赖海洋模式加事后追踪启发式，缺乏端到端、同时输出物理场与涡旋对象轨迹的预报方法。

Method: 两组件分工：(1) HaiRuo-1B，开放权重 1.05B 参数基础模型，在 24 年全球卫星测高观测上无标签预训练，提供 15 天物理场与掩膜；(2) XuanJi 拉格朗日对象解码器，在每个涡旋的随动极坐标帧中采样演化预报场，经三轮前瞻迭代精修 15 天路径，并具对象注意力机制可插拔用于多种骨干网络。

Result: 单一预注册全球评测中，15 天中心误差 25.2 km，优于 14 种方法（对比 30.6 km，-18%），在标注区域外及 ENSO 两相位下均保持优势，并复现西向漂移、50–60°S 东向逆转与气旋-反气旋向极偏转；相同解码器下场技巧 +0.14、mIoU +1.1 点；冻结骨干降低 1.2 km（从头训练 1.35 km），XuanJi 再降 2.5 km（联合微调 2.9 km），跨六个公开骨干降 2.5–3.3 km；经固定追踪器的 10 天流水线中，相对 WenHai/XiHe 中心误差降 37–43%，对象轨迹流水线误差全局降 77–78%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明“物理场 + 对象轨迹”联合建模、无标签大规模预训练加随动极坐标拉格朗日解码，可显著超越事后追踪启发式与传统预报骨干，并提供开放权重与代码，具备较强可复现性。

Abstract: Mesoscale eddies carry most of the ocean's kinetic energy and move heat, salt and nutrients across basins, yet where an eddy will be two weeks ahead is still read off ocean models with post-hoc tracking heuristics. We introduce HaiRuo, which forecasts eddies as fields and as objects at once: from 30 days of satellite sea-level maps, one forward pass returns 15 days of sea level and geostrophic velocity, daily eddy masks and the trajectory of every eddy alive today. HaiRuo pairs two components with a clear division of labour: (1) HaiRuo-1B, an open-weight 1.05-billion-parameter foundation model pretrained without labels on 24 years of global satellite-altimetry observations, which supplies the 15-day physical fields and masks (+0.14 field skill and +1.1 mIoU points under the same decoder) and further lowers 15-day centre error by 1.2 km frozen (1.35 km over training from scratch); and (2) XuanJi, a Lagrangian object decoder that samples the evolving forecast in each eddy's co-moving polar frame and refines the 15-day path across three look-ahead rounds, removing 2.5 km on HaiRuo-1B (2.9 km with joint fine-tuning) and 2.5-3.3 km via its object attention across six published backbones. Under one pre-registered global evaluation over the whole ocean, HaiRuo improves on fourteen methods on every endpoint (25.2 vs. 30.6 km 15-day centre error, -18%), holding its margin outside the labelled regions and through both phases of the El Nino-Southern Oscillation (ENSO), while reproducing westward drift, 50-60 deg S eastward reversal and poleward cyclone-anticyclone deflection. Over ten days through one fixed tracker, HaiRuo's forecast field cuts the eddy-centre error of WenHai and XiHe by 37-43% (-2.8 km vs. day 0 while they degrade +4.3-8.7 km), and its object trajectories cut pipeline error by 77-78% globally. Open-weight checkpoints, code and project page: this https URL

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Topology-Aware Reinforcement Learning for CAD Decomposition toward All-Hexahedral Meshing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12930)
*Jiaming Peng,Zhihao Zheng,Rui Wang,Qinglin Wang,Jing Xiao,Jie Liu,Na Lei,Xinhai Chen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: DecompRL用强化学习学习拓扑感知的CAD内部切割动作序列，配合几何奖励与可网格化反馈，实现面向六面体网格生成的分解；在四个基准中三个上成功率最高（Fusion360达59.6%），运行时间较LMFF最多快约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂CAD模型的自动六面体网格生成长期受困于特征错位（feature misalignment）问题，正交或交互特征共存导致直接六面体网格化不可行，必须分解为可网格化的子块。现有分解方法（frame-field流程与学习型方法）虽然降低了人工干预，但动作空间局限于基于表面的切割，易产生无效或不可网格化的分解，对几何与拓扑复杂度敏感。

Method: 提出DecompRL——面向六面体网格化的CAD分解深度强化学习框架。设计一组拓扑感知的内部切割（internal-splitting）动作，同时利用全局拓扑结构与局部几何属性来分离错位特征；并引入几何奖励与可网格化反馈（meshability feedback）引导策略学习向有效分解与高质量网格收敛。

Result: 在ABC、Fusion360、NIST、DeepCAD四个基准上，DecompRL在三个基准上取得所评估学习型方法中最高的成功率，并保持有竞争力的网格质量；Fusion360上成功率提升最大，达59.6%。LMFF与DecompRL的运行时比最高达两个数量级（注：该比值仅比较完整LMFF流程与DecompRL分解本身，不含后续网格生成）。

Conclusion: 将切割动作从表面扩展到内部、并以拓扑+几何信息驱动强化学习，可有效缓解特征错位导致的分解失败，显著提升CAD六面体网格化分解的成功率与效率，为复杂CAD模型自动六面体网格生成提供可行的学习型方案。

Abstract: Automatic hexahedral meshing for complex CAD models has long been regarded as the "holy grail" of mesh generation. The fundamental challenge arises from feature misalignment, where orthogonal or interacting features coexist, making direct hexahedral meshing infeasible and requiring decomposition into simpler, meshable blocks. While existing decomposition methods, including frame-field pipelines and learning-based techniques, have reduced manual intervention, they can remain sensitive to geometric and topological complexity and may fail to produce valid decompositions for challenging feature configurations. Specifically, their action spaces are typically restricted to surface-based splitting, which often yields invalid or unmeshable decompositions. To address these limitations, we propose DecompRL, a deep reinforcement learning framework for CAD decomposition toward hexahedral meshing. We design a set of topology-aware internal-splitting actions that leverage both global topological structure and local geometric property to effectively separate misaligned features. Furthermore, we introduce geometric rewards together with meshability feedback to guide the learning process toward valid decompositions and high-quality meshes. Extensive experiments on ABC, Fusion360, NIST, and DeepCAD show that DecompRL achieves the highest success rate among the evaluated learning-based methods on three of the four benchmarks, while maintaining competitive mesh quality. The measured LMFF-to-DecompRL runtime ratio reaches up to two orders of magnitude; this compares the full LMFF procedure with DecompRL decomposition alone, excluding subsequent mesh generation. The largest SR improvement is observed on Fusion360, where DecompRL reaches a success rate of 59.6%.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Large-area Spatially Aligned Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12912)
*Joni Vanherck,Brent Zoomers,Lode Jorissen,Isjtar Vandebroeck,Eric Joris,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出一种将头显内空间锚点与外部无漂移跟踪系统对齐的方法：准备阶段在头显与外部跟踪系统中同步布置锚点获得3D-3D对应，运行阶段用点云配准对齐两套锚点云，从而把局部精确的锚点精度扩展到更大范围，解决大空间XR跟踪漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大范围XR体验中，头显自身跟踪存在误差累积导致的漂移，使虚拟与现实几何不一致，破坏同处一地的共位XR交互；而头显内置跟踪仅在局部（锚点附近）精确，外部跟踪虽无漂移但需依赖额外设备。

Method: 以外部无漂移跟踪方法作为ground truth；准备阶段在头显内与外部系统中同时放置锚点，构建3D-3D对应关系形成两组锚点云；运行阶段采用点云配准（cloud registration）对齐两组锚点云，从而得到头显坐标系到外部真值坐标系的变换；运行阶段头显可独立运行，仅用内置跟踪。

Result: 实证评估表明系统可有效将虚拟内容与真实世界对齐并扩大精确跟踪区域；并进一步通过比较两种跟踪方法的相机位姿来定量评估对齐精度。

Conclusion: 提出了一种与头显品牌无关、与外部跟踪方案无关的通用锚点云配准框架，可在保持头显独立运行与用户移动舒适性的同时扩展精确跟踪范围，可应用于制造与建筑、教育、娱乐等领域。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) technologies, including Virtual Reality (VR) and Augmented Reality (AR), offer immersive experiences merging digital content with the real world. Achieving precise spatial tracking over large areas is a critical challenge in XR development. This paper addresses the drift issue, caused by small errors accumulating over time leading to a discrepancy between the real and virtual worlds. Tackling this issue is crucial for co-located XR experiences where virtual and physical elements interact seamlessly. Building upon the locally accurate spatial anchors, we propose a solution that extends this accuracy to larger areas by exploiting an external, drift-corrected tracking method as a ground truth. During the preparation stage, anchors are placed inside the headset and inside the external tracking method simultaneously, yielding 3D-3D correspondences. Both anchor clouds, and thus tracking methods, are aligned using a suitable cloud registration method during the operational stage. Our method enhances user comfort and mobility by leveraging the headset's built-in tracking capabilities during the operational stage, allowing standalone functionality. Additionally, this method can be used with any XR headset that supports spatial anchors and with any drift-free external tracking method. Empirical evaluation demonstrates the system's effectiveness in aligning virtual content with the real world and expanding the accurate tracking area. In addition, the alignment is evaluated by comparing the camera poses of both tracking methods. This approach may benefit a wide range of industries and applications, including manufacturing and construction, education, and entertainment.

</details>
